# Tests

`npm run kit:check` (`tests/unit/`), `npm run test:browser` (`tests/browser/`) and `npm run test:release` (`tests/release/`), all in the development directory only: neither the Skill nor the npm package holds tests. The overview of all systems is [internals.md](internals.md).

## Known failures

These fail on main today (0.3.1, 2026-11-21) for reasons of their own, whatever change is being tested; a run that fails only on them says nothing against the change:

- The browser scenarios `flowWidths`, `handleFollows`, `workbenchPreferences` (it expects three switches in 设置, which has four now), `copyBlockLink` and `addGalleryProject`: mostly assertions that have not caught up with earlier changes of the interface.
- `packageNewWrites` fails at its "one undo step" check. Until 2026-11-22 it passed in one checkout only, whose dev server was slow (it watched a very large folder beside the code) and failed in every other; since the server watches `src/`, `public/` and `skills/gallery/sdk/` alone it fails there too, so the check seems to depend on how fast the server answers. The cause is not found.
- `turnedNewOrGroup` and `workbenchLanguage` have failed only in a run split with `--shards` on a busy machine, and passed again under `--retry`; `workbenchLanguage` also fails when it runs right after `renamedStorage` (the sidebar has one column of favorites more). `shippedPageCards` fails close to midnight (its today, countdown and habit cards go by the date), and `tableViewLatency` on a machine near full (its bounds, under "Browser suite" below).
- The fuzz seed 3044, as `npm run test:fuzz -- 3042 4 40 --more --agent` runs it: after a pull, a list item's words differ by a space after a paste ("q贴一贴二" against "q贴一 贴二").
- `tests/unit/clipboard-media.mjs` failed once in a whole `npm run kit:check` on a machine loaded with other runs and passes run alone; `tests/unit/export-weight.mjs` can fail with "Failed to fetch" while the whole browser suite runs beside it, and passes alone.

## Regression suite

Test runs leave folders in the system's temporary folder when a browser or a run is killed before it tidies up: a headless browser's profile is 10 to 40 MB, and thousands of them once filled a disk (2026-11-21). `tests/sweep-temp.mjs` (`sweepStaleTemp`) removes those of earlier runs, the folders named `puppeteer_dev_chrome_profile-*`, `gallery-browser-*` and `gallery-smoke-*` that were last written over six hours ago and that no running program holds a file of; `npm run kit:check`, `npm run test:browser` and `npm run test:release` call it as they start, and `node tests/sweep-temp.mjs [hours]` runs it by hand. A script of your own that starts a browser with puppeteer should close it in a `finally`, or its profile stays until the next run sweeps it.

`tests/unit/web-link-loading.mjs` checks browser rich-link and iframe-code paste, authentication-title filtering, and anonymous frame-policy uncertainty. The browser scenarios `linkPasteAndAuth` and `linkProviders` exercise title suggestions, safe paste, unchanged form/view layouts, and original URLs used for both loading and storage. Provider responses in these scenarios are fixtures, not proof that a live site's frame policy allows embedding.

`npm run kit:check` runs:

| Contract | Executable example | Boundary checks |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `DocCharts` | report-defined types drawn from their JSON (a drawing and a part with controls of its own), and from a table's rows | type names, one definition a type, a broken declaration alone, a table's rows as the sort reads them (numbers, words, null), merged cells and a table gone |
| `DocTables` | automatic content-based ratios, short/full-width/sticky tables; numeric sorting | P90/classification/compression, resize and hidden panels; a word typed measuring its cell alone, the widths a full measuring gives; null values remain last; reset restores order |
| `DocSheets` | pill tabs in a `DocState`; no dock beside the page | matching unique panels, valid default, keyboard arrows/Home/End, ARIA relationships and cleanup |
| Code | code blocks (`div.doc-code`: the header the kit draws around the title, folding, wrapping) and declared diffs (`.doc-diff`, their counts in the header) with offline Shiki | source-text preservation, safe text rendering, unknown languages, line options, colors after the page shows (the spans a block drawn at once gets), a broken diff alone, export rehydration |
| `DocState` and bound keys | memo, temporary input, filter buttons, a disclosure bound with `data-doc-bind`; transient keys | reload, reset, malformed/version-mismatched snapshots, unavailable storage, repeated export |
| Component linkage | “图表与表格联动” in the Component Catalog's 状态 Sheet, declared with one key | filter → table rows, a chart drawn from the table, table sort → its bound key |

**How much is run when** (the user's of 2026-11-08). A branch runs the unit files and the scenarios it touched, with the quick text checks (`docs`, `skill-docs`, `i18n`, `boundaries`, `engine-names`, `components`, `package-files`), and the fuzz only for work on the commit path. The whole of `npm run kit:check` runs once per wave, on main after the landing. Every scenario runs only at the release gate, sharded (`node tests/browser/run.mjs --shards 4 --retry`, which also fills `node_modules/.cache/scenario-times.json` that the balance of later runs reads), then `npm run test:release`. Two files make the whole suite slow or fragile on a busy machine: `tests/unit/component-command.mjs` alone takes about 21 minutes (it drives a browser), and `tests/unit/cli.mjs` is bound by time (waits for servers and for the update check), so under load it fails where it passes alone: run a failing file alone once before calling it broken. On 2026-11-09 a loop over the unit files on a loaded machine failed four of them (each passed alone), and `cli-server.mjs` hung for 24 minutes right after `cli.mjs` crashed in the middle of its run, then passed in two minutes alone (the cause is unknown: a process the crashed run left behind is a guess no one checked).

`tests/unit/components.mjs` holds the flows to the descriptions: `FLOWS` (`src/shared/components/`) is the one list, `styles/flow.css` spells it once and no other stylesheet does, and the checks fail a `<pre>` straight in each flow; a disclosure holds its summary and its content and nothing else (one given the kit's class without its parts said in one line: write it plain), and `.doc-lede`, `.doc-source` and `.doc-section-header` are named as gone (a header's lines are ordinary paragraphs or small text; a section's title is a plain heading with a paragraph below it); the six block-spacing tokens (`--doc-component-gap`, `-block-gap`, `-media-gap`, `-paragraph-gap`, `-group-gap`, `-list-gap`) are each defined once with their value in `styles/tokens.css`, read by the stylesheet of every block that takes them and named with that value in `docs/render.md` (kit-diff is what proves a change of them leaves every computed style as it was). `tests/unit/runtime.mjs` draws a formula in each flow (`DocKit.flows`), in `[data-doc-math]` and in no other element, holds the view copy's policy and its isolated vendor script to `src/shared/export-wrap.mjs` (the CLI export's), and holds the kit's exports against the calls `REMOVED` names (`DocCode`'s too); it also holds that a page declaring `data-ge-order="file"` opens its tables in the file's order (a header's `data-sort-default`, a bound key's sort not applied) while a sort asked for still sorts, and `tests/unit/editor.mjs` that the editor's served head declares it. It holds 图片放大 too (`runtime/zoom.js`, `src/shared/zoom.mjs`, render.md "Opening a picture large"): which pictures open (an image block's, a cell's, a card's, a node's, an SVG; not a link's, an icon, one under 48px, one that did not load, one outside the page), their caption, the viewer's size (`zoomFit`), its ways to open and close (Esc going no further, the close button, a click beside the picture, `beforeprint`), the focus taken and given back, an export of the view leaving it out, nothing while editing or printing, the zoom-in cursor's mark, the keyboard's way in (the marks on the pictures that open alone, not on an author's own control, named in the kit's words in either language, off while editing and off an export of the view; Enter and Space opening it, Esc giving the focus back), the page (but those marks) and `<html>` left as they were, and its print, focus-ring and reduced-motion rules; the browser scenario `imageZoom` opens and closes it in an exported file (fitted, modal, Tab to the close button, the wheel scrolling nothing, a table cell's and a card's picture, a small one opening nothing, reduced motion, print), in 演示视图 (`?present`), and in the page editor, where a click opens nothing and a double-click opens the editor's viewer (a card's picture too); the browser scenario `imageZoomKeys` reaches a picture with Tab in an exported file (in the tab order, named, its focus ring shown; a small one never reached), opens it with Enter and with Space (the page not scrolled), Esc giving the focus back with its ring, in 演示视图 too, and in the page editor finds no picture marked, Enter opening nothing and `report.html` as it was. The browser scenario `containerEdges` pins the edges of containers (a column, a disclosure's content and a detail card: none, whatever the block; a highlight panel: its text none, a component keeps its margin).

`tests/unit/write-back.mjs` covers a component writing back to the file ([editor.md](editor.md), "A component writes back"), in a workspace of its own. a move made while the card stood going with it, and carrying none of the word, its place gone going again by itself while the write went in), a manifest that changed given up with nothing to decide and nothing waiting; what went in kept for the revert check (`written`: every hash before one of the page's writes but the one the part has again, and the one the last left), an undo a write like any other, the file as read before the write asked of and as read after not, as read between a write and its undo too, `rewrite` writing the part again over the file's hash and asking as a change of theirs when written since, or asking of it no more; the drag undone under the card, its write refused and held, 恢复我的 and 仍然调整 settling the part where the page writes it as the file held it and 保留 Agent 的 asking of it no more, nothing asked of the file as it is after either; what an earlier load kept asked of by the instance's id (`carry`, what says nothing of the kind none), an instance deleted or held not asked of while it is and asked of again once the hold is decided, one an Agent wrote with no id asked of once the page's write named it, and a part the file spells otherwise than the page writes it, holding what the page shows, no card (`heldOtherwise`), one holding the drag a card; the autosave over it (`page-harness.mjs`): such a write heard, the card held and the page waiting, the crash buffer keeping the block to copy, 恢复我的 writing the reader's beside the Agent's change and the page shown again, the next load asking of what it kept and 接受 Agent 的 there keeping the file, and an earlier Gallery's entries (texts alone) read as they were, each asked of under a key of its own, though the earlier load named its block as this one names another, both put back held, and an instance written with no id, its drag saved and the page left (pagehide), asked of in the next load and written again there by 恢复我的; parts written together past what one commit carries waiting whole, and said on the instance, which a page taking up the one gone reads; what a commit wrote whole as the file holds it whatever the page shows, a write going after it (written and deleted in one pause then undone, two undos in one pause, a write right after the undo or while the deletion is on its way, in a section put back, taken out of one or put into one, a copy of an instance holding a write not saved); a write of an old manifest leaving the unit where the file has it; what one commit carries, by unit and by part, the crash buffer keeping all of it; and what a page left that the file did not take listed with the reader's version, but for what a manifest that changed gave up, which the next page says instead. It also holds the one copy of how JSON is written back as it was laid out (`src/shared/json-layout.mjs`: one line kept one line, a value's line alone changed, what JSON cannot hold left out, what could end a script escaped) and the check's word on a write that lays a part out anew, context.editJSON's (`editJSONWrote`) told from a layout of a component's own.

`tests/unit/runtime.mjs` covers, in JSDOM, what a custom block's component hears and is given ([render.md](render.md), "Runtime" and "A custom block on a page"): `source` for an attribute of the file in its slot (`data-checked`, a span, a look class) and for none the kit lays a table out by (`number`, `doc-table--auto`) nor for a disclosure shut, for nothing an instance in its slot draws but for that instance's words, a frame's changes of two tasks in one notice and none before the frame; `readJSON` and `editJSON` (the layout kept, a value returned, nothing written for no change, an end tag escaped, a promise refused, a part not of data thrown, an optional part not held and no JSON each `undefined`, the latter failing the instance, JSON's `null` read as `null` with no failure); `rows` (its columns, values as a chart reads them, links to a document and within it, a move with its status set and back, a row added last and after another, removed, the body as it was, a stale row refused, an unknown column thrown, merged cells none); `tests/unit/choice.mjs` covers, in JSDOM, the kit's menu of choices (`src/render/kit/runtime/choice.js`, [render.md](render.md) "A choice is the kit's menu"): the runtime and the stylesheet carrying it (the card the display menu's look, no outlined row), a `<select>`'s `mousedown` taken (default prevented) and opening one card at the end of the body, a row to each option in order with the chosen one checked (one check, none held for the others) and lit, a disabled option a dimmed row, `<optgroup>` headings and hidden options; the pointer lighting rows (not a disabled one) and a click choosing: `input` then `change` once, the bound key following, the focus back on the select, the page as it was (no node, no attribute) and nothing sent for the option already chosen; the keys (↓ opening, ↓ ↑ Home End stepping over a disabled row, Enter, Space, F4, Alt+↑, a letter, Esc, none of them reaching a listener of the page, an input method's keys left alone); a press outside, the trigger leaving the page and its options changing closing the card; a list box, a disabled select, the right button and a letter on a closed select left to the browser; `DocKit.choose` (pairs, objects, bare words and group headings, the value checked, the value answered, `null` for Esc, a signal and a second call on the anchor, one card at a time, a bad anchor a mistake), the card inside an open `<dialog>`, and a copy of the page made with the menu open holding none (`DocKit.dehydrate`).

What runs in a browser is the scenarios' below. `tests/fixtures/components/local/` holds the two: `strip`, a KPI strip of no script, and `tally`, a script that only reads, its state by instance, a `runtime` and a `dehydrate`.

`shown` read, written only where it holds something or was there, in the catalog's stamp and the `settings` line, and `inDocumentMenus`, the rule the server's states and the page's `followSettings` both go by; `unlisted`, the master switch of 设置 › 组件列表, the same (read as a hand wrote it, kept through a file that does not read, written only where it holds something or was there, in the stamp, in the line's fifth list and in a row, a block unlisted loading as any and out of every menu whatever its places and `shown` say, the menu switch showing what it would be, refused for a built-in); a manifest's `listed: false` (`unlistedBy`: off until gallery.json's `listed` names it, an `unlisted` of before none of its switch, in the stamp, the line's seventh list, a row's `unlisted` and `listedByDefault`, the page's `followSettings`, the dialog's write making `unlisted` its `listed` and taking it out of `unlisted` as it turns on, a `listed` that is no boolean keeping it from loading), and the answer's `packageIcon` the one mark of a custom block; the custom blocks of `tests/fixtures/components/` read and a manifest of every field; each keeping its custom block alone from loading, with why in either language: bad JSON, half written or not, a wrong id, a folder no id can be, a name Windows keeps for a device, each field missing or of the wrong shape, the engines' words (`category`, a unit, a box, a host, a rank) and any field it does not know, a part of a bad selector (a chain from no `:scope`, an unquoted value no browser takes), another `edit`, a `min` over its `max`, `sizes` with a `w` or an `h` beside them, one of them outside a `min` or `max` written beside them (which frees the card: `any`), a text part at what holds no text, closed names in `runtime` in any case, a `generated` that is the whole instance, a manifest or a template too long for every page, `assets` out of the folder or naming no file, a future `apiVersion` (disabled, its `version` kept); a folder in another case found by the id an instance names; a folder with no manifest being no custom block and no problem, and a manifest that is a link out of its folder none; a problem touching no other custom block and no document, the scan, the checks and the note saying why, every custom block told to the kit in a preview and none in an export, what a manifest says staying in its string; `KIT_ASSETS` held to `ASSETS`; a custom block's `stamp` and `definition` kept apart, what reaches no page in neither, the catalog's, and a problem's holding its reason, a file that has not changed read for none; the catalog in the page's map (`menuOf`), what a menu shows and its state and no part, whether a custom block has a slot, a template's markup staying in its text; what gallery.json says of the components (`componentSettings`), whatever it holds and no workspace stopping for it, a custom block hidden being what it was, the catalog's stamp moving and no custom block's, one disabled none to a page or an export, a note and no failure, what a list says of each state (`packageStates`), `writeComponentSettings` keeping all else and refusing what does not read, and `src/server/component-settings.mjs`, the blocks in both languages, the four locked, what is refused writing nothing; on the real clock a look due before the next write came after it on a busy machine and settled nothing, the test timing out), another custom block going through meanwhile, a folder taken away and written again within the moment the same custom block, a watcher firing without end putting no look off, and gallery.json heard as a hand writes it and taken at once as the server does (`taken()`), a custom block turned off held by nothing. In a browser the scenario `catalogHeard` holds an open page to it, in the real workbench: a custom block added, a manifest half written (nothing heard past the time a change takes) then finished, a custom block deleted once the reader's word is on its way, one that does not load with why in its note, each heard with a drawn page in every frame, the instance, its text part and the page's map following; presented, a page holding an instance reloads and one holding none stays. `catalogLeftAlone` holds a page to what changed for it: a custom block it holds no instance of changes nothing there (the selection, an open disclosure, the undo steps and the page itself stay, in the workbench and alone), one it holds is heard, `?solo` hears none, a page presented what it shows, a message that comes as a page is read waits for what the page holds, and one naming no custom block is for a page holding any instance. `catalogUnderWords` types steadily across the catalog's take, the custom block deleted and then its part no longer edited as text: the words stay on the page in a conflict of its own, the file keeps its own, and the file's are what the page shows once the reader takes them. converted and emptied, and a callout's paragraph, kept as the file's own are), a callout made of a block holding it as it is, written as a highlight block is, turned into a disclosure, a quote or a highlight block and back, what an empty title says under the caret whether the file or the page made it (`placeholderOf`), the least share of a column, title sync, cell and link validation, reasons, emptied containers, spacing and runs, CRLF), moving units within their box (page order to moves, spacing with and without blank lines, blocks on one line, comments, edits and deletions inside row left with one column, `into` a box where nothing stayed, every refused target), new blocks and conversions (in place with the comment above kept and the class dropped, refused blocks and markup, bold, then their words plain again), a node turned into an item or a highlight block (a colored one holding content keeping its color in the file: an item holding blocks writes its classes, a container made carries its color, written after the kit's class and checked) and a highlight block into a flowchart of one node, a flowchart made by "/" (one node, its title the empty paragraph) and one joining the flowchart beside it, a flowchart's nodes as no list items (the lines above and below and the items around a block of a node stopping at it; its title split with its rest a plain paragraph, turned into nothing, put back first by a step that leaves a node without one), nodes made on the page written with their color, direction and weight and refused with a class, weight or direction they do not take, lists to paragraphs, headings 1-4, disclosures, highlight panels (Enter on the empty last paragraph leaves the panel, the opening summary included; an `aside.doc-panel` offering a div's types and turned as a div's is), code blocks (made, edited, opened unchanged) and dividers, a disclosure back to paragraphs, new blocks of every type after a card and in page order next to split paragraphs, empty kit containers taking blocks, CRLF, a deleted new section taking its blocks along, refused places, the typing shortcuts' patterns), several blocks turned into one type at once (`groupTargets`: the types they share, no divider, their own marked only when shared, none with an image or a divider among them, a whole quote its paragraphs' types, every step of a flowchart offered lists, 折叠列表 and 高亮块, some of them lists only, nodes' titles nothing; `convertGroup` saved: every node of a flowchart an item of one list with its start and its content kept, two lists and the paragraph between them one list, two nodes one holding a list one bulleted list after what is left of the flowchart, items and a paragraph headings each, one highlight block holding a paragraph, an item in a list of its kind and a heading, and one disclosure with an empty title the same (a flowchart's steps whole with their content too, the blocks of two sections in the first), nothing changed when all are of the type; a list joined to the one before it, its items among those selected afterwards; two lists side by side already of the type left two; a list's items and its nested list's into into bullets and paragraphs, a paragraph joining it as a quote, into a highlight block whole; a highlight block across two sections, the section left empty gone), one item holding blocks from its menu or the format bar (`turnTargets`: another kind of list, a disclosure or a highlight block, wherever it stands in its list; from the middle of a numbered list, a to-do or a flowchart the list split around it, the numbers after it from 1 again, the direction on both parts; a nested one in its own list), one item or quote paragraph turned into a highlight block going in as it is (in a list or quote of its kind), a to-do item's `data-checked="true"` sent bare (`switched`) and a conversion the file refuses taking its move with it, coverage of the real Component Catalog, word-by-word merges of blocks both sides changed (`mergeText()` and through `commitEdits()`), the conflict card's word diff (`wordDiff`, on the merge's one common-subsequence function), a block two cards are about steps (`page/select-all/`, press after press from a text block, a selected block or a selection across blocks: the container blocks around it one level each, a highlight block's without its icon, a row of columns, a Sheet area and a table whole, a step selecting nothing more passed over, sections and lists no step, the page last without its title, an open detail card's content the top, of two cards open the one the press is in, a top holding only the block whose text was selected taking that block), and `writeAtomic` checking the file again before replacing it. What the reader hid of the built-in blocks (`page/offered.mjs`): the rows of "/" and of 在下方新建 and a block's grid less a hidden component's, a block keeping its own, a list left with nothing as it was, what says `hideable: false` staying whatever it is told, every type with a place in a menu listed in the settings in both languages, and the state in a page's map following what it is told. Caret movement, focus, the formatting bar, the "/" menu and the block menu, typing shortcuts as keys, block handles and selection, clicks in disclosure titles and dialogs need a real browser: they are not part of `kit:check`; the paths where work can be lost are in the browser suite below.

`tests/unit/inline-spacing.mjs` runs the same markup through the kit's `runtime/spacing.js` (the reading view) and the editor's `prepareBlock` and compares the text each shows: inline code and formulas beside words, across bold and links, beside one another, beside empty formulas and links, and link views must come out the same, which is the one rule of `src/shared/inline-spacing.mjs`; it also reads that rule from an element and from a text node (`sideOf`, `spaceAround`). Pasted code getting its spaces is in `editor.mjs`'s paste checks. `tests/unit/typed-spaces.mjs` holds the rule that takes out the spaces an edit strands (`src/editor/page/typing/spaces.mjs`) as a table of lines before and after an edit: letters deleted between Chinese characters (one by one either way, selected, at a line's edge, digits, inline code deleted whole, a number before its unit), Chinese typed after or before a word beside its space, letters replaced by Chinese or by letters, a line break, words kept apart by one space, what keeps the space (a Latin word, code or a formula on its other side; Latin punctuation and symbols typed, and deleted again; a Chinese mark beside the reader's own; the "/" menu's 、), C++, C# and 50% as words' ends (but no space autoSpace puts after one, nor a sign of a block written together: `needsSpace`, `writesTogether`), a line break beside the space taking it whatever is on its other side, and what is no such space (one deleted itself, between Chinese characters, between Latin words, after punctuation, one the reader typed, which a deletion keeps and only Chinese typed beside it takes); where the edit is found in a run of the same characters (`editOf`); and on a page (jsdom) the line's text (`lineText`), the caret after the spaces go, a mark between them, the reader's own spaces while the text runs on from their typing and not once it is written anew, a paste's snapshot its own, what was taken used once (by any after(), and forget()) and an input's gone after its task, a "、" parked until its menu is over (taken as any mark when still there, nothing when the menu took it, let go by the next edit), and Enter beside a word's space either side, right before and after code, between code and a word, with the caret in code, and beside a space with no pair.

`tests/unit/storage.mjs` covers what the editor keeps in the browser's storage (`page/storage.mjs`): JSON read back only when fit, the lists of what was used lately (the format bar's colors, the "/" menu's blocks, the formula editor's symbols: newest first, no repeats, a size limit, entries that are not fit left out, a fallback), and a page whose storage refuses going on without it.

`tests/unit/notices.mjs` covers what 减少通知 leaves out (`page/quiet.mjs`): the notices of everyday operations (what was just done, any that offers Ctrl+Z; how to go on: a selection's keys, a drag's prompt; a copy made with Ctrl+C, a save asked for with Ctrl+S, a key that had nothing to do) and the ones that still show (a refusal with its reason, what the page does not show, a link copied from a menu, an export, held saves, conflicts, text not put back), each written as its call site says it, through `t()`.

`tests/unit/editor-look.mjs` covers the editor's visual layer without a browser: a style sheet asked for again (a menu, its submenu, a crop window opening again) is in the head once, the palette first in every document and once; the one z-order (`src/shared/layers.mjs`: each level above the one before, the tooltip over menus, menus over panels) and no other file writing a z-index near the limit; no style declaring a token of the palette again or writing the blue itself (the icon picker's styles too: only `emoji-picker/support.mjs` keeps the older palette (`--c-*`, `--ca-*`), for the media menu and the image's bar); the one button look (`page/look.mjs` `BUTTONS`: its rules draw no line and no ring, a primary is the pale-blue token and never `--ge-accent`, an icon button is gray only pressed; `textButton` makes a `.ge-btn` with its states as attributes; and no editor style of a button writes a line, a ring or the solid blue of its own, each rule of a style read: the board's own forms, as fb18-board left them, excepted from this last one only: the blue check above reads them, their primary's rest, hover and words being the pale-blue tokens); and which files may hold an `!important` (the census). The scenario `editorFinish` holds the finish of 2026-11-17 in the editor (`page/look.mjs`, `ui.mjs` `appear` and `fadeAway`): the block menu 14px round, 5px round its flush 9px rows, the card's shadow, growing out of its side by the grip when the pointer opened it, the "/" menu typed opening at once, a closed menu gone from the page at once while a picture of it fades out and goes; the format bar 6px round its 8px tools, 14px round, the bar's shadow, a fill that comes at once and fades out over 100ms; the bar of the Agent's changes 44px high, 14px round, its buttons 32 and its switches 26 high. The scenarios `editorLook` (the palette sheet once; a menu and its color submenu opened three times add no style sheet; the menu, the format bar and the tooltip stand at their levels of the z-order; the section's bar and the icon picker's buttons show the one tooltip), `flagFrame` (a conflicted block's red frame is drawn in the editor's layer, 2px past the block, with no outline of the block's own, keeps its place when the page scrolls and goes with the flag) `lostPanelLook` (the lost texts' panel is the conflicts' card: the palette's background and shadow, the pale blue primary button, a quoted version), `pageButtonsFlat` (the buttons the page draws, in light and dark: a link's card, an image's media menu (its one button the primary of each tab), the conflict's card, the lost texts' panel and the bar of the Agent's changes have no line, no ring and no fill but the primary's pale blue and a switch's own pale green or red while it is pressed on (its +N and −M, `aria-pressed`), a plain one gray under the pointer; `GALLERY_TEST_SHOTS=<folder>` keeps their pictures) and `emojiPicker` (the icon picker in Gallery's own look under a Mac's user agent: every emoji a Twemoji picture, the skin button's hand and the icon 添加图标 gives and the one picked too; no flag, no 旗帜 group or button, none found by flag, 旗, 中国 or china, one used before not in 最近; nothing bright in what is drawn, the field focused, a cell reached by the keys, the 图标 tab's color menu with 每次先选颜色 on and the 上传 tab: a color's spread of red, green and blue times its opacity at most 24; text tabs, the one shown the only gray block; a group's title stuck at the list's top as it scrolls, and 符号's button bringing its title there, not past it) are the browser side.

`tests/unit/script-loader.mjs` covers the editor's one loader of a script a page was served without (`page/ui.mjs` `loadScript`, used for the kit's tables and code scripts and for KaTeX and mhchem): one load per address, the promise saying whether the script is there, a failed load forgotten and its tag taken out, a script already there left alone. `tests/unit/tables.mjs` covers pictures in a cell (a cell holding them a text block, shown where the server serves them, written back with a width a drag set, a picture the browser put in of no file left out, what `validCell` takes and the lock of one written another way), the table's operations (标题列 giving no look and taking none, a reader's look and an older file's kept, a cell made in it plain, a cell between the header row and the header column taking or losing the header row's look) and also how a cell's words read as a number (`src/shared/number-reading.mjs`, the one reading the kit and 一键优化 share: qualifiers, signs, currencies, grouped digits, units, scales, ranges, accounting negatives, full-width characters, missing values), the alignments the cells' bar and a column's menu offer (`page/table/aligns.mjs`) that a cell look's classes are in the table's description and `styles/table.css`, whether a table is plain, judged once by the server and marked on its unit (`data-ge-grid`: the tags a file leaves out, a tbody before the thead and cells that tile no rectangle are not plain, the page's grid is the marked units' alone, the operation on any other is refused, a table made on the page has the mark) and a commit building a table's grid for its operation once, a cell's start tag changed where it stands (a style with a semicolon in a url() or an entity written back as it was, the other attributes in their places), and the slots the kit, the page editor and the server read a table by (`src/shared/table-slots.mjs`: the DOM and the spans of an operation tile alike, or both tile no rectangle; the cell holding a column's width), and the kit's numeric columns (`cells.js`: none where a header says text or dates; a footer's cell of a number or of nothing in a numeric column takes its look, words and a cell spanning columns not); `tests/unit/runtime.mjs` also lays out flowcharts (`runtime/flowchart.js`): the lines a text takes as the browser breaks it (`linesOf`), a long run (`src/shared/long-runs.mjs`) and every run where the block breaks anywhere (`word-break: break-all`) each character its own piece, an ordinary word one, the step's widest word still the whole run (`stepShape`), a card's height with its content (`cardHeight`: a paragraph's lines, a list's items and what they hold at its indent, an empty block a line, a picture more than any card, the margin between two blocks the larger of the two and the last one's inside the card, a quote's paragraphs inside its padding), and side by side or one below another by the flowchart's width, its steps' widest words, their titles' lines and their cards' heights, a step holding a picture one below another, an author's `row` within its limits, a reader's weights in 自动 counting only where a card would be under its least width (`flowLayout` alone, and on a page: a direction fixed and taken away, an item of a list a step holds with a long word, a block with no box (an empty bookmark a reader is not shown) left out, the numbers drawn again by the kit's mark and never by the list's style), the layout and the name the kit gave taken off an export of the view, the name going with a flowchart that leaves the page; on paper a flowchart kept on one page (a print rule of its own), and the arrow a page's break parts from the row it leads to left out (`pagedApart`: a row not the row gap under the one before, a column too, a step not drawn left out; `flowRules` `cut`). It also holds the kit's tables with a merged header, body or footer cell (`mergedCells`), a footer's cells measured with the body's (a total widening its column), a rowspan past the header row left to the browser, the frame's narrowest width leaving with the layout that set it, and the direction a sort arrow carries (`data-doc-sort-arrow` is `asc` or `desc`).

### Browser suite (`npm run test:browser`)

`tests/browser/` drives the live preview in an installed Chrome or Edge through `puppeteer-core` (no browser is downloaded; `GALLERY_BROWSER=<executable>` picks one, otherwise the usual install locations of Chrome, Edge and Chromium are tried; `HEADFUL=1` shows the browser's window, drawing with the GPU as a reader's does). It is for maintainers: the release package does not include `tests/browser/`. Run it after changing the editor, the preview server, the commit endpoints, the workbench or the export; a full run takes over an hour (487 scenarios in 73 minutes on 2026-11-06; one alone takes seconds), and ends with its elapsed time (split into the test Gallery's start, the scenarios and its stop) and the ten slowest scenarios (each one's time includes the reset of the test Gallery before it), to plan a gate by.

- **The machine.** A run shares the machine with whoever is working at it. `harness.mjs` lowers the priority of the run and of all it starts, and a studio (one Chrome, about eight processes, and one server) holds one of the machine's `GALLERY_TEST_STUDIOS` slots (6) while it is open, whichever worktree or session it belongs to: past them a studio waits its turn, and a run says so once. A slot is a file in `gallery-browser-slots/` in the OS temp folder, kept fresh while its run lives, so that a killed run's slot is free after three minutes. Waiting ends in an error after an hour. The browser's processes are held at that low priority too (`keepLow`, again every three seconds, as Chrome starts renderers anew), so that when the machine is nearly full a keystroke's time to the next frame can stretch by seconds: typing is timed by `typeTimed` (`scenarios.mjs`) two ways, as the time from the keydown to the frame after it and as the main thread's CPU time (CDP `Performance.getMetrics` `ThreadTime`), which the machine's load does not stretch, and what the latency scenarios hold is the second.
- **A slow page.** `GALLERY_TEST_CPU_THROTTLE=<rate>` (4, 6, 12) slows each studio page's renderer that many times (`Emulation.setCPUThrottlingRate` through CDP), as a busy machine slows a reader's page, without loading the machine for the runs beside it; the server and the test's own steps run at full speed, so it slows the page alone (fuzz seed 4102's failure needed the opposite, the test's steps slow beside the page: the page's question asked before the Agent's write, which throttling the page makes rarer).
- **Sharding** (`node tests/browser/run.mjs --shards N [--retry] [scenario …]`, `tests/browser/shards.mjs`). The scenarios, all or those named, run in N runs at once instead of one after the other (a full pass took 73 minutes). Each run is a `run.mjs` of its own on a share of the scenarios, in `run.mjs`'s order, the shares balanced by what each scenario took the last time: every `run.mjs` writes its scenarios' times at its end into `node_modules/.cache/scenario-times.json` (merged over what it holds, written to a file and renamed, shared by the worktrees; a scenario never timed counts as the middle of the timed ones), and the longest go first, each into the share with the least so far. Each run has a range of ports of its own, `GALLERY_TEST_PORTS` cut into N equal parts when it is set (refused when a part would have under 6 ports), else 4400 upwards, 40 each, and a Vite cache of its own: `harness.mjs` gives each studio's server `GALLERY_VITE_CACHE_DIR`, which `src/vite.config.ts` takes `cacheDir` from, a folder under `node_modules/.cache/vite-test/` taken away with the studio (one a killed run left, by the next run once it is a day old; a value already in the environment is kept as it is), because servers of runs at once that shared `node_modules/.vite` answered `504 Outdated Optimize Dep` to each other (2026-11-08). The launcher builds the shared kit once before it starts them (`GALLERY_TEST_KIT_BUILT`: the harness then does not, the runs would write the same files at once). The runs hold the machine's studio slots as above, so shards past them wait their turn. Each run's whole output goes to `gallery-shards/<time>-<pid>/run-N.log` in the OS temp folder; the launcher says at the end each run's summary line, every FAIL line with its scenario, and the scenarios that failed, a run that stopped with no summary line having failed the one it stopped in and those after it (its last lines are shown), then the total. With `--retry` the scenarios that failed run once more, together in one run, and the ones that passed only then are named (flaky, or bound by the load of a shared machine): it exits 0 only if everything passed by then. Without it 1 on any failure; 2 on bad arguments. `tests/unit/shards.mjs` holds the arguments, the shares, the ranges, the times file and the reading of a run's output, and by their text the harness's and Vite's cache. 8 scenarios in 2 runs took 88 s where their times add up to about 114 s.
- **Isolation.** `harness.mjs` makes a temporary directory whose `reports/` is the workspace (`GALLERY_WORKSPACE`, laid out as any workspace is: the documents at its root, `exports/` and `.gallery/` with the outline, trash, 写作偏好 page and the editor's state in it), so documents, built previews, exports and the editor's state (token, snapshots) are read and written there, never in the project (see `src/shared/paths.mjs` and `workspace.mjs`). Between scenarios it takes away every document, page, outline and export a scenario made (and what `gallery.json` says of the custom pages, their order and `homeMade`, as of its default look), and keeps the document state (`.gallery/state/`) and shared service announcement (`<studio>/data/server.json`), as the edit state was kept before, but for the review of the Agent's changes (`.gallery/state/changes/`), taken away once the fixtures are written back, so that the last scenario's file never becomes the next one's baseline (the server's look at that write finds no review then, and only takes the file). Each scenario's browser context has 显示编辑框 on and 显示变更 off (the scenarios were written before either showed), unless the scenario keeps preferences of its own. It copies the fixture document into it (`sample`, the document the editing scenarios work on: `tests/browser/fixtures/sample/`, a copy of an earlier Component Catalog kept as it is, so that the Component Catalog can change without them), checks it once (`gallery check`: it must pass) and writes the shared kit files (`src/cli/build-kit.mjs`), and starts `vite` as `dev:local` does, with `GALLERY_NO_DEFAULT_HOME` set (no default home page: the workbench opens the sample when its address names nothing, as the scenarios were written for; the home page's own scenarios start it again without it, and every reset writes the home page's first-open note closed, `.gallery/hints.json`, which `homePageHint` takes away) (with `GALLERY_TEST_SERVER=release`, the release's own server instead, `<repository>/release/npm/dist/server.mjs` as `npm run release:build` made it, started from the home folder as a gallery command starts it: `GALLERY_TEST_PORTS=4200-4260 GALLERY_TEST_SERVER=release node tests/browser/run.mjs unchanged history …` runs the scenarios against the package), on a free port of its own (the first free one of `GALLERY_TEST_PORTS`, as `4200-4260`, when it is set; else one the system gives), so a preview server already running is left alone. Scenarios run on the fixtures given block ids (`addBlockIds()`, as a document is from its first version on); those in `cleanFixture` (lazy ids, the migration) get them as they are on disk. Downloads go to `downloads/` beside the workspace. Each scenario starts from the fixtures as they are on disk, in a fresh browser context (empty storage). The pages of the scenario before leave for about:blank before its context closes: Chrome runs the unload handlers of a page it closes and waits for its answer, and a page reloading by itself just then (after a foreign write, or after sending what the crash buffer held, as at the end of `crashBuffer` and `lostState`) never gives one, so the close would hang (`Target.disposeBrowserContext timed out`). Restarting the server stops and starts that process for real. Documents are type-checked as if they were in a `reports/` folder beside the SDK's (a path of the checker's alone, `src/render/typecheck.mjs`), so their JSDoc imports of `../../sdk/` resolve wherever they live. The studio's server and the commands it runs have `GALLERY_DATA_DIR` set to a folder of the run's own, so that the registry of workspaces ([internals.md](internals.md)) is never the machine's. `studioEnv(root)` is what they share besides: `TEMP`, `TMP` and `TMPDIR` set to `<studio>/tmp` (temporary command files stay inside the studio; every explicitly used workspace is registered in its isolated data directory), `GALLERY_DATA_DIR` to `<studio>/data`, and `GALLERY_DEFAULT_WORKSPACE` to the studio's own `reports/`, so that the studio is the default workspace as `~/Documents/Gallery` is a user's (its own column is 默认文档). The harness sets `GALLERY_NO_BROWSER=1` (no server of a studio opens a browser or a file manager) and `GALLERY_SITE_ICON=local` (a web link asks no real site for its icon). A scenario sees them in `t.env`, runs a command with others besides by `t.galleryWith(env, command, …args)` (`t.gallery` is it with none; `--workspace` selects another storage folder), and reads the shared service port in `t.port`.
- **Opening a document.** `t.open()` opens the page as a reader new to the document does: a one-shot `evaluateOnNewDocument` takes every `gallery-edit:*:journal` key (the editor's per-document journal of what its page committed and made, kept in `localStorage` so that a page opened on another version of the file can tell the Agent's write from the reader's own: [editor.md](editor.md), "Someone else's writes") out of storage before the page's scripts run. A scenario that writes its fixture anew and then calls `t.open()` therefore starts fresh, as it did before the journal existed; one that is about the journal opens its page again by `page.reload()`, by the workbench or by a new tab, which keep it (`agentRevertAfterReload`, `agentRevertReopened`, `agentRevertNewTab`). `pasteBench.reset(t)` writes the bench back and calls `t.open()`.
- **Scenarios** (`scenarios.mjs`, run in order, or by name: `npm run test:browser -- agentMerge conflict`). The page edits from the start and saves by itself; `t.settle()` waits for `data-ge-save` to say `saved`, and `t.offline()` makes commits fail as if the server could not be reached (to hold an edit back until an Agent has written, or to leave it in the crash buffer). Opening, and a character typed and deleted again, send no commit and leave the file byte for byte, with no toolbar (`unchanged`); one edited paragraph saves after the pause, without a reload, and changes only that paragraph and the ids around it (`oneParagraph`); after the migration, a paragraph the Agent moved to another chapter and changed is found by its id and both edits go in there, and the page then shows the file (`blockIds`); typing, a split, a keyboard move and a drag, saved as they go, undo back to the page as it opened (and the file with it) and redo to the end (`history`); an Agent's write to the same paragraph elsewhere is merged, said, and shown after the page reloads by itself (`agentMerge`); both changing the same words holds that paragraph back alone, flagged with a card and its word diff, while other edits save and the page does not reload; 保留我的 writes the reader's words, 用文件里的 gives a second one up and shows the file's; a new block the file cannot take (a list written in a section the Agent deleted meanwhile) is held back too, Ctrl+S says so, the page does not reload over it, its card has the reader's words, and left and opened again the page lists them until the reader is done with them (`conflict`); no notice covers a bottom panel: on a page alone at 1024 wide the toast stands above the conflict's card as it is held and after Ctrl+S, comes back to its place once 收起 hides the card, and stands above the panel of what a page opened again could not put back, and in the workbench at 1280 its toast stands above the card in the frame, and at 360 wide each toast stands inside the window, more than half of it wide, its words wrapped (`noticeClearsPanels`); with a conflict card standing, the caret at the end of a paragraph under it and words typed, then Enter and more words, its line comes above the card (the page's scroll-padding-bottom is the room the panels take and 8px), and the card gone the room is given back (`caretClearsPanels`); a new disclosure whose title is still empty is not reloaded away when the Agent writes the file, and given a title it is saved and the page shows the write (`unfinishedBlock`); an Agent rewriting the whole file from what it read before the reader's saved edit gets a card instead of a reload, and 恢复我的 writes the edit again (`agentRevert`), and so does a block the reader made that such a write took out (`agentRevertBlock`); a paragraph the reader wrote (saved) that the Agent, asked to, replaced with a picture (no id, the picture's file first), in the middle of a section and as the last of one (a figure with a caption): no card and nothing said, the picture taken in where the page stands, no reload; one the Agent took out with nothing in its place still a card (`agentReplacesNewBlock`, which failed before: the card came, the page held behind it, the feedback of 2026-11-17); in a document without ids (the usual case), no card either with the paragraph after the reader's reworded by the same write, the reader's replaced together with the one after it, and the replacement in two writes 3.5 s apart, a card while the paragraph is out that goes once the picture stands where it stood, the page then showing the file, the cards' panel gone and the bar of the Agent's changes back in the middle of the bottom line (it runs with the Agent's changes shown, as they are by default); a write from what the Agent read before the reader appended their paragraph, its own appended there, still a card, the units beside it named by the reader's commit and that write having them without (`agentReplacesAround`: the first three failed before, each a card, the review of 2026-11-17; the last guards that a block beside it the write has without its id is not stepped over as one it took out); such a write landing after the page's last question before it shows an Agent's earlier write and before the page shown again is served (each answer to the revert check held in the page until the test writes): the page shown again opens on it and asks at once, the text put back a card and the block the reader made listed with its words, the list shown (`agentRevertAtReload`, which failed before, the page shown again holding none of them, the file neither; its write of words alone the page would take in where it stands, so it runs with the pull off); an Agent's write of words alone taken in where the page stands (editor.md "The pull"), in the workbench with no page loaded beside it and no swap, the reader's caret, its place among their unsaved words and their typing kept (`agentPullInPlace`), their earlier typing undone and redone three ways after it, the Agent's words kept (`agentPullUndo`), a paragraph split off with Enter, the Agent's words pulled into it, the split undone: the paragraph goes, a card asks and the file keeps the Agent's words, 保留 Agent 的 showing them (`agentPullUndoSplit`, which before took them out with no card), an input method's composition left whole, the pull waiting for its end (`agentPullIme`), a paragraph put in and a table put in both taken in where the page stands, with no reload (`agentPullStructure`); and the Agent's blocks put in, taken out and moved: paragraphs added at the end while the reader types near the top, their caret and unsaved words kept, every key saved where typed, their undo and redo taking out and putting back their own words alone (`agentPullAppend`), paragraphs added above where the reader reads, the paragraph at the window's top standing where it stood, the page scrolled by what went in (`agentPullInsertAbove`), a paragraph the reader is not in taken out, their split before it undone and redone with the deletion standing (`agentPullDelete`), the section the reader types in moved out of a Sheet's panel to the top of the page, the same element with the caret and their words in it, their keys saved where typed and their undo and redo after it leaving the section where the Agent put it (`agentPullMoveSection`, which found that a tab's shell held its panel's words: every write into a panel was a swap), the paragraph the reader is typing in taken out, their commit first and a card with their words, nothing lost (`agentPullDeleteTyping`), a section the reader types in replaced by an unrelated one of the same shape at the end, the reader's next words where the Agent's paragraph reads alike around them: a card with their words, the Agent's paragraph untouched (`agentPullReplacedSection`, which before a review's fix merged them into it silently), the paragraph the reader split off moved by the Agent to the end of the page, the split's undo refused and said, its words once (`agentPullUndoSplitMoved`, which before put them back twice), an input method composing while a paragraph is put in, nothing pulled until it ends (`agentPullImeStructure`), and a burst of paragraphs put in, a write every 2.5 s while the reader types in the workbench, each taken in where the page stands, no page loaded beside it, never inert, every key in the file (`agentPullBurst`); a write adding the page's script (a chart's type) a second after one the pull took in, the Agent writing on (a paragraph every 2 s), the reader's caret in a list item of the summary where they typed and saved: shown anew once the reader paused, while the Agent still writes (not at the end of its writing: a write the pull takes in makes no burst), the chart drawn, the caret back in its item, every write after it shown (`agentPullScriptAfterPull`); a tab area written as plain sections under `data-doc-sheets` put on the sample (which loads sheets.js) in place, and on a page with none (served without sheets.js) by the page shown anew, each with its first tab chosen and that panel alone shown (`agentPullSheetsArrive`); changes that are not words (formulas, instances, tables, code blocks; since 2026-11-13): formulas the Agent changes and puts in (inline and a display formula) drawn in place while the reader writes elsewhere, and the formula box open holding a write back until it closes, both in the file (`agentPullFormula`), an instance of a package rewritten while on screen put in whole and mounted by its component, the one it replaced gone (`agentPullInstance`), a table with its scope line put in above the paragraph the reader types in, its cells text blocks, the caret and the paragraph where they stood (`agentPullTableAbove`), 用文件里的 on a card about words showing the file's words in place, the block editable again (`agentPullTheirs`), and on a card about a paragraph the reader split (Enter) that the Agent reworded, with words typed in the block split off while that commit was on its way, the page shown anew with pull on and off alike, the Agent's words once, nothing of the block split off written then or later, and the words the card did not show listed (`agentPullSplitTheirs`: fuzz `--words` 9304, which before dropped them without a word with the pull off, and with it on wrote the block split off again, its words twice), a page opened on its own pulling a code block in and showing a head change by the browser's reload (`agentPullStandalone`), and the tab id a workbench swap answers with (`workbenchSwapTab`: it changes, from one id to another, and nothing is lost by it: a write after the swap putting the reader's saved words back is a card on the page shown anew), and a write putting the reader's saved words back landing while the page reads itself for a pull asked of, a card (`agentRevertAtPull`); `t.reloaded()` waits for a reload or a pull that leaves nothing of the file to show and nothing waiting (`data-ge-pulls` grown, no `data-ge-behind`, `data-ge-save` saved or held), as a page loaded anew, and says which with `GALLERY_TEST_SHOW_RELOADS` set (`t.lastShown`), and `GALLERY_TEST_PULL=off` runs any scenario with the pull off; the same where no page was there to hear the write, asked at once by the page that opens next: the reader's reload (F5) right as the Agent writes, a reload on the file as the page left it asking nothing (`agentRevertAfterReload`), the document opened again in the workbench after another one (`agentRevertReopened`) and in a new tab while the tab that had it shows something else (`agentRevertNewTab`), each failing before (no card, nothing listed); the Agent's changes marked on the page shown again (+2 −2 on the bar, the new paragraph filled green as an overlay of its own, the words added highlighted, the words taken out shown where they were and none of the paragraph's text, the list item deleted a ghost in room of its own over no block, no card and no red line) and none of it in the file an edit then saves (`agentChangesShown`); the words taken out and the blocks deleted none of the document: drawn, nothing waits to be saved; a copy of the changed paragraph (its text, its HTML, the page's own blocks) carries none of the words taken out; typed into, they go first and the file holds exactly the reader's edit, and they are not drawn in it again while the caret is there; undo puts none back; the caret gone, they show again; an input method composing in it takes them out first and its words go in as typed; 确认 takes every mark, ghost and room away, and so does 撤回 after another write (`agentChangesInline`); a selection across blocks begun between a paragraph's marks deleting what it holds with Backspace, its tail among it, and staying on the same words while the review is read again (`agentChangesAcross`); the paragraph before a list and the list's first item deleted one ghost over no block, and a Sheet's middle tab deleted leaving the other tabs in one row, its ghost before the bar (`agentChangesRooms`); a column fitted by a double click as wide with a cell's marks shown as with none, and 标题行 turned off leaving no copy of a header cell's mark, nothing said of a stray (`agentChangesInTable`); the reader's own edits never counted (one saved before the Agent's write, one in a block it did not touch and a paragraph made after, one typed into the Agent's paragraph), on the page opened again too (`agentChangesOwnEdits`); who a change belongs to after the conflict card about words both changed (the summary's first item): 保留我的 leaving the words kept unmarked and no bar with nothing else of the Agent's, then with another paragraph of the same write changed that one alone marked and counted (+1 −1), the reader's edit in that paragraph apart from the Agent's words unmarked, and 用文件里的 showing the Agent's words marked as the Agent's (`agentChangesKeepMine`; the unit side in `tests/unit/changes.mjs`, kept again after a second refusal too); the reader's words the file does not have never marked as the Agent's: the item the reader and the Agent's write both changed unmarked while the reader's words wait to be sent and while the card stands, drawn again too (the red number off and on), the other paragraph of the write marked and the bar counting both, 用文件里的 marking the Agent's words, and a word typed in the item and not sent as the Agent writes elsewhere leaving it unmarked until, undone, the caret leaves it (`agentChangesConflictMarks`); a bar 2px wide 6px left of the words beside the paragraph an Agent added and the one whose words alone it changed, a red one on the ghost, the write lighting all three as it arrives (the fill and the ghost marked arriving, a glow over the paragraph of words) and resting 1.4s later, nothing lit again by the +N switch off and on (the green bars going and coming with it), or by the page opened again, with reduced motion the next write lit over 400ms with no hold and resting soon after, a page shown anew by a load for someone else's write lighting what that write brought alone, and 确认 fading the marks out before they go (`agentChangesBars`); the card given up (用文件里的) in a commit that saved another paragraph of the reader's too: the item marked as the Agent's change of the words before its write, not of those given up, and 撤回 writing those words back, never the ones given up, the saved paragraph kept (`agentChangesGivenUp`; the unit side in `tests/unit/changes.mjs`: a text, a deletion refused, words kept and refused again, words in a block the Agent deleted, a commit writing ids alone, a list deleted item by item with one item refused (`went`), 仍然删除 alone and beside another block, 保留我的 after 确认; and in `tests/unit/write-back.mjs` a component's write that partly went in, then 仍然调整); 确认 ending the review, its baseline gone, and the Agent's next write all the next one counts (`agentChangesAccept`); 撤回 refused while an edit cannot be saved, then the baseline written with the reader's saved edits in it and doc.json's title back, and 撤销 bringing the Agent's version and its review back (`agentChangesRevert`); `tableViewLatency`: typing twenty characters into a cell of a table of 500 rows that three views read, each keystroke read once for the three (their rows the same objects) and one card of each view made again, and a keystroke's cost, the CPU time of the page's main thread (`typeTimed`, CDP `Performance.getMetrics` `ThreadTime`; the time from its keydown to the frame after it printed beside), its median no more than `VIEW_ADDS` (60%) over the same table's with no view, measured in the same run, and its 90th percentile no more than `VIEW_LATENCY` (3) times that median. The bounds were 60 ms and 250 ms of the time to the frame, set with the engine of a table's views on 2026-11-08 (27 ms and 147 ms measured then, the table alone about 95 ms a keystroke, most of it the kit's own layout of the table, `tables.js` `refreshLayout`), which that same version failed as main did later that day with other runs going on, the two run in turn three times each (the table alone about 200–230 ms a keystroke and the views adding about 70–80 ms on both; a keystroke's frame waiting seconds for the page's turn on a machine near full): the main thread's CPU time grows with the load too (about 90 ms a keystroke quiet, 205 busy), but the views' share of it held at 35% to 41% that day where the time to the frame's ranged from 25% to 109%. Its commit is waited for by `t.settle()`'s 15 s, which every commit of that table ran past 40 s (about 60–70 s, both commits) until `commit.mjs` found the cells the answer names by an index of their paths (`pathIndex`: about 0.3 s since).
