typing filtering it, the section of what it finds kept; Gallery's own picked (`gallery/countdown`) put into `components/`, its block running, its card written at the first free spot by its default size, the page shown again with it; Ctrl+Z on that page taking it out, Ctrl+Shift+Z putting it back; the block turned off turned on again (`gallery.json`), the page, whose only instance of it is held in a template, shown again with its card running. `pageBoardBlockDrag`: a block's own drag (`DocKit.drag`) writing its table and moving no card, undone by Ctrl+Z; a drag on a card's body moving none. `pageBoardAgent` (in the workbench): Escape while a card is held putting it back (the page took the keyboard as its grip was pressed); the page's own write not showing it again; an Agent's write of a card showing it again at once, and a write of another card going on working; a field typed in and not left keeping the page from being shown again; the move of a card the Agent wrote refused and said in the workbench's toast, the Agent's write kept; the field left, its write going and the page shown again; `gallery page add` while the page is open going through the server's queue, its card id said, and the open page shown again with the card. `pageBoardTwenty`: twenty cards from the address to drawn (every instance of a block with a script mounted), said in milliseconds, under 2 s the first time and again. `boardFixedCells` (in the workbench, the default home page's six cards of Gallery's own blocks; the user's word of 2026-11-09): at 1440 and at 1280, the sidebar open at its default width and folded, twelve columns of 64px cells each time, 944px, the cards in the middle of the frame, every card's box the same size open and folded (folding only moves them right), nothing scrolling sideways, the file untouched; `GALLERY_TEST_SHOTS=<folder>` keeps the home page at 1440 open and folded, at 1920, and narrow (the sidebar at its widest, 480px, on a 1280 window). `boardNarrowView` (the page alone; a board's two views, the user's word of 2026-11-13): at 1280 the wide view, twelve columns, each card at its place; at 760 the narrow view's 8 columns of 64px, never arranged yet so the cards one after another in their order, each as large as it is, in the middle, each block told its size, nothing of the narrow view written, the grip's tooltip saying it drags; a drag two rows down there writes every card's narrow place (`data-narrow-*`), the cards it meets pushed down, the wide places as they were, the narrow view drawn as written; at 1280 again the wide view as the file says, at 760 again the narrow view as arranged; Ctrl+Z in the wide view takes the narrow drag back, the wide places untouched; at 390 a phone's room, 4 columns of 64px, the narrow view's cards flowed, an 8 wide card held to 4 and its block told so, its grip's tooltip saying 点击打开菜单 and 窗口宽一些才能拖动 and a drag of it moving nothing; at 320 4 smaller cells; nothing sideways, the grip (shown on a card as the window narrowed) on that card's top-right corner inside the window, and one set at 900px widening nothing (the layer is clipped at the page's width). `pageBoardMove` drags a card in the narrow view (760) too: its narrow places written, every other byte as it was, a click on the grip opening the menu. `customPageCards`' block says `places` document and page: a block whose places lack page does not run on a page.
- **设置 › 组件列表** ([render.md](render.md), "Settings"): `settingsListWaits`: the page's own reads of its list held or failed: a custom block hidden and one disabled gone from "/" at once, reads that cross going by the last one begun, a custom block off brought by no paste, an instance of a changed custom block back while the list is on its way or after it failed asking for the page, and one of no custom block back after another changed asking for nothing; `settingsDialogFocus`: a page shown again under the dialog with the caret in a block of an id, a key going nowhere behind it, Esc closing it, and the caret back with the focus; `settingsMenus`: a custom block hidden and one disabled gone from the real "/", 建议 and a search by its name among it, and 在下方新建, on the page that is open with nothing shown again and after a reload, what its map says, the instance of the one hidden going by its name, copied as itself and back from an undo running, and on again, on a page served with it off and holding none, its row back and the block it makes bringing its custom block, with no page shown again; `settingsHidden`: a custom block hidden, heard by the open page with nothing shown again, its instances running and its map saying its state, after a reload, in `gallery export`, the workbench's 导出 and a view copy, `gallery check` saying nothing; `settingsDisabled`: a custom block with a script and one with none turned off while a page holding them is open, a word saved just before, the page swapped in with no frame undrawn, no tag and no route of theirs, the fallback saying why and using no style of theirs, after a reload, each export opened from its file with the network off, on again as they were, and a page holding none left alone; `settingsHeldWord`: a word typed and not saved as its custom block goes off, held on the page, a card and a notice saying what happened and blaming no file, the file as it was, and the file's taken for the fallback; `settingsInstanceBack`: an undo that brings an instance back to a page that held none as its custom block went off or came on, the page shown again for the one and bringing the other, as the file now is and taking what is typed, and no page shown again for a custom block hidden; `fallbackNoteHandle`: the handle on a fallback's note as on what it holds; `settingsMissing`: an acceptance check of the fallbacks: an instance of no custom block, the same fallback in a preview, after a reload and in each export, `gallery check`'s note; `settingsBlocks`: a built-in hidden while the page is open, gone from "/", its suggestions, 在下方新建 and the handle's grid with no page shown again and after a reload, a block of it keeping its mark, 组成分区, a turn-into and its Markdown shortcut reaching it, back at once, and the four staying whether the server is asked or a hand wrote it; `settingsJunk`: gallery.json holding what it may, no page shown again, the instances running, what can be read read, the rest passed over, `gallery check` running, a slip in the file turning nothing back on and said to who asks, a write over a file that does not read refused and the file as it was; `settingsShown`: a custom block of a custom page's alone (`places: ["page"]`) in no document's "/" or 在下方新建 and its map saying `hidden`, switched on (`shown` written, the open page hearing the settings line with it, "/" and 在下方新建 offering it) and off again with no page shown again, a document's block beside it going by `hidden` as ever, and its row in 设置 › 组件列表 saying where it stands and what it uses, its switch off and writing `shown`; `settingsPage`: the page itself, each built-in line with the "/" row's icon left of its name (代码块's, 分区's 组成新分区), each custom block's line one line high with the one mark of a custom block (`BLOCK_ICON`, 20px, inside its button, on the line of its name) and one master switch, on, a click on the line opening its id and version, words, state and the sentence of its master switch, its other switches each with its own words, and another click shutting it, a grey word on a shut line for one disabled or not loaded, why one does not load, one with no template, a built-in's switch named as a custom block's (在文档菜单里显示, on for what the menus offer), the switches written to the file (the master switch off and on leaving `unlisted` an empty list) and locked where they cannot move, the four, the focus going in, round and back, an id and a version in one piece at a phone's width, what is written elsewhere followed while it is open and the lines as open as they were, a slip locking every switch until it is mended, Esc and a press outside, in English, and an empty workspace's one line; `settingsMaster`: the master switch pressed for real in the dialog (a click on the line opens it and a click on the switch opens nothing), the block gone from a document's "/" and 在下方新建 and from a custom page's 添加自定义块, the instance already in the document showing as ever and staying in the file, `components.unlisted` written and the workbench's list saying it, and on again by the same switch with all of it back (`GALLERY_TEST_SHOTS=<folder>` keeps the pictures of 组件列表 shut and with two lines open, and of the mark in "/" and in 添加自定义块, light and dark, at 1x and 2x).
- **What a change of the kit reaches** (`kit-diff.mjs`: `node tests/browser/kit-diff.mjs report.css=<earlier file> tables.js=<earlier file> … [--reports <slug>,…] [--ignore <regex>] [--limit <n>]`). The editing scenarios' sample document (`tests/browser/fixtures/sample/`, with its two sticky-first tables), the Component Catalog and, when `GALLERY_WORKSPACE` names a workspace, every document of it (or those of `--reports`; with none named the tool's workspace is the unit tests' empty temporary folder, `tests/unit/workspace-env.mjs`, which adds none), each exported by the tool itself (`gallery export --all`, in a temporary workspace that holds copies of them: the named workspace is only read, nothing in its `exports/` is written, so no export can be older than the tokens or the sources; a document that fails its checks stops the run with exit code 2), and a page of the kit's components nested in each other (the four media blocks among them: captioned, with an empty caption, spanned, in a column, left empty), each with the given earlier kit files inlined and with the kit as `src/render/kit/` builds it now, compared element by element: its attributes (a table's column widths, a chart's geometry, what the kit's scripts set; ids a chart numbers as it draws are left out) and its computed style with its `::before`, `::after` and `::marker`, at 1280, 700, 600 and 400px (the kit's breakpoints are 760, 640 and 420), light and dark, in print, and with reduced motion, on every Sheet. The page of nested components also holds cells with fills and header cells that stick, in a panel and a disclosure of a color, headings deep in a section (a callout's, a card's, a quote's), and a numbered code block and diff. Reorganizing the kit must leave nothing different (the split of `report.css`, `tables.js` and `charts.js` into `styles/`, `tables/` and `charts/` left 104,187 element states the same); a change to one component's styles lists every place it reaches. Keep a copy of the kit file before changing it (`public/kit/` holds the last build). A change that deletes a custom property shows it on every element as gone: `--ignore` leaves the properties whose names match out (the names deleted on purpose). `--limit <n>` raises or lowers the differences printed for each page and setting (40 by default; the count above them is always complete, and the last line of a long list says how many were left out). The pages are drawn with the media type and the features set together in one `Emulation.setEmulatedMedia` call (light or dark, reduced motion, screen or print): puppeteer's `emulateMediaType` and `emulateMediaFeatures` each replace what the other set, so the kit's reduced-motion rules were never in the comparison before; the nine settings above draw with motion as it is, and a tenth (1280px, light, reduced motion) is the one that sees a `prefers-reduced-motion` rule of the kit. Each page is waited for until its scripts have stopped drawing (its code blocks colored first, `DocCode.colorAll()`, which a kit that colors them once the page is idle has; then the styles they set the same in four looks 100ms apart, not a wait of fixed length), each of the two in front while it is read, and a Sheet other than the first is drawn from the start with that Sheet remembered (the page's state in `localStorage`), as a reader coming back finds it: a Sheet shown after the page was drawn is laid out by what happens to be going on then. A margin that is `auto` is read from where the element is drawn (its distance from its container's edge), not from the computed property, which Chrome does not always give the same for the same page. The same kit against itself exits 0 (five runs in a row on the page of nested components, and a document with the sample once: 80,640 element states in 6 minutes). The reduced-motion setting leaves the sample out: some of its tables (in a column, in its second and third Sheets) end at one of two widths from one load to the next when motion is reduced, as a reader with that setting would see them, which the layout in `tables/layout.js` should not allow (a finding of the tool, not fixed by it). A table in a Sheet of a workspace's longer document did the same at 1280px with motion reduced: the kit against itself found it at one width or the other on one load and the next (2026-11-07, the document look's branch), so a difference there alone is no change of the kit's. Interactions (sorting, a chart's own controls) are not part of it.
- **The "/" menu's trigger and its field** ([editor.md](editor.md), "New blocks and conversions"; 2026-11-20): `slashOnlyTyped` (a deletion, a cut, an undo or redo, a paste, the arrow keys and a script's selection that leave the caret after a "/", "、" or "@" open no menu; a key, an input method's text and a composition committed alone or with a phrase still do) and `slashField` (the pale field over "/", "/gs" and a "、" amid words stays in its line, clear of the lines above and below and of the characters beside it, with 0.5px corners, in light and dark; `GALLERY_SHOTS=<folder>` saves close-up pictures of it).
- **Still by hand.** Input methods cannot be driven reliably by synthetic key events, so before a release check with a real Chinese IME (Microsoft Pinyin or similar) on Windows: typing and confirming a phrase in a paragraph, Enter while composing (confirms, does not split), Backspace while composing, typing in a cell, a disclosure title and a renamed Sheet tab, and that undo after composing takes back the whole phrase. On a Mac (the scenarios judge the colour and the smoothing by emulation only, and nothing here runs Safari), the body text's colour and stroke weight (#262626, antialiased) next to DingTalk's, as the user sees them (decided 2026-11-08). In a real browser, the sidebar's web links (since 2026-11-08): one site that can be embedded and one that refuses to be (github.com: its card and 在浏览器中打开), and entries dragged between columns by hand. Visual QA (layout, fonts, dark mode) stays manual as well.

`tests/unit/block-ids.mjs` covers block ids: that they bind nothing, finding blocks through `commitEdits()` by id when the Agent moved and changed them (a card's text through its card), identical paragraphs, a repeated id a conflict and a vanished one found as the page opened the block, deletions around an edit as conflicts, lazy ids (edits, card text, splits, moves, sizes, new blocks; kept on conversion; none without an edit), the migration (nothing else changed, repeated ids, a second run; what it gave listed in the file's order by start tag, `startTagLabel()`, a value's line breaks and tabs one space, and found at the line its start tag opens on, `placeGivenIds()`, as `blocksWithoutIds()` listed it before, a start tag over several lines too; an empty id replaced, not followed by a second), what `gallery check` says an export will give an id (`pendingBlockIds()`: the page as shown, at the file's lines; none for repeated ids), the repeated-id check (the Component Catalog given ids passing it, an old `"template": true` changing nothing), the random ids the page and the server make (`src/shared/ids.mjs`: block ids and commit ids, one format each), ids through the commit endpoint, a unit's hash the same with ids and without (its own and its blocks', written without quotes or in single quotes too; given to start tags that end in whitespace, `<hr />`, a `>` on a line of its own; written in capitals, for `locate()` too; an id a code sample spells counts), and the hand-in (`POST /__gallery/edit/hand-in`, `handIn`: the file as pages see it with every block an id, each said with its line in the file written, its start tag and its id, written once, a second hand-in doing nothing; repeated ids left as they are, an old `"template": true` changing nothing; a command of another directory of documents refused).

`tests/unit/pull.mjs` covers the page's pull ([editor.md](editor.md), "The pull"): what the server tells apart (a unit's shell and the document's frame, `source/units.mjs`: the same for words changed, the title, ids given and another indentation, an item added to a list leaving the list's; another for a class, the head), carried by the page's map and a commit's answer; the plan (`page/pull/plan.mjs`, on what `commits.pullView()` gives of a page opened on a file and the map of the file served after a write): nothing to show for the same file, the words of a paragraph, a nested item and the title to show, units put in, taken out and moved counted (a paragraph added or taken out, an item added, a paragraph made a heading taken out and a heading put in, units swapped by their ids and with none by what they say, the first taken out and one added at the end, an item taken out and another added, a section moved, a section's blocks taken out of it, a paragraph put in a new section, words changed in place; in a Sheet, words in a panel, a paragraph put in and taken out of one, a section moved out of one to the top, each explained by the tab whose hash holds its panel's), no plan for a class, the head, words the page cannot show in place or a panel's own markup, an id an export gave taken, units moved around id-less ones never paired as words changed (`shift`; words written into one of two empty paragraphs is in place), and a hash changed with no words changed refused (a `<pre>` no text block is indented otherwise, whitespace in an attribute's value, with words changed in that unit too); and, in jsdom with the server played (`page-harness.mjs`, the page served as the preview serves it), the pull taken in (`pull/apply.mjs`): words shown in two paragraphs with nothing left to commit and the page's commits after it written where they should (a block pulled edited from the file's words), a block the reader changed and has not saved keeping their words while the others show the file's (`partial`, its commit then merging), a changed block in a unit with no id committed first (`idless`), a paragraph added after the one the reader types in, one taken out, the section moved, in place: the reader's words and the same element kept, nothing to commit but their words, which go in where typed, the undo of their split before it taking out none of the Agent's, and a table put in or a formula no pull; the paragraph the reader types in taken out committed first, a card (`busy`), the one the caret is in left for later (`held`), a paragraph in a section the Agent made a callout of moved into the callout as the same element (fuzz `--structure` seed 9205: once looked up after the section left the page), the reader's paragraph split off and moved by the Agent within its section still taken out by the split's undo, the reader's paragraph put before a section the file has after it pairing the section all the same; and a review of 2026-11-13, each probe that reproduced: units known by an id the file has no more never paired by place (a section replaced by an unrelated one, two steps written back swapped without their ids: their unsaved words committed first, a card, with the pull as without it, and through the autosave), the journal of a block taken out carried (written back as it was: a card), what each kind it puts in (a paragraph, a heading, a list with nested items, sub-items put under an item and taken from one, a section, a callout, a quote, a flowchart, columns) element for element as a load makes it and a detail block and a Sheet not put in, the split's undo after the paragraph split off moved into another section and a step of words in a paragraph taken out refused, a paragraph put at a box's top before a table's scope line, a box of 1,500 paragraphs with one moved no plan (`large`), a paragraph put in between a selection's ends held, the caret in a paragraph taken out held while the reader is at it and then the page shown anew; and a table and a formula put in in place and the head no plan, the same through the autosave, each kind it puts in or replaces (a table with its scope line and one with a row added, a code block and one whose code changed, a display formula and one in words, a picture and one whose source changed, a card, a callout recoloured, a detail block, a Sheet and a tab renamed) element for element as a load makes it, 用文件里的 on a card about words shown by a pull with no reload and a write the reader asked for (showOwn) taken in where the page stands, twin paragraphs with no id after a paragraph the reader added named by their places in the version pulled (the second edited, not the first), the autosave taking a write in with no reload, its late announcement no write of anyone else's, a write of more than words after those it took in shown by a reload once the reader has paused (writes the pull takes in make no burst; 2026-11-14), a head changed no pull whichever version was read (`frame`, never `stale`), a second head 5 s after the first, the page shown anew between them (the tab's sessionStorage kept), one of a burst and shown once the file is still, a write heard while the page waits for the reader's pause shown with it and making no burst; a tab area as an Agent writes it put on a page served with the kit's tags (`withKitTags`) in place as a load makes it where sheets.js is there and no pull (`kit`) where it is not, and `kitMissing` taking a kit file the editor loaded by its address as the page's; a write putting the reader's saved words back a card with nothing pulled while the reader decides; undo of a split and redo of a deletion after the Agent's words were pulled into the block a card, the file keeping them (`commits.unseen`); a version pulled before and written back once the page knows another a write of theirs (shown, or a card for the reader's words it put back); a pull that leaves the page holding something to commit shown anew, the next page sending the reader's unsaved words from the crash buffer; and what a page of an earlier Gallery kept of the version it opened on (the count and hashes) read as each unit's number there.

`tests/unit/settle.mjs` covers half-written files ([editor.md](editor.md), "Writing"): the signs `halfWritten()` (`source/whole.mjs`) tells one by, on files mid-way through a change (a disclosure's start written before its end or its end first, a section wrapped or its end cut, the main not closed, a table not closed, a Sheet area's tabs with no panels or three tabs and one panel, a panel twice), and the files it must not hold (an unclosed `<p>`, `<li>`, `<td>`… a browser closes as its author meant, comments spelling block tags, a script containing `"<div>"` and end tags, a JSON script, a style, code samples in `pre › code` and end tags in `kbd`, `samp`, `var`, an attribute's value, a textarea, a drawing, a template, a byte order mark; a raw `<div>` in a paragraph's inline code is one, as a browser opens the block there), each sign with its tag as written; when `createSettle()` (`settle.mjs`, on a clock of its own) takes a file (held until it is whole, or for `HOLD` from its last write, so a slow change of edits 20s apart is never given up between two, and at most `LIMIT` from the write that made it half written; what made it odd then holding it no more while a new sign does, a div opened where the taken one was closed too; a file seen half written long after its write taken at once, pages told after the quiet, the service's own write at once); the announcer (`src/server/announce.mjs`) telling pages of a second edit whose event came while the first was judged at once, not after the first's hold, finding a held file whole within `recheck` with no event, and telling a taken file after its quiet; the edit service holding a commit until the second edit lands (applied to the whole file, no recent version of the half-written one), answering a commit and the revert check busy after its wait, `whenTaken` giving the file as it is when the wait ends, and taking an odd file after `HOLD`; a page's load of a held file served what the preview last took after the wait (`readTaken`, at once when asked not to wait, the file as it is on a server that has taken none), a commit sent again answered at once from what it kept, the reader's hand-in busy on a held file and keeping what it wrote of natural HTML (the Agent's own keeping nothing); and what a reader's commit wrote of natural HTML as the kit's markup (a chart with its key), kept in the state directory under its directory of documents or in memory, taken once (one that cannot be taken away, locked, stopping nothing), and said by `rewriteLines()` with its time (the date when it is not today).

`tests/unit/routes.mjs` covers the preview server's routes (`src/server/routes.mjs`, called as either server calls them, with a request and a response of their own): a request naming another host (or no port, or another) refused with 403 before anything; of the routes that hand out a file, nothing there answered 404 and nothing else, a page icon's type and caching, the type and caching of an editor module (kept for good under a stamp, `no-store` by its path alone), mhchem and the emoji data, a slug only as a document's slug, and the server's own writers (`files.mjs`, a component's `file.mjs`) never served; the stamps of files (`src/server/stamps.mjs`); and, in a Gallery directory of its own (`fixtures/preview-pages.mjs`), a document's page as the server keeps it (`src/server/page-cache.mjs`): the editor and the kit's files under their stamps and answered for good, the same page for the same text, made again at once when a file of its folder is put in or taken out, once the watcher tells of a change the folder's times do not show, and once the server takes a new catalog of custom blocks, which its live-reload script then knows; in another (`fixtures/custom-pages.mjs`), a custom page at `/pages/<slug>` (the kit's tags every custom page loads and the board's, after its main and under their stamps, no other, its live script on `?page=<slug>` told the version it was served at, the workbench's session module, no editor nor display button, its page map saying the home page's first-open note (`hint`) until it is closed for the workspace; a page not there, a slug that is none and its files by their path passed on; its own files at `/pages/<slug>/assets/<file>`, a picture by its type and `no-cache`, an HTML file handed out for saving (octet-stream, attachment, `nosniff`), none for a name the route takes none by, another page's or a slug that is none, nothing for a folder in `assets/`, `..` or `page.json`; its references to them addressed there as it is served, one the route takes none by as written, the file as it was; a block's write of a picture through the server's page service, a POST of the page's own, written to the file with its folder's address and answered with the parts as the page shows them, which the page's undo puts back), what the watcher makes of a change under `pages/` (a page's file, a write's temp file, a document called `pages` and its pictures), and its channel told a write of `page.html` once, by its version, and the catalog and settings as it connects; and the route of a custom block's files (`src/server/component-route.mjs`): what its manifest allows, each by its type with `nosniff` (a module and WebAssembly as a browser asks), for good under its stamp and `no-store` by its path alone, under another stamp or written since, the disk looked at for its own stamp alone, 404 and no one else's for anything else of its folder or beside it, another case, a colon, a dot or a space at the end of a name, an encoded way out, a link or junction out of the folder, and a custom block that does not load, which the server's log says as it starts and as it comes; and what a page's stream hears, the catalog as it connects, then which custom block changed; a file of a document's folder written again (`src/server/asset-news.mjs`): no stamp as a page connects before any, `asset new` to the page open once it is quiet, `asset now` to one that connects after (what was told, no file looked at), nothing for one taken away and forgotten for the next; a page icon's picture of the folder tagged by its stamp, `no-cache`, 304 while the browser holds it and the new bytes once it changed, 404 for a slug that is none; and (`fixtures/custom-pages.mjs`) a custom page's own picture written told its page (`asset <file>=<stamp>`, on which its live script shows it again), a document's picture not.

`tests/unit/updates.mjs` covers the pages someone else wrote (`src/server/updates.mjs`, [render.md](render.md) "Live preview"): the record told once a page's text changed by anyone but the reader, never the reader's own write, the same text again, a page made since the start on its first write (its next write is one), one there at the start not seeded yet, one gone forgotten; then (`tests/unit/fixtures/updated-pages.mjs`, in a workspace of its own) the routes' workbench stream told `updated <key>` of an Agent's write of a document's `doc.html` and of a custom page's `page.html` (`@<slug>`), and not of the same text again, `doc.json` or `page.json` alone, the reader's rename through the edit service or a document's first write; no document's page hears it.

`tests/unit/production-server.mjs` runs the release's own server (`src/server/production.mjs`: node:http, no Vite) from source, in a process and a workspace of its own (`fixtures/production-server.mjs`), on a free port of `GALLERY_TEST_PORTS` (else 4240–4260), with a workbench of two files standing in for the built one: `/` never kept and its `assets/` kept for good, a kit file by its path (`no-cache`) and by its stamp (for good), KaTeX's fonts, a document's page and its pictures; 404 for anything else, a path leaving its folder refused (`..%2f`, `%2e%2e`, an encoded backslash, a backslash, a drive, a NUL: `static.mjs` `fileIn`), another host refused (403); its announcement while it runs; the default home page made as it starts (`GALLERY_NO_DEFAULT_HOME` unset for it: `pages/home` with today and note, `gallery.json` saying so, `/pages/home` served with its first-open note), a page's event stream told of its document's change through the watcher (`src/server/watch.mjs`), and of the catalog of custom blocks as it opens and as a custom block comes and goes, the page made again; POST stop refused without the token, then ending it within 2 s with an event stream open, its announcement and its uploads' folder gone (an upload never imported in it; the process given a temporary folder of its own) and its port kept; and the watcher one folder at a time, as on Linux (the root, `.gallery/`, each document's folder and its `assets/`, `pages/` and the home page's folder, a new document's once it is there, the 写作偏好 page's in `.gallery/pages/`, all of `components/` as it comes and goes, never `.gallery/state/`).

`tests/unit/layout.mjs` covers the layout notes of `gallery check` and `gallery export` (`src/render/layout-notes.mjs`): none for body text standing in the page (a flat document, a preface, an epilogue, a run with its own heading), one for body text between two sections without a heading of its own, in the page and in Sheet panels, naming the first block by its line, its tag and its first words, cut at 30 characters, and one for body text given a width of its own (inline or by a rule for body text; not a widget's own text, not widths that narrow nothing); and the lines the kit holds together (`runtime/spacing.js`): a formula and the marks after it (Latin ones and the end of a range it begins), its drawing wrapping only between its boxes, number ranges as one word (not dates, versions, code or a formula's text), long runs (`src/shared/long-runs.mjs`: more than 20 characters by their pieces, a path between Chinese quotes, a URL, not a word of 20 letters or a run of short pieces) each in a span that breaks anywhere, a piece of inline code holding one held whole but in a cell, none in a code block, edited text, an instance's own region or outside the report, each let go as written.

the outline in full from 1008, the rooms down to the handle's 52px and its strip below, the list never narrower: 1280 with the sidebar open its strip), the room of 批注's cards (the column left by max(0, (1488 − width) / 2) on what the outline leaves, the card giving what is short from 1152 down to 240 at 1084, folded under that and beside a column read full width, kept 16px under the width it needs, the outline's form the same with them; in the head's script too; taken back on the page the list only where it fits, `listFits`, the whole width needed), the outline's switch kept per document (`shared/preferences.mjs` `outlineOpen`: off for every document at first, on for one leaving the others, switched on again the one switched on last, 100 at most, what cannot be read off, the old switch for every document no preference and let go as one is kept, another page's switch heard by its key alone), the head's script taking the room before the page shows when its own document has it on (the width dragged; never with `?solo`), on the page in jsdom the head's form kept as the editor starts, the width dragged (`outlineMost`), a resize heard when the room moves the column, the width moving (a sidebar sliding: each width laid out at once, the list coming at 1008 going up and kept to 992 going down, 200px all along), the tree by levels (a level skipped not indenting twice, a row of columns or a disclosure standing where its highest heading would and closing its own headings when it ends, a tabs area's tabs in their row's order, one with no heading left out), the rows with folds and the row marking the current heading, the tree's keys, the strip's lines (the gap shrinking to 4px, then the deepest levels left out, 240px at the most for 130 headings, a run of top-level rows taken every so many, a line left out marked by the last before it), the current heading (`currentOf`: the one furthest down, the first of those level with each other) and the list following it (`followTop`: at most a third of its clear view down, two rows from its top at least, as little as that takes), the spy's line coming down over the page's last window (`spyLine`), a far jump gliding only its last window (`glideFrom`), what is folded kept per document (the keys no longer on the page dropped, the 100 documents folded last) and carried to a node's new key (`carryFolds`), a formula as a row reads it (`texReading`), and the page read into entries in jsdom (the title, a figure's title, an empty heading, one in a list item and a detail card's left out; not words typed in a paragraph, a paragraph added or a mark).

`tests/unit/heading-folds.mjs` covers the headings' folds' pure rule (`page/fold/rule.mjs`, jsdom on an instrumented page): which headings fold (in a section, a highlight block, a column, a disclosure; not the title, one in a list item or a detail card, nor an empty one), what a fold takes (to the next heading of its level, a section or a row of columns holding one ending it, one holding only deeper headings not, a tabs area holding one ending it, never past its box, a table with its scope line, a script no block of it), what goes with a folded heading (what it hides and folds inside it, in order and once, the section it covers whole with its divider, not one with a paragraph before it), Ctrl+Shift+↑/↓'s steps (past the next section, onto an h1 past its own words, back to the block that ends one, to the box's start), and the keys a fold is kept by (`b:` and `t:…#n`, found again once the heading has an id, under `gallery-edit:heading-folds`, the outline's left alone).

KaTeX, data-math, MathJax and TeX in HTML text), colors onto the page's nine, the Word filter (`import/office.mjs`; [editor.md](editor.md), "Pasting from Word") rule by rule on small bodies of Word's HTML (its source wrapped, with Chinese and Korean; tabs, hidden text, conditional comments, breaks, paragraph styles, styles made tags, colors on and off, review comments, footnotes, self-closing tags, lists from level 2, in a cell, three levels deep and numbered from 5 (starting at 5), markers of each kind with nothing after them numbered or bulleted (WPS's, Word's "follow number with nothing"), Word's list definitions deciding first, a numbered item holding bullets, a paragraph beginning with a number left one, merged cells, spacer runs, never throwing; a pasted Markdown table's cells' pictures; `tabbed`'s quoted cells, and cells only for two lines or more, each of as many cells, two or more), and new tables and formula blocks made on the page and written by the server, the code languages read from the shared table (`src/shared/code-languages.mjs`), one address one href (`links/address.mjs`) and blocks made to fit (600 of them, a space in an address, a tabs area in a disclosure, a row of columns in a column, icons and sizes), and the kit's own flowchart copied from a reader's page (`fixtures/paste/kit-flowchart.html`, Chrome's HTML of a selection across one in an export, a step's own weight 600 in its style) a flowchart of nodes again, each title bold and its description apart, its color; written the short way, its own words its bold title, an emoji its icon (a set's icon left out), its direction and first number; one in a quote its nodes' blocks. `pasteEditorMarkdown` checks IDE Markdown with syntax-colored HTML becoming document blocks, saving and undoing in one step, and remaining literal when pasted into existing code. The browser's `pasteFromPrograms` pastes a web page, Excel's cells, an AI answer and Markdown where the caret is (an empty paragraph, the middle of one, a list item) and checks what is saved. `pasteIntoCell` pastes several colored lines of Google Docs into a table cell, which cannot split, and checks they go in joined with the colors the single line has, and so do several lines of Markdown with no HTML (the list markers gone, the bold kept); `copyCardInContainer` copies a panel holding a table and pastes a second panel with its own table; `copyTableToAnotherReport` copies a styled table (colors, a width, a merged cell, a cell's list) into another document, with Ctrl+C and Ctrl+V between two pages; `copyHandWrittenTable` copies a table an Agent wrote by hand (two colors on a cell, a header of two rows with a merged cell, a width in %) and pastes it on its own page, where it is the table again, and in another document, where it is what that document can carry; `pasteLongPage` pastes a web page of 520 paragraphs as blocks, its heading and list kept; `cutCards` cuts a card and a panel holding one (refused with a notice, both stay on the page) and a table and a paragraph (cut and pasted, none lost); `mediaCutPaste` cuts an image (its side, width, alt words and a caption with bold and a link) and pastes it back as it was, its own file and no other made, two undo steps bringing the file back; copies it with a paragraph into another document (its picture's file copied into that folder byte for byte, by sha-256, named as its own); cuts a file block in one tab, pastes it in another document's tab (its file copied in) and undoes the cut in the first (back there, still in the other); and copies an embed of a file, a bookmark and a highlight block holding an image into the other document, each with its files; then cuts the first of two images with no caption, opens the document again (its units numbered anew) and pastes it (its own picture, not a copy of the other's), and in a document of blocks an Agent wrote that carry no data (an image of a web address, a file block whose size is `2.3 MB`) has their cut refused (both still there) and copies each within its document (its source copied, no link with the page's address); `imageToClipboard` copies an image alone and reads the system clipboard (CDP's clipboard permissions): a PNG of the picture (a JPEG drawn into one of its size) and HTML of it by its whole address carrying the page's own data; pasted back on its page the image of its own file (no PNG uploaded), in another document its file copied byte for byte; copied with a paragraph HTML and Markdown, the picture by its whole address, no PNG; 复制图片 in its block menu a PNG; and another program's picture (a PNG with a web page's HTML) pasted a new image of its own file, as before; `copyFormulaBlock` copies a formula block and an empty one from their handles and pastes each as a second one that is saved; `copyTextInBlock` copies text selected in one block (a list item's words, a heading's, a checked to-do's, two lines of code, a cell's, a paragraph with a formula and a link) and pastes it in a paragraph, a cell and a selected cell: the words with their marks, no "1.", no new code block or table, the formula drawn, and Ctrl+X; a bold word copied with its bold and pasted inside bold as one bold, an item's words pasted inside bold with their own marks (the bold split around them), a triple click in an item holding a paragraph copied by the page (its words), a selection ending at a formula's start copying no formula, and a detail card's entry pasted as its entry; cells copied with the table's tools pasted in a paragraph (one its words, no divider; four a table of them as they were) and the table copied as a block pasted over a cell (its cells with their marks); `pasteKeepsSpaces` copies words with a space before, after and on each side of them (Ctrl+C, Ctrl+V) into a paragraph's middle, its start and end, beside Chinese and inline code and into a cell, and pastes another program's plain text and formatted line with spaces at their ends: the spaces shown on the page and written in the file, an ordinary space where the page shows a non-breaking one, the words typed after one pasted at a paragraph's end a space apart, and a paragraph's words copied whole without its source's indentation; `copyAcross` drags from the middle of a list's first item to the middle of its third, copies, and pastes in a paragraph's middle, an empty paragraph, an item of the list's kind, a cell and a selected cell, then over a checked to-do, a quote and a heading, from a paragraph's middle, and a cut pasted back, each one undo step; then from a paragraph into a Sheet panel (copied as a tabs area of the tab shown, pasted in an empty paragraph; cut, its tabs staying) and across a detail card's items (copied as items, cut). What blocks copied as themselves say where they cannot be made (`joinedHtml`): a table its cells, a card the words of its markup, a chart's data none of them.

`tests/unit/paste-yuque.mjs` covers pasting from Yuque (`page/clipboard/import/yuque.mjs`; [editor.md](editor.md), "Pasting from Yuque") in jsdom with the user's samples (`tests/unit/fixtures/paste/yuque/`, whose README.md and manifest.json say what each file is; the identifying parts of their URLs replaced, the document's host and account path by `yuque.example.com/example.user/book01/sampledoc0000001`, the picture's host and account id by `example.com` and `100000000`, in every encoding, every other byte the capture's, the manifest's sizes and hashes the capture's before that): ⌘A ⌘C's formats read alike (`text/ne-inode`, the `<ne-clipboard>` of its `text/html`, `text/lake`, lake as `text/html`, all of them; a broken `<ne-clipboard>` its HTML), every block of the document of its kind (headings 5 and 6 heading 4, a soft line break, marks and a link, `*` `[ ]` `|` as written, underline, sub, sup and the colors as the page's nearest, a list three levels deep, a numbered list going on around the list its item holds, to-dos and their checks, a quote, a 提示 a callout of its kind's color, code in its language with a literal `#` title, inline code holding backticks, an inline formula and one alone a formula block, a table's `A|B` cell and its alignments, merged cells' spans and a cell's lines, a picture with no caption, a disclosure, the board said in gray; a card of a file a link, one of nothing said, spans that tile no table spread, the callout taken out of the registry its paragraph in place), the toolbar's 复制为 Markdown making the blocks ⌘A ⌘C makes but the board (its marks, `<u>` `<sub>` `<sup>` `<font style>`, `:::info`, `$ … $`, code, a cell's `A|B` and alignments, merged cells empty, its `<img>` and `<details>` as ⌘A ⌘C's) and read so only when it is Yuque's (its marks, its toolbar's span; another Markdown each line a paragraph), and one line copied as rich text. The browser's `yuquePaste` pastes both copies through a real paste event with their MIME types into an empty paragraph and checks the file (each block as Gallery writes it, h5 and h6 h4, the to-dos, the callout, the formulas, a cell two columns wide and one two rows high in the header row 一键优化 makes, the picture downloaded from a server of the test's at the sample's path, the board's gray line), the page (formulas drawn, merged cells, code, a callout) and that one undo step takes each away.

`tests/unit/paste.mjs` also covers an AI answer's cards (`page/clipboard/import/cards.mjs`; [editor.md](editor.md), "Cards of an AI answer") on `tests/unit/fixtures/paste/ai-cards.html`, a ChatGPT copy written after the user's two (their structures, other words): the shape of the blocks it makes (cards highlight blocks with no icon and their marks, rows of small cells columns of 3 + 3 and 2 + 2, a 2 × 2 of cards columns of highlight blocks, a flow of cards between empty divs, scenes beside their pictures' links one under another, list items, tables and headings as before), a few small cases (a card holding a list, a row of cards copied alone or at the clipboard's top, five cells 3 + 2), and that nothing else is read for cards: every sample of the two corpora (Word's through its filter) gives the blocks it gave, and so do styled, classed and `data-` divs, a text editor's line divs, an answer of paragraphs, items holding divs, a section, a caption above a table, paragraphs a page wraps in divs and Office's HTML. The browser's `aiCardsPaste` pastes the sample through a real paste event (with its text/plain) into an empty paragraph and checks the file (the cards as `div.doc-panel` with no icon, the rows as `div.doc-columns`, an item holding divs an item, a scene's picture link a line of its card, nothing of the copy's), the page, that one undo step takes it away, that a card's lines and a column's in it edit and save as any, that a web page's styled divs and bare items holding divs make no card, and that the document exports.

`tests/unit/model.mjs` runs scripted sessions first (`SCENARIOS`: explicit steps on a document of their own, and what the file and the conflicts must say at the end: a list deleted while the Agent adds an item to it, a Sheet tab deleted while the Agent changes its panel, a tab deleted after its own panel was edited) and then edits at random against one page's commits and autosave (in jsdom, `tests/unit/page-harness.mjs`: the page with its commits and autosave, the server's `commitEdits()` keeping answers by commit id as the endpoint does, the network, storage and clock played), the reader typing, splitting, merging, deleting, converting, making lists and sections, dissolving and making sections of blocks, moving, indenting and outdenting items, turning a list into the other kind or into paragraphs and a paragraph into an item, undoing, and putting a block the file refused nearby, with everything around the reader decided by the seed too: the network (an answer lost after the file was written, held while the reader goes on, busy, the server unreachable), an Agent rewording, deleting and adding paragraphs and writing back the file the page opened on (what it read then: the page asks about the texts and blocks that put back), the page closed, crashed or reloading by itself and opened again (sending what it left first), and time. A seed is one interleaving, the same every run (the browser's fuzz has the machine's timing). The live-reload channel announces each write of the file a little later to the page open then. After every step nothing throws, no conflict shows and no write is taken for someone else's without an Agent, every element keeps the identity it was given and no two on the page share one (`data-ge-id`, docs/editor.md "Sync state"), a block made on the page goes as what it is (a commit gives it the type of its element, as built: one sent again went as the page was then; the seed-3035 section that went as a paragraph), the page reloads by itself only when every word of the reader's it shows is in the file, and closed and opened again it keeps every word of the reader's it showed (in the file or listed: `checkLeft()` included), and with no Agent shows exactly what it showed and lists nothing new, a commit whose answer it never learned or not (the page kept what it knew: `recover()` takes it up); at the end, once every answer came, with no Agent the file reads as the page does and nothing is listed, with one every word of the reader's on the page is in the file, held for the reader or in a block not finished yet. The reader's words are markers (〔m1〕…), each typed once. A failing seed is cut down to the steps it needs and printed with its commits and buffers: `node tests/unit/model.mjs [first seed] [how many] [steps]` (kit:check runs the first 30); `node tests/unit/model.mjs --steps "remove(0.201,0.859) · undo(…)"` runs and traces the steps a document printed. `tests/unit/autosave.mjs` covers when commits go and what happens around them (`autosave.mjs` over `commit.mjs` in jsdom against `commitEdits()`, with the network, the storage and the clock played by the test): a commit whose answer is lost after the file took it, or that the server was too busy for, sent again as it went (the words written since go in after it, no conflict with oneself), the crash buffer keeping a commit on its way when the page is put back as it was, leaving sending nothing more while one is on its way, a block the file refuses and a block no commit can carry yet holding off the page's reload and kept in the buffer, the reload's check for texts put back asking everything again after its wait, a block committed by two loads one text to it in the next (where it started, what it was between, the reader's last words, held for the reader when written back as the second load read it; one a later load committed back as the first found it held when written back as the first load read it), what the page committed kept for its next load when it goes away (pagehide) as when it is shown again, the page that opens next asking at once (`opened()`) where it opened on another file than the one the page before last knew (an Agent's write from what it read before, landing as the page was shown again, or while the page was away: the reader's reload, the document opened again in another tab, that tab's departure taken only once no tab showing the document has it: the text put back held, the block made listed with its words, also two loads after the one that made it; it failed before, nothing held) and nothing where it opened on that very file, the reader's own undo sent as the page went taken for no one else's write, and a write the reader asked for (`showOwn`) known by the version it wrote, a buffer that cannot be written said once, and `recover()`: a buffer kept while the server is busy, what the file does not take (and the texts no commit carried) listed under `lostKey` before the buffer goes, a commit never answered sent first and the rest going on from it (what was written since in a block it made listed, not lost), and found again exactly when the page kept what it knew (a block added into a section that commit made, a list put before a paragraph: nothing listed), kept so through the flush of the visibilitychange that follows pagehide, what the commits know packed as JSON and back (`pack()`, `unpack()`), and a buffer of a format it does not know listed instead of sent. `tests/unit/commit.mjs` covers the autosave commits (`page/commit.mjs` in jsdom against `commitEdits()`): units named by id or, before they have one, by their opening number and hash (identical ones by their order), ids given to what a commit changes and the units around it, merges with changes elsewhere and word by word, a conflict held back alone, split-off paragraphs, new sections, new and dissolved sections and conversions taken in as ordinary blocks, fields by their path and, when the structure moved, by their text or as a conflict, text outside any unit, typing and moves while a commit is on its way, deletions guarded by the hash the page saw, ids given while the page is open on a version without them (an export's: a commit built before lands on the same blocks with the same result, each keeping the file's id; twins told apart by their order, and by what comes before them when the Agent added a third; start tags that end in whitespace, `<hr />`, a `>` on a line of its own, deleted and edited after the export as before it), a block the page made that says what a block of the file without an id says (the same words right before it) never taken for the one the page names by its opening hash, the commit carrying the page's ids (`pageIds`), the title changed meanwhile too, undo across commits (a deleted block or section put back from its sealed source, also when undone while the commit deleting it was on its way, a forged source refused, a merge, a conversion, a split undone and redone), a commit sent twice writing once (the ids the page proposes; the second send answered as applied, nothing said to be merged), a block made next to a block without an id yet that the Agent deleted with its section refused rather than put next to one only standing where it stood, and put right after it when the Agent reworded it, a section dissolved while the Agent deleted the block its blocks go after not deleted with them (tried again at the end of the page, then dissolved), a list whose last item is deleted taken out and named `gone` (the page makes it anew, and the item written in it later goes in with it), an empty list still a box, the reader's decisions on conflicts, a block whose place went put at the end of its box, texts someone else's write put back (`revertedTexts()`: as before the first of the page's commits of them, or as one between left them, and once back as at first as what they were between; two naming one block, by its unit's id and where the page opened on it, overlapping when asked of at once and each answered when asked of one by one, blinding it to no other), what the page knows after a commit coming from the file (an item whose only item joined it closed after its text and edited again without a conflict, a paragraph over two lines moved and indented anew then edited, a block deleted while the commit writing it was on its way taken out with the next, a paragraph emptied while one split off it is written becoming a new block, and someone else's move kept when the file was not the page's alone), an emptied block kept, empty, and moved where the page has it (what is typed into it meanwhile written where it stands; a file asked to anyway saying so), items split off a moving item going with it, a list whose items all go kept, empty, when a block is placed next to it, a paragraph converted in a section put back carrying its text alone, a block the file kept though the page took it for gone taken in again from the file's text (and a pasted copy not written again), a file the Agent changed meanwhile (a delete of a block it changed or dropped, a list it added to, deleted item by item so that what it added stays, a Sheet tab whose panel it changed, a conflict, and the page's hash of a tab kept right by what the reader changed in its panel, the same words on both sides, twin text told apart, moves and sizes with its changes in the moved block, a conversion of a paragraph it changed), and the endpoints: a commit sent again under its id getting the answer it had (after a restart too) and refused with other contents, the recent versions kept after each commit too (the three state directories trimmed to the newest 50, 30 and 200), the request checks, no temp file left, the title in `<title>` and `report.json` (and the note when `report.json` cannot take it), an Agent writing right before the file is replaced (applied again, `busy` after three tries), the recent versions kept, the revert check, and the token and opening version outliving a restart; and a document written as natural HTML: its page opening on the kit's text (`read()`), a commit that changes nothing (nothing sent, or refused) leaving the file as written and taking it for no one else's write, the revert check telling the page's version, and the first commit that changes something writing the kit's markup with the typing in it, said once in the dev server's log (`log`).

`tests/unit/media.mjs` covers the media tables of `src/shared/media.mjs` and what stands on them: the one rule that names a file of a document's folder (capital letters and `.ico` for pictures, files, embeds and a page's icon, which takes no `.ico`) and what breaks it, each reason apart (`assetProblem`: a web address, another folder, `./`, `ASSETS/`, `..`, a folder of `assets/`, a name's characters, length or first character with a name the rule takes, a picture's format; none for a name the rule takes and one for every other), the media types, the routes the preview serves them at with each one's headers (`server/media-route.mjs`: a picture as its type, a file only for saving, an embed's HTML in a sandbox), the same picture shown by the preview and an export in every place a picture shows (`render/media-ref.mjs`), what an upload may be by kind (`editor/media-store.mjs`: limit and message, a link read no further than its limit), and the media blocks as the component descriptions declare them (`media`: classes, selector, the empty bars the server serves), and what the four blocks make alike (`components/media/`: a new block's figure, placeholder and marks, the caption node and its words, a file shown and what the file says of it, the empty figure, the one escape). The browser scenarios `mediaCaptions` (Enter and emptying in the caption of each of the four blocks, an existing caption emptied being saved, the editing mark of a caption in neither the file nor an undo step), `mediaCaptionClose` (标题 from an image's toolbar and a file's block menu leaving no block selected; no fill under the caret or the pointer; Backspace or Delete in an empty caption of an image, a file, a bookmark and an embed closing it and selecting the block, the file unchanged; a caption emptied by the reader closed by the next key; a Backspace held down stopping at the emptied caption; Backspace again deleting the selected image and Ctrl+Z bringing it back; Backspace at the start of a caption with words leaving it as it is) and `mediaFailures` (a link that cannot be downloaded: the placeholder as it was) run beside `imageBlock`, `imageGrips` (the pointer on a picture showing nothing; a click selecting the image, a gray L at each of its four corners and its toolbar at its top right as far from the top as from the right edge (16px) and clear of the grips, each grip one plain gray L with no edge line round it, the toolbar above a picture too narrow to hold it; the Ls read on a black picture in light mode and a white one in dark mode, measured from the screen's pixels: a pixel of each L reads 4.5 to 1 on the picture and 2.5 to 1 on the page at once, the L having no edge round it; a corner dragged in, the centered picture narrower by twice as much, its shape kept, its share saved, the grips following it live, one undo step; Esc during a drag putting it back; the ··· menu with 同步宽度到全局 first and none of the toolbar's rows; 同步宽度到全局 giving the page's other two pictures this one's width, written, said, and one undo step bringing every width back with the image selected again; GALLERY_TEST_SHOTS keeps the selected image in light and dark and a narrow one), `imageToolbarClicks` (each of the selected image's toolbar buttons pressed by the mouse as a person does, held down a moment so frames pass before it is let go: the button on top at its middle, 展开 opening the viewer, 标题 the caret in the caption, 对齐方式 its popover and 左对齐 in it, held the same way, writing the side, 更多 the block menu; the buttons' icons put in anew every frame lost every such click, 2026-11-09), `fileBlock`, `bookmarkBlock`, `embedBlock`, `mediaRhythm` and `imageTall` (a tall picture at 80% of the window's height, its width following, centered with its caption; the reader's span as tall as its width makes it; an Agent's `width="356"` opening as half the reading width and written as the figure's span by the first edit); `imageBlock` also pastes a phone's screenshot on a screen of twice the density, which takes no span and stands at most 80% of the window tall; `emptyBlocksKept` also puts the handle of each empty media block at its top.

`tests/unit/written-files.mjs` covers a file of a document's folder written again under its name ([editor.md](editor.md) "A file of the folder written again"): the stamp (`render/media-ref.mjs` `fileStamp`: the same file the same stamp, a new one written in place and for a copy of the same size and modification time over it; `servedUrl` none for a file not there; `servedStamps` the stamps a page as rendered names its files by, every route's, none of another document's, of no stamp, of a longer one or of a name the preview serves none by), an export embedding the bytes the file has now, the route's `ETag` and its 304 for a request holding it (one of several tags too), the new bytes once the file changed whatever the request holds; a picture whole by its own format (`server/asset-news.mjs` `wholePicture`: a PNG, a JPEG, under a `.png` name too, a GIF, a WebP and an SVG whole and cut short, an empty file, a format that says no end); the news (`createAssetNews`): a picture written in three pieces told once, whole and quiet, a stamp told again not told, one cut short and a motion photo (a JPEG with data after its end of image) told as they are once quiet for their patience and not before, what was told kept for a page that connects (`told`), another document's apart, one taken away not told and forgotten; and the page (`editor/page/assets-live.mjs` `swapFiles` in jsdom): every address of the file swapped (an image block's, an editor's of no stamp, a srcset's candidate, a link under another workspace's base, a frame's with its origin and fragment, an SVG image's `xlink:href`, a style's and a `<style>`'s `url()`), the new picture loaded once before any address takes it, what the file says (`data-ge-src`) kept, another file's, another document's, a longer name's and an address with a query of its own left; told the stamp it shows, nothing loaded or swapped; a picture that does not load kept while a link takes its address; an address changed meanwhile left to what changed it; `followFiles` swapping what the live script heard of before it ran and each `doc-files-written` after, counted on the root, and by itself what comes back from before a write (a pull's unit, a cell's HTML put back as undo puts it, a block taken off the page and put back, an attribute set to an old address), never one of a file not heard of or of another document; one told again while the first new picture still loads ending at the last stamp (one pass at a time); `stampedUrl` naming the stamp heard last; `core.mjs` `mentionOf` taking a pasted chip's served icon with its stamp or none. The scenario `agentReplacesPicture` does it in the workbench: a picture written alone, in three pieces with pauses longer than the quiet (the picture shown kept until it is whole), with a write of the document's words and with one of its own caption, each shown in the open page (about half a second after the write alone) with the same page all along, nothing loaded beside it, the picture never blank or broken in any frame, the other picture never fetched again, nothing committed and the file holding no address of the page's; after a reload the new picture, the other one answered 304. The scenario `agentPictureComesBack` does what comes back into the page from before a write: a letter typed in a cell holding a picture, the picture written again (the cell swaps it), Ctrl+Z (the cell's HTML put back shows the new picture, and a moment later too); a picture block deleted, its file written again while it is off the page, Ctrl+Z (the block back with the new picture); the table tools' copy, the cell's picture by its stamped address, pasted back as another program's table (its picture the folder's own file, no copy downloaded into the folder); a motion photo written over a photo, shown once the server's patience is over. `pagePictureWrittenAgain`: a custom page's own picture written again under its name, the page shown again with the new one, its file untouched.

`tests/unit/own-icons.mjs` holds the editor's icons (docs/editor.md, "Icons"): every one of them (分栏, bookmark, file, embed, the media toolbar, tabs, the table grip's dots, the icon picker's, the face and the picture) is a plain 20-unit svg with no inline style, the placeholder bars' CSS has no `!important`, no editor code writes a bar's class (`.ge-image-empty` and the like) out by hand, the kit's file row masks are the editor's two file drawings, the code gutter and its open code read the kit's `--doc-code-number-*` and `--doc-code-line-height` properties, each with a fallback, and no path written under `src/` matches a fingerprint in `tests/unit/fixtures/icons/third-party-icons.json` (a hash of the whole path, or two shared long segments; `fingerprints.mjs` there makes them).

`tests/unit/checks.mjs` covers the checks that read a document's real markup (`render/pipeline.mjs`, `typecheck.mjs` `codeUses`, `asset-references.mjs`): the network and storage checks over its code alone (scripts of any JavaScript type, on* attributes, javascript: addresses with a tab in them; never a code sample, prose, a comment, a string or a chart's JSON; the global reached by an element of `window`, passed on, called through `.call` or taken out of `window`, but not asked for with `typeof`, nor a name the code binds itself; `navigator.sendBeacon`, `EventSource` and `import()` too), repeated block ids counted on its elements with their lines (and columns, two on one line), and `add-block-ids.mjs` refusing such a document without writing anything (run with `GALLERY_WORKSPACE` on a copy), a TeX formula between single dollars failing at its line (`component-checks.mjs`; a subscript after a letter of its own, a bracket or a brace too; never money, a variable's name, `$JAVA_HOME`, `$_GET`, `${user_name}`, `$my_var$`, `$$…$$`, an escaped dollar, code, a formula of the kit's or an element with `data-doc-math-dollar`), `main[data-doc-id]` missing or not the slug, the size limit's message, the export's policy after the page's real `<head …>` (a byte order mark before it too; none: an error), the page's tags and the references to files of its folder read from its elements (there, the media blocks' however their tags are written, a `>` in a quoted value and character references too; nothing a comment, a `<textarea>`, a script's string or a diff's JSON spells; a picture in a cell, a link, a CSS background or a srcset passing, as an export holds them; an `<object>`'s data, `@import` and a PDF in a frame of its own failing, as it cannot), and the workbench's 导出 checked in every format (the Component Catalog's offline file and its check, a Markdown export held to the same check, a failing page answered 422 with why, a natural picture in a page nobody opened made the kit's before the check, one whose report.json still says `"template": true` too, a page the hand-in leaves as it is failing: repeated ids 422 as the check says them, a file that kept changing 409); `workbenchTransfer` (tests/browser/) shows a subpage failing it: no file, and the 导出失败 dialog. And the notes `gallery check` and `gallery export` print besides (`notesOf`), neither a failure, both among the notes to act on first (`notesByKind`'s `fix`, which `reportNotes` prints first, of every document, then the rest as `note`): each path of this machine in the text a reader reads, at its line, with what to write instead (`localPathNotes`: a drive's, after a Chinese character too, a Unix home's or system folder's, `~/` and `$HOME/`, a share's, `%TEMP%`, a path through AppData, a scratchpad or a temporary folder in a path from a drive or the root, two a semicolon parts, the `<title>`'s, one ending at a Chinese character after its extension; none in code, an attribute, a comment, a script, a `<template>`, hidden text, TeX (single dollars too where the page asks for them, and the kit's formulas), a web address whatever its folders' names, a choice `A:/B`, a project's relative `build/tmp/` or prose's "scratchpad"; twelve one by one, the rest by their lines), and each picture an export embeds as it is over 1 MB or longer than 2400px (`largePictureNotes`; none for the picture of an `<img>` the export makes a copy of, a JPEG at a 全宽 document's ceiling heavy again, a CSS background's weighed, `pictureSize` on a PNG, a JPEG with segments before its frame, a GIF, the three WebPs and an AVIF, none for an SVG), with its lines, size and pixels, what the export holds of it each time and what a copy would weigh (a JPEG's 2000px copy, a PNG's or a lossless WebP's as it is, a PNG's as a JPEG; none that is not a quarter lighter); none for a small picture, one a code sample spells or a srcset's smaller candidate; a heavy picture named so that it fails (Chinese and a space, a folder of `assets/`) weighed all the same, beside its failure saying why; and a picture shown more than once whatever its size (two image blocks and a picture of one file, a CSS background and a picture), but no link's icon beside each link to its site.

`tests/unit/references.mjs` covers the references to files of the document's folder outside the media blocks (`render/asset-references.mjs`): a document with every kind of them (a picture in a table cell, a figure of two, a picture in a link, a whole block's picture and background, links to a file, `url()` and `image-set()` in a `<style>` and a style attribute, a CSS escape, a font, a `srcset` and a `<source>`'s, a poster, an SVG's image, a frame's page, a table cell's `background`, the tab's icon) passing what an export needs, its export holding each as a data: URL of its file's bytes (a srcset its largest candidate, a link the name to save it by), no `assets/` left (and every one left with `files` false), the preview's address of each (its route by what the page does with it, what the file has in `data-ge-<attribute>`, once where the file writes one too); a CSS string or comment holding none, an `@import` after a long comment found; what fails, each with its line (an `<object>` or `<embed>`, a script's `src`, a stylesheet linked or imported, a frame of a PDF or a picture, a file not there or leaving the folder, a name the preview cannot serve and why, a link that would open an HTML file, an icon's picture written as an icon is not read) and stays as written; repeats outside the media blocks over the budget, and four image blocks of one picture passing; the Markdown export (a cell's picture in its table, a figure's pictures, a list item's, no link's icon, an alt's brackets escaped, links to files, the files beside the page, each once in the zip); and the Component Catalog and the sample rendered as their media blocks alone make them, byte for byte.

`tests/unit/images.mjs`, `files.mjs`, `bookmarks.mjs` and `embeds.mjs` each cover one media block: its markup and the checks of it (`render/images.mjs`, `files.mjs`, `embeds.mjs`), the picture, file or frame where the preview serves it and inside an export, the block as the editor finds it (a card with a caption that may be empty) and writes a new one, and what the document's folder keeps of it (`editor/media-store.mjs`); `bookmarks.mjs` also what a web page says of itself (`editor/unfurl.mjs`: title, description, icon, cover, through the proxy) and `embeds.mjs` whether a page lets frames show it.

`tests/unit/icons.mjs` covers the page's icon: its three kinds of value and their markup, the 图标 tab's icons (`icons.json`: 443 pairs and Tabler's 40 numbers, every name once in its set, by category with the numbers last in 数字与符号, each category titled through `t()` with its English; a number drawn and valued as Tabler's whichever set is shown; search by Chinese name, set name, search word, earlier name and digit, no English filter inside a word), the pictures the preview, a build and an export show them as (`render/emoji.mjs`), the images the workbench gets by value, the emoji data (`src/shared/emoji/data/`: the files byte for byte what `src/cli/import-emoji-data.mjs` makes of the emojibase-data package in the npm cache, a note and no comparison where the package is not there;
