no flag in any file or in the pack; an emoji one whether its variation selectors are written or not, ☺ and ☺️, ❤ and ❤️; `positions.json` gone, the two licence files there with their grant; 推荐's 24 emojis each offered, as the data writes it), the data as the editor reads it (an emoji found however it is written, a name in the UI's language, a skin tone chosen taken by two people and two hands too, 随机's pool) and the picker's search (`emoji-search.mjs` `rank`: 笑 猫 火 心 heart cat fire smile best first, a short code between colons, a name in any case, an emoticon, the emoji itself typed, no flag found; among equal ones an English name holding the word first, the one it ends before the others, then the shorter: thumbs 👍, party 🎉, moon the moons before 🥮, money 💸 💰, no 🙅; a short code still before it, and a small word such as a or on finds no name; between equal ones Gallery's everyday emojis, `shared/emoji/everyday.mjs`: one of them before one that is not, below the closeness, so 爱 idea yes 好 time home 家 雪 music 红 find ❤️ 💡 👍 ⏰ 🏠 ❄️ 🎵 🔴 first, happy wow win 😀 🤩 🏆, sad 😢, green 🟢 (the status dot), while mail finds 📧 and cold 🥶 by their names and panda 🐼; the list a few hundred, each offered as the data writes it, once; no filter ranks every emoji alike), an emoji the pack lacks (a flag) left as its character on the page and drawn in the system's emoji font by the icon route, the browser's emoji modules reading no user agent (Twemoji on every system), the check of a document's icon (each failure at its line, two page icons' too; an icon in single quotes said so, with the icon as the file writes it; one holding no icon saying what it holds and the three kinds, an example of each, the examples icons; a name its set has not saying the other set's, or names near it), the images kept in the document's folder and the kit's ten icon colors held to `shared/emoji/icon-value.mjs`.

`tests/unit/read.mjs` covers `gallery read` ([internals.md](internals.md), "Reading a document"; `src/cli/read.mjs`, `src/transfer/read-view.mjs`, each kind's `read`): every kind of unit the editor knows has its `read` (a name and its rows; the fields it may have functions), and every row of "Blocks at a glance" a mark in the view of `skills/gallery/references/example-report.html`, which shows each block once (a kind or a row added later fails here until it has one); the default view of that report as an Agent writes it (natural HTML) and as an export leaves it (an id for every block), ending saying it is whole, with every `data-block-id` of the file in its left column, every word of the file's `<main>` in it, and, read in the test's own process, every unit a row of its own (`unwritten`) and none written as HTML for want of a way; each format (the export's Markdown ending with what it left out, the words alone, `html` the file's `<main>` and one block's span byte for byte, the JSON's keys, ids, a chart's data, a paragraph's line, text, Markdown and HTML); the outline and the map; one block by `--block` and by an address naming it, a section by a heading's id, by its words and by a heading inside it; a custom block instance (its block, data, own HTML with its slot cut out, its slot's blocks), merged cells (⟵, ↑) with a cell's look and data, what Markdown has no mark for in the file's terms; two headings of the same words refused (exit 1); another workspace's document by its `~ws-…` address and by its path; a page whose edits are not saved (a server of the test's own announcing itself: the 提示 line, `unsaved` in the JSON); a document or block not there (1) and the command line's mistakes (2); and, after all of it, the two workspaces and the machine's registry byte for byte as they were.

`tests/unit/status-notes.mjs` covers `gallery status` and `gallery notes` (docs/workbench.md "Status", "Annotations"; `src/shared/presence.mjs`, `src/workbench/presence.mjs`, `src/cli/status.mjs`, `src/cli/notes.mjs`): a tab's report (its shape; 200 characters of selected words, 20 blocks, ids and counts cleaned; the save state as one word), the server's memory of the tabs (the one used last first, a heartbeat that is not use, a closed tab and one that stopped reporting gone), the lines the command prints (a `问题：` line for a document not saved), `markItems` / `withoutItems` / `unknownIds`, the edit service's annotations (done and undone, removal by ids / done / all, an unknown id refusing the whole request, a removal by a command offered back and put back, a rewritten suggestion no longer done, 复制批注 leaving done ones out, a store that never checks), and, against a real server started by `gallery open`, the routes' checks (Host, Origin, the token) and the commands with the server and without (the scenarios `statusOpen` and `annotateDone` are their browser side). `tests/unit/annotations.mjs` covers 批注 (`src/shared/annotations.mjs`, `src/editor/annotations.mjs`, `src/editor/dropped-annotations.mjs`; [editor.md](editor.md), "Annotations"): what a check deleted told once (never a removal, a clear or a restore), kept, and said once to the Agent with each one's id, text and quote (one back in the file since left out; the log's line without a time); a quote found again as it is (and only as it is, the page's first look), across a block and the next after Enter in its middle (begun in its own, not the same words in a container after it), by its context, and with half of its characters kept in order where it stood (words typed into it, a word changed at either end, too few kept, under ten characters, too far apart; deleted and not moved onto a parallel sentence or a phrase sharing its words, gone when what follows it was rewritten too), the check deleting what lost its words or blocks and rewriting a quote edited in part, page order, an id not given yet, words found once elsewhere in the innermost block, the Markdown of 复制批注 (and its words in another language; one said as one, 页首 before the first heading, a suggestion's blank lines), an annotation with no words refused (`put`), left out of a `restore`, read as none from a file, the anchors the page tells before a commit (`sameWords`, `cleanAnchor`, the `anchors` request: written as told, then found by the check after the commit), where the cards stand beside the page (`cardTops`: level ones by when they were made, a gap stopping those that go up, the page's top), clear of the button beside a block, the markers' stacking (the page's top and end; `stackItems` still steps over obstacles and keeps a card's line, as asked), the one place of a marker and of the button beside a block (`markerLeft`, `markerTop`: 5px right of a block anchor's bar, the window's edge, several blocks' middle), what stands beside another (`standsBeside`) and the markers' room (inside the column's 64px gutter, clear of the outline's guide and its grip at the workbench's widths: they ask nothing of `editor/page/margin.mjs`), what stands beside another (`standsBeside`: a row of columns, two nodes on a line, not a node alone on its line), a document's units (a formula in its delimiters) and the place each names (a tab, the headings above it, a disclosure), and the file on the server (put, remove, clear, restore never over a newer text, the check after a write, no file when none is left; a new annotation whose blocks or words the file does not hold yet standing as it was sent through saves and writes, judged as any other once it does, and going when its commit never comes). The browser scenarios `annotateButton` (the top bar's button opening its list, its tooltip 批注 with the shortcut, the line bubble with no plus, a selection alone offering nothing, the format bar as ever, Ctrl+B as ever, its 批注 starting one, typing saves, Enter to a marker in the gutter with the caret back, hover showing the card by its words with them lit, a click on the marker editing it, emptied deletes (撤销 bringing it back), nothing pressed for the marker and the mark to be there), `annotateAnchors` (a quote following an edit, deleted with its words, back with an undo, an Agent's rewrite), `annotateIds` (a document without ids: the block named by a commit, nothing else changed), `annotateLayout` (at `NO_ROOM`'s 1200 with the sidebar and at 1024, where the cards have no room: the column never moving as they come, the markers 10px out in the column's gutter, three on one line stacked down at one x, a card by its words inside the column), `annotateCopy` (the Markdown on the clipboard with 减少通知 on, 全部删除, the button kept through a switch of documents, and 撤销 after it putting them back in the document they came from), `annotateEdges` (a closed disclosure's marker on its title's line, a card near the page's end inside it, a card shown by hover with words selected, the format bar's 批注 taking a selection widened with Shift+→, Ctrl+A's steps up to the page, nothing in a detail card's dialog, where the format bar has no 批注), `annotateAgent` (an Agent's write while a card is typed in: no key lost, the page shown again once it closes; an input method's letters not saved), `annotateOffline` (what could not be saved going with the next page that reads them, never over a newer text), `annotateHover` (resting on one of three markers on a line: its card by its words, steadily, nothing moving under the pointer; the marker beside it switching at once; the card kept while the pointer goes slowly from the marker over to it; the same with words selected on the line; a card being written keeping every key while another marker is clicked and a third passed over), `annotateFollow` (a character typed into a quote under ten characters and Enter in the middle of one: kept, the file's anchor told before the commit and kept by the check, undo back as it was; an Agent changing the short quote deletes it), `annotateKeys` (the right column's marker first on a line of two columns, Ctrl+Alt+M opening a card and closing it, the card of blocks at the last one's bottom right, at the window's bottom right (above the notices there) where that bottom is below the window, the blocks' selection and its status line gone once it opens), `annotateCard` (the card where the format bar stood, following its words as the page scrolls; left with nothing typed, or typed and emptied, nothing left (the saved one offered back); Esc's block with its card at its bottom right), `annotateWriting` (a card being written keeping the place it opened at: by a long figure at the window's bottom growing upward there, by words held in the window when the page is scrolled and when it grows at the window's bottom, typing scrolling nothing; a card of blocks at the window's bottom above the notices at 1024; whitespace alone keeping the format bar, without its 批注), `annotateOutline` (the two together, 目录 on and 批注 coming to a page with none, at 1380, 1280 and 1024 with the sidebar, and 1120 without it, where the list in full leaves the cards no room (a page of 1170, 1154 once they stand): the column in the same place in every frame as they come, the outline as it was (the list in full, or, at 1024 with the sidebar, its strip, whose card opens under the pointer), the markers clear of it, 10px out; a page shown again for an Agent's write has the outline and the column from before it shows), `annotateFormatBar` (批注 the last row of 更多, with its key, resting on 更多 or a click opening the list; the type row all of its row in its own look, opening the types; a press keeping the selection until the card has it; a cell with nothing below the buttons and 批注 in the list; a detail card's dialog with the type row and no such row; text across two blocks annotated, 链接 off and no 详情卡片, its strips gone), `annotateBeside` (the handle left of a block's middle line, the button right of it, 24×24, radius 6, 10px right of the column; no frame with neither or both across the line and the gap; a pointer trembling on the line changing nothing; the grip's fill and its tooltip; the wheel and typing hiding it, a menu and a drag showing none; a press making the block's card at its bottom right; a block in a column, a flowchart's nodes in a row keeping the handle, those one below another with the button; the title and a detail card's block without it; a wide block, a narrow window; a section's strip 6px out and 16px wide, found 28px out, the button past it (24px out), and found above and below it; a short table's corner + clear of the button and of the marker it becomes), `annotateBesideStrip` (right of a section's strip the button 24px out and the marker it becomes at its pixel, one on words in the section too and one of blocks that run into it from before it, a block outside a section 10px out; in every frame on the way across the strip; the strip pressable at the button's height and the marker's, 2px clear of the button, its menu; a section in a framed Sheet panel leaving the 10px; a narrow window; the outline in full at its tightest, clear of its guide and grip and of the strip), `annotateOnePlace` (the button and the marker it becomes 24×24 at the same pixel on a paragraph, a list's item, a heading, a section's paragraph, a code block, a table, a closed disclosure, a picture at its top and not by its caption, and in a narrow window), `annotateTaken` (no button on a block with an annotation, by its words and whole, the handle at its right; its words still annotated from the format bar; the annotation gone, the button back), `annotateCovered` (no button on a block whose button's place a marker stands on: one stacked down from the block above, a closed disclosure's for a block in it, a whole section's on its heading; the handle across its width, the shortcut still annotating it), `annotateUnnamed` (blocks with no id yet and the commits held: a second annotation leaving the first in the file and on the page, a divider's too, two blocks selected with one named and the other not staying two, the file naming all of them once the commit lands), `annotateCaretBack` (a card the pointer opened on a block taller than the window, by the button or the marker, closing with no caret in the block and the page where it was; the keys' giving the caret back), `annotateSeveral` (the button beside any selected block without an annotation, none beside the annotated one, the press keeping the selection until the card has it, one annotation of all of them in the file; its row order, words and ellipsis; a click opening it and a click on it closing it, resting on the button opening nothing, the button pressed only while it is open; no row lit on opening, by a click and by the key under a resting pointer, and none once the pointer has left it; a press in the page and in the workbench closing it; locating into a closed disclosure in a tab not shown, the focus left alone; the card shown so going when its words or blocks leave the window; the list following a new annotation and the count; its empty state), `annotateNoMenuRow` (no row in the block menu, for a block and for two selected, no line left for it; the key on the two selected one annotation on both, the button beside a block its card), `annotateShortcut` (the words selected, the blocks selected, the marked words' card, the words of an annotation there, the caret's block, the title leaving the key to the workbench, which opens and closes the list, a caret in a block with an annotation off its words, and words in a code block, which has no format bar), `annotateFills` (the fills of an annotation of a nested list's item, three items of a list, a disclosure, a table, a flowchart's node and its nodes by a box, a quote and a highlight block the same, to the pixel, as those of selecting them, a list's marker inside the fill, its own colour, gone with the card;
