its fill and tooltip; a press moving nothing, a click deleting at once, the caret back by its words, 撤销 in the toast putting it back; from a card shown by hover, the focus left alone; typed and not saved yet, nothing left) and `annotateNone` (a page with none: editing with the read held back, then no layer, no marks and no observer, its list opened or not; a card opened and left empty taking its layer away again) drive it in the workbench, but for `annotateBeside`, `annotateOnePlace`, `annotateCovered`, `annotateUnnamed`, `annotateCaretBack`, `annotateSeveral`, `annotateNoMenuRow` and `annotateFills` on the page itself; `annotateButton` also reloads with one and checks the column stands where it does with none from before the page shows.

`tests/unit/changes.mjs` covers the Agent's changes (`src/editor/source/changes.mjs`, `src/editor/changes.mjs`, `src/editor/page/changes/words.mjs`; [editor.md](editor.md), "Agent's changes"): what `diffRows` counts as added, changed and deleted (a list and a section by what they hold, a container put around blocks that stay as one, a block moved deleted and added, ids, indentation and natural HTML's writing no change, twins told apart by their order, a picture changed whole, a list item's own words), the starter's first write no review (`isNewDocument`), the words the page marks (`wordMarks`), the reader's commit applied to the baseline (`rebaseOnto`: a block the Agent did not touch takes it, words beside the Agent's merge, a block the Agent added does not), and the edit service's review through `POST /__gallery/edit/changes` with real commits built by the page's side in jsdom: started by a write not its own, the reader's edits and new blocks counting for nothing, a write the announcement had not told yet the Agent's all the same, 确认 refused with a stamp grown past, 撤回 keeping the reader's saved edits and doc.json's title in step, 撤销 and its refusal once the file moved on, kept over a restart and a write made while no server ran, forgotten (`forgetChanges`, its folder deleted), the 写作偏好 page never reviewed and nothing of it in the file; and a new document's starter filled (the first writing gone since 2026-11-13): no review, nothing kept but `seen` and no `draft` in `read`'s answer, the write right after it starting a review against what it left (+1) and the next growing it, a `state.json` an older Gallery wrote with `draft` read without it (the next write reviewed, the field gone), the starter taken on disk by `noteNew` in a process of its own so that a server whose first look is the Agent's first write takes it against the starter, a document not new at the Agent's first write reviewed from it, and in memory the same. `tests/unit/cli.mjs` has `gallery open` ask the announced server's edit service nothing, and the Agent's write after a new document's first reviewed.

`tests/unit/history.mjs` covers the recent versions and the Agent's changes taken back (`src/editor/history.mjs`, the edit service's `POST /__gallery/edit/history`, `src/cli/history.mjs`, `src/cli/revert.mjs`; [editor.md](editor.md), "Recent versions" and "Agent's changes"): the store (who made each version beside it, the first one not known, the same text twice in a row kept once with the service's own word on who wins, later than the last whatever the clock, thinned to the last 30 and the last of each hour before them with their `.json`, a version named by its id, its version or the start of either and not by 4 characters, an older Gallery's named to the second and not known, a file not of its version left out); the edit service (a page served the first version, a reader's commit the reader's with the block it changed by id, words and kind, an Agent's write announced a version of its own and under review, the revert of `gallery revert` saying its blocks and the version kept, the Agent's restore under review and restored back by the version kept before it, the same text answered `same`, the reader's restore ending the review and its 撤销 putting file and review back once, a version's words shown, nothing in the document's folder); and the commands on a real server (ports from `GALLERY_TEST_PORTS`) and with none: `gallery revert` with nothing to take back (exit 1, its 问题 and 下一步 lines), of a new document's starter filled (nothing under review: exit 1, nothing written), of the Agent's write right after it, which the server reviewed as it took it with no command between (the open page's event stream told), `gallery history`'s list and `--json` (the user's commit through the server, the Agent's revert and write, 未知 for the first), `--restore` and back by the version it kept (under review as the Agent's), a wrong version (exit 1) and option (exit 2), a write while no server ran, under review from the next server's start and taken back, and a document made again under its slug by `gallery new` (its folder deleted by hand), whose versions are its own, its starter filled nothing to take back and its first version, its own starter, brought back by `--restore`; a version's page (历史版本's view): its address in the list, its key opening that version alone (`versionShown`, `readVersionIn` refusing a path), the preview serving it presented and frozen (no edit map, no live script, its script before the kit's, framed by Gallery alone, never kept), another key, a name that is no version's and a request another site started refused.

`tests/unit/workspace-facts.mjs` covers the workspace's facts and its record of edits ([workbench.md](workbench.md) "The workspace's facts"): a document's to-dos as its pages show them (their words, a nested list's and a held block's apart; the headings above each, the title none; which of the same words under the same path; block ids; done; none in a template, a script or a plain list), each query's shape and order and its options' bounds (a kind it does not know 400, `table` 501; `document`, one document's facts, when it was made its doc.json's `created`, else the earliest its files tell, who made its last change of text (`editedBy`, as the record counted it, kept with it and read back), and in the export's snapshot), facts read again by a file's time alone and kept otherwise, never of a file held half written (through `readOf`, the edit service's `readTaken`, as the server reads them: while an Agent is between two edits of one change, the to-dos its page is served, read again once it is taken though its time is the same), the kinds a change changes (a to-do added every one; the same text written again no edit; a document gone its lists; weighed against what the pages were last told, so that a query, the menu's count or the search's lines between a write and its announcement hide none of them, and the same text told again nothing); the record counting the reader's and the Agent's apart, a net change of words below 0, what was written while no server ran counted on its file's day as a start catches up (a document never seen with all its words, nothing again after), `days()` zero-filled from the day it started, a record that does not read starting again, the days past 53 weeks gone as it is written, every day a query may ask kept (`QUERY_LIMITS.activity.most` is `KEEP_DAYS`); and a document's page's live script, run in jsdom with an EventSource of the test's, ignoring every message it does not know (`workspace`, `page`, nonsense: no reload), handing `data <kind>` to the kit and telling each kind again once its stream comes back.

`tests/unit/workspace.mjs` covers the workbench's document commands (`/__gallery/workspace/*`, [workbench.md](workbench.md)): a workspace's custom blocks no document, found by no search and taken for one by no command, and a document called `components` listed beside them, its copy and the trash taking what is its own and leaving a custom block being written, back from the trash where it stood, or under the next free name when something took a place of its there, and made anew under that name; the same request checks as a commit, the outline kept apart from document content (in `.gallery/outline.json`), `POST move` (a document with those under it under a parent, after one of its level, first or last; there already writing nothing; refused under itself or one under it, after itself, at another level, for what the outline holds not or another workspace's command; each outline written told, `changed`, and the outline's lock let go), documents moved to the trash and back (a new document and each copy in a folder `doc-<page id>` of its own, the eight the address ends with, a copy's not the original's name and `-copy`; one back from the trash under a name taken meanwhile: a `doc-<id>` name under a new `doc-<id>`, any other the next free one), the 写作偏好 page made in `.gallery/pages/`, and the custom pages (`GET outline`'s `pages`, home first, apart from the documents; `rename` and `trash` with `kind: "page"`: `page.json`'s title alone, the folder whole as an entry of kind `page`, never the home page; back under the next free slug, `data-page-id` with it, when a document took its own; its slug taken by no new document while in the trash). `tests/unit/pages.mjs` covers the custom pages (`src/shared/pages.mjs`): `pages/<slug>/` with `page.html`, `page.json` and its own files' `assets/`, and the 写作偏好 page's folder apart (`preferencesPage`), a scratch copy of `gallery page try`'s or check's found by its slug where `pages/` has none of it (and listed nowhere; a document's copy no page), a new page's `main.gallery-page`, one board and no script, its title escaped, one set of slugs with the documents (a document's, a kept name, `assets` and `materials`, one of the trash refused), made once, the list home first then `gallery.json`'s `pages.order` then by title, a folder with no `page.html` passed over, a rename of `page.json`'s title alone (a page with none given one), a page's version, `gallery.json`'s `pages.homeMade` written beside what else it holds and once (never into one that does not read or is another program's), into the file's own text (CRLF, four spaces, a list on one line and a BOM kept, `pages` added last as the file indents; tabs, `homeMade` after `pages.order` on its own line; a file on one line, spaced or compact; `homeMade: false` and a `pages` that is no object or empty replaced in their place; a string holding quotes and a brace read past; Gallery's own file as `JSON.stringify` lays it out), the default home page's title and cards, its first-open note closed in `.gallery/hints.json` beside the file's other notes, and a trash entry written before kinds came read as a document's; and the cards of a `page.html` as the server reads and writes them (`src/shared/page-cards.mjs`): each board's cards, the ids a page goes by (its own, else the lowest `c<n>`, a repeated or ill-formed one given another, the same however often read), a card's base (its instance changed: another; the page's head: the same), its cells written (the value that changed alone), a card taken out and put back byte for byte, a new card's markup on an empty board, a card's markup alone, and the page as it is served (ids, bases, fit, bare, a block of documents held in a template). `tests/unit/board-places.mjs` covers where a board's cards go (`src/shared/board-places.mjs`): cards that share a cell, held to the board, settled (a board that shares no cell left as it is, one drawn higher pushing those under it down, one shrunk pulling none up), pushed down under a card moved (one it lands on going under it though it stood above), added or resized, where a dragged card lands (`landing`: along the README take's drag of the home page's note past 最近编辑 no step pushing it, the free places around the note taken on the way and where it was going kept where none is, the rule before pushing it at the fourth step; a card covering more than half of another, or a small one inside a big one, put there and the other giving way; half and a quarter only clips; a clip beside a free place taking it; two as near, the top one; held to the row; the narrow view's 8 columns alike), the first free spot, a block's default size, its listed sizes and its min and max, and the cells and pixels of the grid. `tests/unit/page-service.mjs` covers the page service (`src/editor/page-service.mjs`) over a server of its own with the edit service's checks and queue: another token, origin or a body of no JSON refused, a command of another workspace, a page not there; the cards served with the ids its first write gives them and their bases; `place` pushing down and writing those ids, every other byte as it was and the file kept; a card someone else changed refused (`changed`), one gone (`gone`), another card's change or the head's no matter, the answer's `before` saying the page was written meanwhile; a resize to the block's nearest size, none for a block that does not resize; `arrange` putting pushed cards back, held to each base; `add` at the first free spot by the block's default size or where told, refused for a block of documents, one turned off, one not there, and Gallery's own (from a folder of the test's, `tests/fixtures/page-blocks/shipped/`) put in first and the catalog taken; `remove` and the `add` of its markup putting the file back byte for byte, a card with a handler refused; `edit` of a data part (one, or several by `parts`), what would end its script or no such part refused; `settings` held to each field's type and written as `jsonIn` lays it out, a block with none refused; a block that says no height added 4 × 2 with `measure` and placed at a measured height though it does not resize, never at another width; two ops at once one after the other; the last 20 versions kept; a part of HTML holding a picture of the page's own (a block of the test's, put on by an add that says `agent: true`) sent as served, written as the folder's and answered as the page shows it (the service made with the server's `shown`), and sent back so, written as the file had it; each write told in `writes.jsonl` with its time, `by` the page or the Agent (the add with `agent: true` alone) and its cards' kinds (the first `place`'s three cards' cells, the ids it filled none); `restore-defaults` (the home page's alone, 409 on another; where the test's folder of Gallery's own blocks holds neither today nor note, both skipped as missing and nothing written, no version kept; in a workspace of its own with the package's blocks, today put back at the first free spot by its default size and put into the workspace first, the note an Agent wrote and every other byte as they were, its card's markup and its name in both languages answered; nothing missing, nothing written; today turned off, skipped as such); `POST hint` closing the home page's first-open note for the workspace (another page's refused, another token refused); and `GET library`. `tests/unit/workspace-layout.mjs` covers the workspace itself (`src/shared/workspace.mjs`, [internals.md](internals.md) "Where things live"): `components/` (and `pages/`, the custom pages', the same), no name a new document takes whatever stands there, `newDocSlug` (a `doc-<id>` folder name whose id no name of the workspace has, a custom page's, 写作偏好's and the trash's included, an old name's hash and the earlier id of a `doc-<eight>` folder too, its folder free; `idHolder` naming whose id a `doc-<id>` slug would take), no document of itself and hiding none, what makes a folder of it a custom block (`packageFolders`), passed by in `gallery list`, `gallery check --all` and `gallery export --all`, what the watcher makes of a change in it (`changeOf`), and a custom block copied in by `copyOldDocuments` when the workspace holds none of its id, a document of that name left behind on a line of its own; where it is found (`--workspace`, `GALLERY_WORKSPACE`, the nearest `gallery.json` of ours, `~/Documents/Gallery`; never another program's `gallery.json`, and never the user's own in the tests), the Documents folder it is made in (`documentsFolder()` with the registry, the files and the user's home given: Windows' Personal shell folder, OneDrive's and a Chinese name whole, a variable unset, a folder not there, a value that is no path and a failing `reg.exe` passed over, and never read for another home; Linux's XDG folder, turned off or not there passed over, `XDG_CONFIG_HOME` read; macOS `~/Documents`; this system's own read for real), made once, which folders are documents (doc.html, or an old one's report.html, read and written under its own names), the 写作偏好 page's folder and slug, a commit written into each document's own file, an export leaving every other file of `exports/` alone, what the preview's watcher hears (`changeOf`, `ignoredIn`: the page's files too, never `.gallery/`'s own), `copyOldDocuments` (`gallery init --from`: each part, slugs taken skipped, the old folder only read, held to by a digest of it before and after), `adoptOldLayout` (once, the old copies left), and the default home page (`src/editor/home-page.mjs` `makeDefaultHome`): none from `ensureWorkspace` or under `GALLERY_NO_DEFAULT_HOME` (set but to `""`, `"0"` or `"false"`), a new workspace's made with today and note at the first free spots by their default sizes, its `page.json` 首页 and no script, their blocks put in and kept in `.gallery/blocks.json`, `gallery.json` saying `pages.homeMade` and nothing else changed; a second call and one after it was taken away making none; an existing workspace's made beside its documents, `gallery.json`'s other keys kept; an Agent's `pages/home` kept byte for byte and only marked, no block put in; none for a `gallery.json` that does not read; none, and not marked, where a document is called `home`; nothing thrown where it cannot be made (a file where `pages/` goes: `failed`, step `page`, `homeFailure`'s line, nothing marked; on Windows a read-only `gallery.json`: the page made, `failed` at `mark` with `EPERM`, the next call the same, the file as it was); the dev server's `adoptWorkspace` making it once, and starting on a workspace where it cannot be made, saying so in one `console.warn` line. `tests/unit/workspace-env.mjs` is imported first by every unit test file: unless one is named, the workspace (`GALLERY_WORKSPACE`) is an empty folder in the run's own temporary data folder (`GALLERY_DATA_DIR`, taken away when the run ends), and so is the default workspace (`GALLERY_DEFAULT_WORKSPACE`), so a file run alone (`node tests/unit/model.mjs`) never resolves a workspace of its own and never reads or writes documents of the checkout; the Component Catalog is read from `tests/fixtures/component-catalog/`, and a test that needs documents makes a temporary workspace and names it. `tests/unit/workbench.mjs` type-checks the workbench app (`src/workbench/app/`: strict, no unused names, its session types against the shared protocol module); what the app does is tested in the browser suite's workbench scenarios.

`tests/unit/web-links.mjs` covers the sidebar's columns and web links ([workbench.md](workbench.md) "Columns", "Web links"): the outline's shape (`src/shared/outline-shape.mjs`: a column's top key, the columns kept, a new id), its file of version 2 (`src/shared/outline.mjs`: 文档's (the own column's: 默认文档 in the default workspace) tree where version 1 had the whole tree, every other column in `groups`, read back as written, version 1 read as 文档 alone, the own column titled 文档 or Documents, its old name, read as no title while a name the user gave and another column's 文档 stay, a draft of another workspace's spelling valid), `writtenAs` with and without columns, entries moved across columns and under a link, the column of the links made once; a link's file (`src/shared/web-links.mjs`: http or https, no `javascript:`, `file:` or `data:`, a name of 1 to 200, an emoji or a glyph, this Gallery's own address by any of its names, a slug of the title's Latin words or the site's name); the service's `POST link` (the column of the links the first time, under an entry, after one, first; places not there and this Gallery's address refused with nothing made), `link-set`, `POST outline` with columns made, ordered and removed (the link back last in 文档) and a tree of version 1 keeping the other columns, the text search passing links over, trash and restore of kind `link` (back where it was, a slug taken meanwhile the next free one), `frameable` kept an hour and `title`; and `gallery link add/set/remove` with `gallery list` and `list --tree` (column by column) with no server running.


`tests/unit/drafts.mjs` covers registered storage roots and owner identities (`~<ws id>/<slug>`): unknown and ambiguous IDs refused, document/link metadata and missing files, pasted file URLs, shared placement and hierarchy, joining the default tree, project choices and file-manager arguments. `shared-tree.mjs` and `copy-tree.mjs` cover global ordering and confirmed copies; `projects.mjs` covers owner-prefixed HTTP requests, original-folder saves, assets, search, trash, canonical addresses and same-origin write checks. `cli-server.mjs` verifies concurrent commands from different folders converge on one PID/port and stop that service from either folder. No project-level services or cross-origin relay endpoints remain.

`tests/unit/extensions.mjs` covers the sidebar columns extensions provide ([workbench.md](workbench.md) "Extension columns"): an extension's id and its column's (`src/shared/outline-shape.mjs`: 48 letters at most, never a workspace id's notation, a column whose id is not its extension's an ordinary one, no entry of the outline's in its column), the file's rules (`src/shared/extension-sidebar.mjs`: titles tidied, an address as the URL spells it, each reason that makes a file unusable said as a template with its values, an icon that is none only a note, 8 levels, 2000 entries and 1 MB), the folders read (`src/shared/extensions.mjs`: no dot name, assets, uppercase or workspace id, an extension with no `sidebar.json` listed apart, a file that does not read keeping the name it says), the outline keeping a column's place as the user dragged it and never an entry, the watcher's reading of a path (`changeOf`, `ignoredIn`: an extension's `node_modules` and `.git` are no news), what the server tells the workbench of an extension's folder (`tests/unit/fixtures/extension-watch.mjs`, run in a workspace of its own: a `sidebar.json` written is told; news of the folder or of another file in it only when the columns changed, so that a file the extension's own program writes there, which the recursive watch of Windows and macOS reports as the folder's change, is not; a folder moved in with its `sidebar.json` or moved away is), the workspace service listing the columns with the outline and refusing an entry placed in one, and `gallery extension list` and `check <id>` with `gallery list --tree` in the sidebar's order (they write nothing, each failure its exit code).

`tests/unit/document-look.mjs` covers a document's look (字体与布局, `src/shared/document-look.mjs`, [render.md](render.md) "Design defaults"): what `doc.json` keeps of it (each choice that is not the workspace's default look's, an explicit default too, an unknown value left out, the field in its place or last, gone when none is left, the other fields kept: `ownLookOf`, `ownLookFor`, `withLookField`), the look a document shows choice by choice (`effectiveLook`: its own, else the workspace's, else the default; what is kept always showing the look asked for), the workspace's default in `gallery.json` (`src/shared/workspace-look.mjs`: written beside its components and in its place, each default left out, the field gone at the default, nothing written for the same look, the file made when there is none and none made for the default, one that does not read or is another program's refused and left as it was and read as none), its attributes on `<html>` as `withLook` puts them (the file's own taken off; none without a look) and as the render does from both files (`renderReportHtml`: the workspace's for a document without its own, its own beside it, its explicit defaults over it, a `doc.json` that does not read giving the workspace's and saying it does not read, the 写作偏好 page none), set and read on an element, the live reload's word for it, a copy of the view keeping it (the page's `runtime.state`), 全宽 (`look.full`: a choice beside 正文宽度 neither takes away, on `<html>` as the kit's own `data-doc-width="full"`, put on and taken off an element, kept in `doc.json` and as an explicit default over a workspace's, no rule of the look's own, `page.css`'s one rule of the wide page), and the kit's rules of it (`src/render/look-css.mjs`): `@kit-look;` once in `tokens.css`, written where it stands in `report.css`, every rule one of the look's attributes (a document without them takes none), the default giving no token another value, the widths 648px, 712px (the kit's) and 776px whatever the size (40, 44 and 48 characters of the default 16px body text), 字体大小 giving the text's sizes and line heights and nothing else, the gaps and `--doc-space` 正文间距's alone, every value a whole pixel, the serif the system's with the kit's controls in the sans and 混合体's rule of no weight, and `flow.css` and `page.css` taking their own margins at `--doc-space`. `tests/unit/workspace.mjs` holds `POST look` (written, left out at the default, kept by a rename, nothing written for the same look; under a workspace's default, 默认 kept as an explicit default and a choice the workspace's makes too left out) and `POST looks` (refused with every `doc.json` as it was while `gallery.json` does not read; the workspace's default written in a `gallery.json` made for it, every document's own taken away, an old one's `report.json` included and the rest of it kept, the 写作偏好 page's left alone, a `doc.json` with none not written, how many replaced (one whose own showed the same not counted), every document listed with it and a new one too, and applied again from a document of the default look taking the field away).

`tests/unit/page-links.mjs` covers a document's link in the workbench's address (`src/shared/page-links.mjs`, [workbench.md](workbench.md)): the page id, of a block id's look and the same for ever (pinned for a few folder names), a `doc-` and eight characters (a lowercase letter, then lowercase letters or digits) having those eight as its id and the near misses (another case, a digit first, one character more or fewer, a suffix) keeping the hash they had (pinned), a folder of that shape an Agent made before (`doc-overview`) still opened by the address it had (pinned: the hash `pageId` gave, `earlierId`, read after every folder's own eight, `earlierLink`), a new id picked from the random source it is given and another while one is held, what a link cannot hold, a title that leaves no words, what a link reads as (a folder name first, else the id after its last dash, whatever words stand before it), the trash, and what an address written for anyone goes by. The scenarios `workbenchLinks`, `pageLinkWorkedOut` (nothing written, a `links.json` left there read by nothing, a title alone naming none, a reload and a server started again) and `pageLinkRename` (a title changed and changed back, the trash, a page restored, one restored under another folder name) drive it in the workbench; `pageLinkFolderFree` a new folder, `doc-<page id>`, beside a page titled the same, its address agreeing with the folder's eight, and the next one another's; `pageLinkOldTitle` an address written before a title changed, from the address bar, a click, a presentation, a paste and the link card, across a server started again, of a page in the trash, and with a new page of that title; `blockLinkOutlasts` what all this is for: a block's address copied, the title changed twice, another page given the old title, the address opening the first page at the block and the bar then showing the title of now; `blockLinkTitle` 复制块链接 from an old document and a new one of one title, with a title not saved yet, an h1 its `doc.json` does not hold, a title with no words and no list read; `linkShownByTitle` what a reader is shown of where a link leads (the card's field, a page in the trash too, its copy button, a link made, the search's rows).

`tests/unit/page-search.mjs` covers the search of a link card, a page block's box and "@" (`src/editor/page/links/pages.mjs`): its ranking (a title's start, a word's, anywhere, its characters in order; the sidebar's order within each; no pinyin; six rows), the list as the server gives it (the sidebar's order, column after column, 写作偏好 and web links out, a document under a link in, this page by the title it shows, the page each is under, each page's link, the trash), the list held for a copy (the last one read, this page's link by the title it shows now, with no list read and for a page newer than the list, what stays when no answer comes), an address read in it, and a list that cannot be read. The scenario `linkBoxPages` drives the card: rows under the field, a press on one, Enter's order, a paste, a link's own address beside its title, an input method, the window's end; `pasteOwnLink` our own address pasted and a chip copied to another document; `pageChip`, `pageChipReload`, `pageChipShown` and `pageChipCreate` the page block (its look, keys and click; the empty form; the served title in the preview, a presentation and an export, no commit of it, 批注 reading the file's words; a new subpage); `mentionMenuIme`, `mentionNeverCommitted`, `mentionGiveUp`, `mentionPlaces` and `mentionLook` the "@" menu; `slashNeverCommitted` the "/" menu's filter kept out of the file.

`tests/unit/kept-view.mjs` covers where the reader was kept across a page shown again (`src/shared/kept-view.mjs`, [workbench.md](workbench.md) "Where the reader was"): a view kept and taken once (unreadable or without a scroll position none), one left as a page goes never started at by itself and forgotten when a page starts, kept for the page only within `LEFT_FOR`, which page the workbench shows again (the same document's report area made again, its reload by Vite, the area gone first and Vite's reload heard after; not the reader's F5, a document come to otherwise, nor back to it), the view a workbench hands a frame (`handedView`: once, none from no frame, unreadable words or an unreachable one, ahead of a view kept), the workbench's memory of where the reader left a page (`state/recall.ts`: a copy, back within `RECALL_FOR` and not past it, forgotten then, a fresh arrival not, another page's ask not this one's, nothing readable nothing kept, `caretIn`), no storage at all; `reading-place.mjs` `elementOf` with more written above (a table's row found in its block where it stood, a place kept before `inner` its block, the element gone its block, the block gone none; on a custom page by its own id and its card), and `onReaderMove` (a wheel once, a scroll of the page's own none, a move before it listened told at once through `earlyMoveScript`).

`tests/unit/page-address.mjs` covers a document's addresses (`src/shared/page-address.mjs`, [workbench.md](workbench.md)) and what an export writes of them (`src/render/page-link-rewrite.mjs`): every form with a block and without, what needs the list, what is no address (a web address, another host, a slug the list does not hold, a workbench's address with no list), what a file and a full address write, an address on another port, an address read on a server, by the words it has on another port (a `doc-<eight>` folder's too by the link it had before, under the same words), a link read as its file reads it; a link written anew on the page's tree, what only reads like one left alone, a link left open that the parser reads twice, another program's address; what a page block's address may be and how a page shows one (`src/render/page-chips.mjs`). The scenarios `pageLinkClick`, `pageLinkForms`, `pageLinkElsewhere`, `pageLinkMoves`, `pageLinkTrashed`, `pageLinkBack`, `blockReveal`, `blockAddress`, `hashSilent`, `copyBlockLink`, `blockLinkSolo`, `pageLinkAlone`, `pageLinkPresent`, `pageLinkPresentList`, `pageLinkExport` and `pageLinkViewCopy` drive them in a workbench, on a page alone, in a presentation and on disk. `pageLinkAlone` and `pageLinkPresent` also follow an address that names a web link of the sidebar (`#<slug>`) or an entry of an extension's column (`#~<id>/<entry id>`, an entry with no address no place, said, the page staying): a page alone in a tab sends the tab to the workbench, which opens it, rather than saying it found no page, and a presentation opens a tab and never the document route of a folder that holds no document. `blockLinkBack`, `blockLinkBackAlone` and `blockLinkBackReader` hold a link to a block of the page as a step of the history (`src/shared/reading-place.mjs`) in a workbench, on a page alone and in an exported file: the way back where the reader was (a disclosure the jump opened above the place, a detail card it opened closed, the caret back where the page edits), the way forth to the block, no step for the block just gone to, and from the jumped place to another document and back twice.

`tests/unit/transfer.mjs` covers 导入 and 导出 (`src/transfer/`): zips written and read back (UTF-8 names, a GBK name, zip64, a file streamed out), how an export names its files and links them, the export's options, a document as Notion's Markdown (what a box shows that no unit stands for kept, what no reader sees not; a flowchart an ordered list, each step's content under it; written as nodes, each node's emoji and title, bold as written, its item's line, an icon of the 图标 tab no emoji, a flowchart in a node its item's nested list), and files made pages: Markdown with its pictures (Notion's alt text and repeated caption), CSV, a Notion export (its icon, picture, bookmark, PDF and subpage), Markdown in folders, Word and EPUB; and 写作偏好 as `reports/preferences/通用写作.md`, the file a new workspace starts with (under `# 通用写作`, its file's name, whatever the page's title, the heading alone while the page has nothing written, blocks left empty included; written only when saved (the request without `opening`), the file's digest, the page's version and the lines the page does not show kept as `madeSame` in its `report.json`, an old `synced` gone; opened with neither changed, answered from what was kept (nothing read as Markdown), a `madeSame` of before `unshown` given them; as the dialog opens, the page made again of a file someone else wrote, of Gallery's default made the first time, once (opened again, not made again), and of the file after the page was typed into or colored and not saved (the color not in the Markdown), the page as it was kept first and the file left byte for byte; a page saved shown as saved, its color too, not made again; with no `madeSame` yet, a page saying the same as the file left as it is; a hand-written file's page made once, its lines the page cannot show named at each opening, the file kept as written until the page is saved; a file with what a user's own rules hold and the short default does not (numbered steps, one continued on an indented line, bold, inline code, an HTML example in a code block, a table) made a page showing every line, the file left as written; `unshownLines` itself: joined, spaced, renumbered words, tags and a picture's address are no loss, a comment's words and a link's address are).

`tests/unit/cli-export-look.mjs` covers the commands that give an Agent what the workbench's dialogs give the user, each run as a user runs it in a workspace and a data directory of its own: `gallery set`'s `--font`, `--size`, `--spacing`, `--width`, `--full` and `--all` (only the choices named written into doc.json's `look`, the others kept, a default named kept as a choice, `--title` in the same command, every value that is not a choice and `--font` without a value refused with 2 and nothing written, `--all` writing `appearance.json` of the data directory and leaving a document's own choices, `--all` alone applying the look the document shows); `gallery emoji add` and `list --json` (the name as the picker makes it, the library's file and entry, a taken name, a standard emoji's name, a bad name, a file that is no image and one that is not there refused), and `gallery set --icon <name>` and `:name:` (the image copied into the document's `assets/` and made the icon, a name that is none refused with 2); `gallery export --format` (the offline file as before with no `--format` and with `--format html`, Markdown as `exports/<slug>.md` with the hand-in said and the deliverable line last, `--out`, a zip with the page named by its title and the picture in `assets/`, `--no-files`, `<slug>-no-files.html`, `--subpages` flat and with `--folders`, the subpage handed in with the page and its failure failing the page above it, a document the checks fail written in no format, `--allow-failures`, and every option that does not go together or is out of range refused with 2); the PDF (`%PDF-`, the paper's size from the page box, the command ending when the PDF is made, not a minute later) where Chrome or Edge is installed, said and skipped where it is not; and, with a production server running in the process, `gallery set` going through it (the event stream hears `look`), `gallery export` with its hand-in, and `gallery open <document> --present` giving the workbench's address with `?present` before the `#`, refused without a document or with `--settings`. The browser scenario `workbenchPresentLink` opens that address: the workbench opens the document, shows its 演示视图 over the window (the reader's page, no editor), takes `?present` off the address, and presents nothing again on a refresh or with another query.

`tests/unit/export-weight.mjs` covers what an export weighs: the grammars of `code.js` (`src/shared/code-grammars.mjs`): every one carried a line each, in the list's order; a language's grammar (`bash` and `sh` Shiki's `shellscript`, `text` and Rust none) and what it embeds (HTML: JavaScript and CSS); the grammars a page uses read off its source (`pageGrammars`) and by the kit off the same page (`DocCode.grammarsUsed`, in jsdom), the two agreeing on every page there (none, JSON, five languages, HTML, a language by its title or its code alone or a `language-` class, a diff by its path or its `language`, Rust, a template's, a script of JSON and the kit's own counting for nothing, a script, a module, an event handler, a `javascript:` address and a package's script naming `DocCode` or `ReportCode` keeping all, and so does one that puts a code block or a diff on the page as it runs, by `doc-code`, `doc-diff` or `report-code`, while one that names neither keeps the page's own); a block whose language is only its code's `language-python` class, its code SQL, read as Python by both and still Python once the kit has drawn it, and drawn again; a file carrying JSON's and HTML's alone colors JSON and draws Python, Rust and HTML (its embeds missing) plain, failing nowhere; and `offlineReports` carrying none for a page without code, JSON's for one JSON block, five for five languages. And the pictures an export embeds (`src/shared/export-pictures.mjs`, `render/export-pictures.mjs`): the ceiling by the page's look (1424, 1296 偏窄, 1552 偏宽, none at 全宽); what `pictureFacts` reads of a header (a PNG's long side and an animated one's acTL, a JPEG's frame, a lossy, lossless and extended WebP, an animated one, a GIF none); which `pictureTask` tries (a PNG within the ceiling or over it, and at 全宽; not 16 KB or less, nor an animation; a JPEG wider than the ceiling, not one within it nor any at 全宽; a tall lossy WebP by its long side; not an SVG); a copy kept a tenth lighter only; `smallerPictures` with an encoder of the test's taking each `<img>`'s picture once with the page's ceiling and quality, writing the copies in the `<img>`s' `src` alone (a CSS background's, a srcset's, a template's and one not a tenth lighter as they were), and with an encoder that has no browser leaving the file as it was and saying why, and with one that cannot read one picture (its Error) leaving that one as it was, the others' copies in, `unread` and `why` said; and, where Chrome or Edge is installed, real pictures made in it (a 2000px screenshot-like PNG, one see-through in a corner, a photograph-like 3000px JPEG, one 1000px wide, a small PNG, a PNG a CSS background names) exported by `offlineReports` with `encodePictures`: WebPs 1424px wide that decode, the transparency kept, the 1000px JPEG, the small PNG and the background as they were, three made lighter and the pictures less than half their bytes; at 全宽 the PNGs WebPs at their own width and the JPEGs as they were; with `{ pictures: false }`, or no encoder, every picture as it was; `encodePictures` given a 1000×20000 PNG and, at 全宽, a 20000×400 one (each ending in a red band) making them 819×16383 and 16383×328 with the band there, a broken PNG an Error of its own and the screenshot beside it made all the same. Last, `gallery check` and `gallery export`: a heavy screenshot gets no note where Chrome or Edge is there, and one saying why with a `GALLERY_BROWSER` that is not there and with `--original-images`.

`tests/unit/export-platform.mjs` covers the export for another platform (`gallery export --allow-images`, `--embeds-as-links`): what `imageSources` takes (`https:`, origins, a `*.` first label, a port, commas and the option again, each once, 20 at most) and every source it refuses, with why, in the policy's own call too (empty, a space, a line break, a tab, `;`, quotes, `'unsafe-inline'`, `'self'`, letters outside ASCII, `data:`, `blob:`, `http:`, other schemes, `*`, a path, a query, a user, a port out of range, two wildcards, an IPv6 host); the widened policy (its `img-src` the sources added, every other directive as `EXPORT_CSP`, the same ones in the same order) in the file, read back as an export's own metas; `embedsAsLinks` (a web page's card, by its host or its frame's title, escaped; a file's, by its name and why, linking nowhere, in the page's language; the figure's classes, id and caption kept; an empty block as it is; a web card a bookmark by the kit's check); `offlineReports` with both (no frame, the PDF not carried, its own pictures inside) and the workbench's 导出 route and `exportPage`, which a request's options never widen; `gallery export` with them (`<slug>-web.html` and `-no-files-web.html`, the deliverable's label, the `network:` line under `Exported`, `--out`, the offline file left as it was, and every mistake refused with 2 and nothing written); a document the checks fail (its starter text) refused by them as by the offline file's export (exit 1, `FAIL`, no `问题：` line, nothing written, `--allow-failures` exiting 0 and writing none), while the 导出 dialog's (`--no-files` alone) writes it and says `文件照写了`; the dialog's export of a document no export can be made of (an embed's address holding a quote) never saying it was written; with `--subpages`, a page under it that fails failing the file (its own `FAIL`, the parent's saying which), and once mended `<slug>-web.zip` labelled `离线文件 压缩包，可显示网络图片` beside `<slug>.zip`, which stays as it was; and, where Chrome or Edge is installed, the files opened from disk, a picture put in the text as the platform's program puts one, answered by request interception (a request the policy blocks is never made): from an allowed source it loads, from another it is blocked (a `securitypolicyviolation` of `img-src`), and in the offline file it is blocked; the embeds are cards, the web page's a link to it.

`tests/unit/from-markdown.mjs` covers a Markdown document made a document (`gallery import`, `src/cli/import.mjs`, and the workbench's 导入 of a `.md`: `importMarkdown`, `markdownPage` in `src/transfer/import.mjs`, its reader `readMarkdownDocument` in `src/editor/page/clipboard/import/markdown.mjs`), each construct as the document has it: `$…$`, `$$…$$`, `\(…\)` and `\[…\]` formulas while prices stay words (Pandoc's rule: `$5 and $6`, `US$3`, `$5-$10`, `US$6 or $$`, an escaped `\$`), `$$…$$` amid words a formula block, a display formula closed before an empty line (`$$ (1)` its `\tag`, words after its close a paragraph; one not closed ending at its empty line, said, and what follows read as it stands; one with nothing in it its words), a LaTeX environment on lines of its own (one not ended given its `\end`), formulas KaTeX draws as the page does (the kit's macros, mhchem) and one it cannot said at its line; GFM tables (pipes at the ends or not, right after a paragraph's line, the alignment row, an escaped pipe in code, a formula and words), task lists, strike-through, autolinks (`<…>`, `www.`, a bare address with its escapes read, an e-mail in `<>` or bare), a picture in a cell said at its row's line; footnotes (a superscript link, the notes a section at the end, 注释 or Notes, one with no definition noted at its line); fenced code with its language and title (`title=` in the info string, Pandoc's `{.py title=…}`; a bold line above a fence stays a paragraph); headings (the front matter's title, a folded one too, a `#` heading repeating it leaving the body, and the body's only `#` heading when it is its first, said; the file's name without either; the first `#` heading the title wherever it stands; `##` the sections wherever the first stands, a divider before one at its top, `###` an h3 and deeper an h4, setext, `{#id}` left off, a later `#` a section of its own, with no `##` the shallowest headings the sections); a quote's lazy line, a quote holding a list, code or a heading (those in place out of it, no empty quote, said), GitHub's alerts, `<details>`, lines joined as a viewer joins them (none between Chinese characters) and hard breaks; lists holding their paragraphs and code and keeping their numbers (`start`, one going on after a block); pictures copied (one file a picture), their alt text or title the caption, one in an item held by it, one in a heading its words, an HTML block of pictures alone pictures, one in an HTML block kept as written given the copy's address, one not found left out at its line (the words around it one paragraph again); HTML blocks kept as written, `<script>`, a comment and a lone `<br>` left out; a mermaid chain of steps an `ol.doc-flow`, any other mermaid its code, noted; links to a file beside the document or to places in it said (chosen with its file in the workbench, a link alone in its paragraph a file); the reader's lines counted after a front matter; and a Markdown file chosen with its picture and alone (`importFiles`: its notes name the picture). A paste makes each line a block. Then real documents, those `GALLERY_MARKDOWN_SAMPLES` names (paths separated by `;`; none by default, as the tests go with the skill's package; read-only copies in a folder of the test's own): nothing of the conversion's own noted, no formula left as a raw `$` (a price's stays), every table and heading the document's, a section for each `##` heading (else each shallowest), and each document passing `gallery check` (`checkReports` on the document as its preview shows it).

`tests/unit/roundtrip.mjs` covers the import's round trip (docs/workbench.md "Import and export"): a document of each thing an export changes (`tests/unit/fixtures/roundtrip.mjs`: natural HTML, a picture in a cell, a disclosure, a code block, formulas, a chart card and one of the document's own chart type, a whole block with its own `<style>` and a style attribute's `url()`, picture, file and embed blocks, a link to a CSV with `download`, a picture as the page's icon, emoji and glyph icons in highlight blocks, a Sheet tab's emoji), with what an import once got wrong around it (its `lang`, a head of its own, a comment and a kicker above a title with markup and entities, a bookmark's pictures, a cropped picture, a picture also a link to save it, a Sheet tab's picture of its own), once with LF line breaks and once with CRLF, exported by `gallery export` in a Gallery directory of its own and imported into another through the transfer service (POST upload, POST import): its report.html is the source as the export read it, byte for byte, but for the slug, the names of the files an Agent named (an upload's name, a link's `download` name given) and the crop the export did not carry, every file back with its bytes and once, report.json's title and icon; each export says the sha256 of the report.html it was made from, which the import takes out; exported again, it is the first export but for its slug, the crop and that sha256. `tests/unit/transfer.mjs` holds an own document's page as written (its report.html, an export, one with the kit in its main, a view copy) and a document's folder, zipped or chosen together, coming in as one page.

`tests/unit/i18n.mjs` holds the UI's two languages (`src/shared/i18n/`): every Chinese string the workbench and the page editor show goes through `t()` and has its English in `en.mjs` with the same `{0}`, `{1}`…, none is left outside `t()` (a block's search `words` and console messages aside; a style sheet's comments are not read), the server's messages the UI shows have their English too (one with parts filled in sent as `said()`'s template, a literal: a server file composing Chinese with values in a template literal fails, as does a `said()` whose template is no literal; `told()` says a template in the UI's language, its parts as they are), and `en.mjs` holds no entry no string uses (but those in the test's `AHEAD` list, made before the wave that uses them: a wave takes its key out of the list); an entry may be a function of the values for a count's words (`many()`, `one()` at the head of `en.mjs`), which must fill in every value the Chinese does and leave no Chinese or `{0}` in its forms, and the plurals that matter ("1 conflict", "1 edit conflicts … wasn't saved", rows and columns, entries, a count written as text) are held at one and at many. `tests/unit/kit-words.mjs` holds the kit's own words (`src/render/kit/words.js`) to the same: each in Chinese and English with the same `{0}`, `{1}`…, each one a kit script, a chart's reason (`src/shared/table-chart.mjs`) or the stylesheet says in its bundle's part and none unsaid, no Chinese in a stylesheet's `content` but the table's; the language the kit speaks (the UI's `data-ge-language`, else the page's lang: en English, any other Chinese) as a code block's header, the display settings and a flowchart's name in built bundles show it; and the page's first script (`languageScript`) setting the UI's language as the UI reads it. The browser suite runs in Chinese (`--lang=zh-CN`: the UI follows the browser's language until one is chosen); `workbenchLanguage` switches to English and back, `serverMessagesEnglish` has a server's messages with parts filled in said in English (the workbench's export check dialog, the page's size refusal and offline-file export through `post.mjs`) and the import's toast listing the files not taken with the UI's separator, `kitWords` checks the kit's and the editor's served words in both. It also starts each page with 显示编辑框 on (the scenarios were written with the dashed frame showing; it is off by default) unless a scenario keeps preferences of its own, as `workbenchPreferences` does.

`tests/unit/boundaries.mjs` holds the boundaries of `src/` ([internals.md](internals.md), "Where things live"): each system imports only itself and `src/shared/`, browser code imports nothing that needs Node or the server's component modules, the editor's served modules import by relative path only, an editing tool imports no other tool's folder but the few it lists ("Where the editing tools live" in [editor.md](editor.md)), and an engine of `src/editor/page/` imports no component's `page.mjs` but the few it lists, `editor.mjs` making them through `components/pages.mjs`, which lists every `create(ctx)` ("Component definitions" in [editor.md](editor.md)).

`tests/unit/file-language.mjs` holds the language of the words Gallery itself writes into files (`src/shared/file-language.mjs`): the rule (the locale Node gives before the usual variables, `C`/`POSIX` no language, `GALLERY_LANGUAGE` over it, anything but `zh` and `en` ignored; Intl is faked to say a system's language), then what is written by it, in English and in Chinese: the default home page (Home or 首页 in page.json and `<title>`, the page's lang; a home already made is left as it is when the system's language changes), a custom page's lang, the starter (`starterPage`: its lang, its section's words, found by `data-doc-placeholder` and `<!-- Starter content:` in both, `isNewDocument` and `gallery check` failing while it remains the same; a `lang` the caller knows says the words too; the marks filled in one pass), and `gallery new`'s description, a copy's title suffix (`gallery duplicate`) and an import's description, run as the command in each language. Every unit test and browser scenario runs with `GALLERY_LANGUAGE=zh` (`workspace-env.mjs`, `harness.mjs`, the release tests' environments), so nothing depends on the machine; this file puts the English side on and back.

`tests/unit/engine-names.mjs` holds the engines to the components' definitions ("Component definitions" in [editor.md](editor.md)): it reads the syntax tree of each engine file (everything under `src/editor` but the components and `page/table/`, `src/transfer`, `src/render` but the kit and the starter, `src/cli`, `src/server`) and counts the places that name a concrete component: any string in the code that is a component's name, wherever it stands (a comparison either way round, a `case`, a list, a return, a value), each key of an object with two or more keys that are names, a use of an `isX` imported from a component's folder, a `matches`, `closest`, `querySelector` or `classList.contains` of a `doc-*` name, and a comparison of a `localName`, `tagName` or `tag` with the tag of a component's blocks. The names are the registry's unit kinds, box kinds, block types and host kinds, but `text` and `label`. It fails a file whose count differs from its entry in `ENGINE_NAMES`, either way, or that has none. A lower number is written down; a higher one means a component's knowledge went into an engine, and a definition field is the place for it. It counts what the code says, so a string that only reads like a name counts too: the numbers are a measure to bring down, not a list of faults, and a new such line raises its file's number with a reason in its commit. An entry with a comment keeps its places on purpose and says why, what stays in the engines by the decision of 2026-10-30 or strings that only read like a name, and a comment that counts them keeps that many, the rest of its number being left to bring down; an entry with none is what is left to bring down. No page engine spells an instance's attribute (`data-doc-component`) either: the board reads a card's instance through `src/editor/components/instances.mjs`, the host's own rule (a `div` or `section`), and `tests/unit/pages.mjs` holds it to the server's `readPage` for a card of a div, a section, an article, a span and none.

`tests/unit/docs.mjs` holds the documents to the tree: every `--doc-*` custom property named in [render.md](render.md) is a stylesheet's or a kit script's (a name written as a family, `--doc-h1-*`, needs one property of it defined), every `src/` path and every `.mjs` file the documents name exists (the browser harness's files, `tests/browser/`, are checked only where that folder is), every link from one document to a file of the repository reaches it and its anchor a heading of that file (as GitHub names headings; code samples and inline code hold no links), every link of a Markdown file of the skill (`src/cli/package-skill.mjs`, `SKILL_FILES`: inline links, with a title or in angle brackets, a reference's definition, an `<a href>` and an `<img src>` in the text; none in a code sample, fenced by backticks or tildes, or in a code span of any number of backticks) reaches a file of the skill, `skills/gallery/references/` holds the references the skill takes by name (`writing-html.md` and the example document, `example-report.html`) and nothing else (a component's card goes in `docs/components/`), and `SKILL.md` links each of them, every document a document names in inline code (a file or folder of `docs/`, `skills/`, the skill's references/ or sdk/, or the Component Catalog's) is there and its anchor a heading of it (for the Agent's documents, `skills/gallery/SKILL.md` and `skills/gallery/references/`, a file of the skill, written from the Skill's root, where a bare Markdown name is a file at the root; for the maintainers', a file of the repository), the Agent's documents name no maintainers' reference (no `docs/` path, in inline code or not), card or Component Catalog and hedge on no package ("if your package has it"), and the skill's own (`skills/gallery/SKILL.md`, `skills/gallery/references/`) no source file (`src/`), npm script (`npm run`) or `reports/` folder: the tools are the gallery command, and every file of `tests/unit/` is named here and run by `run.mjs`. It checks names, never what a sentence says of behaviour; those are held by the tests of the behaviour.

`tests/unit/skill-docs.mjs` holds the Agent's documents (`skills/gallery/SKILL.md`, `skills/gallery/references/`) clear of writing style and of faithfulness to the materials (tone, structure, an opening summary, length, wording, which block to use more or less, a chart's form, emoji, headings, numbers that trace back: the user's word, all of it 写作偏好's, the user's own files, Gallery's default 通用写作 only a short generic start; the skill keeps the tool's rules and says to read 写作偏好 and follow it), and the examples of the component cards (`docs/components/`), which left the skill but still show what the tools take; each of `SKILL.md` and the references is kept under 50,000 characters, so that one Read takes it whole (the tool's cap is 25,000 tokens, about 60,000 characters of these documents: the blind test of 2026-11-09 found `building-blocks.md`, then 82,000, read to its line 428, with Going online and Gallery's own blocks after it, and it is three references by topic now: `building-blocks.md` the block, `block-script.md` its script and writing back, with the two examples, the Writing back sample, the list of what `on` hears and the types a script names, and `pages-and-data.md` a card of a custom page and what a block reads beyond its parts: the workspace's data, a table's views, the web, whose Going online sample names in its needs the host it asks, one that does not redirect, and tells an Agent to `curl -i` an address first); `SKILL.md`'s rule on a script that replaces several spans names Node, which is always there; its rule on the kit says what an Agent may add, a document that uses a custom block made first, and sends it to `skills/gallery/references/building-blocks.md`, whose flow starts with that too, opens the check's pictures by its overviews and names those opened in the handoff, and says what a document's words hold around an instance (a value a gesture changes only with when it held, how to use the block in the handoff), whose manifest spells its Chinese name in pinyin among its words (`wantsPinyin()`, the check's note), whose repeated group is parts a manifest holds (`readCatalog()`), which opens with which kind of part to choose and the two hosts side by side, before its flow (a row for each kind the catalog takes, text, data, none and flow, each saying what a document and a custom page do with it, a slot not allowed on a custom page as `readCatalog()` refuses one to a block whose places hold page, the hosts naming each of the SDK's `context.place` values, the card's id, `context.size` and `layout`, and no second table of the two places), and which says what `min` and `max` count, what the user does with an instance in the page (no item of a group of text parts added there, one of a data part by a control of the component's own) and how a block is tried there (each gesture a reader makes by `gallery block try`, one command each, its lines of the write and of the undo quoted as it prints them, `TRY_LINES`, and what its exit 1 asks, `doc.html` copied first, what a `note` line asks, a `FAIL` saying `doc.html` holds the gesture's write the Agent's to put back, an exit 3 a trial that left nothing of the gesture, and each try shown on the user's page too; a gesture it cannot make on the Agent's own address by the DevTools protocol, each gesture's write waited for by `data-ge-save`, read in the file and undone once, the file left as it was; SKILL.md's row of that address says it is the page alone, editable, with no frame, and its rows of `gallery block try`'s lines and exit code quote how those lines start, say what a `note` asks and that a write left in `doc.html` is put back, an exit 3 leaving none), what Gallery draws of a script of data, what an export shows of a block with a script and that data a component cannot read goes to Gallery by `context.fail`, how the board hides its table (`table.hidden`, named in `runtime`) and that a list a card drops on cancels `dragenter` too; whose list of what `on` hears is every signal `skills/gallery/sdk/doc-kit.d.ts` declares, `owns` beside it and when `source` comes after an edit said, and whose `--doc-*` names are each one the kit's tokens define (`tokens.css`), text on an accent's fill said; its board's drop and scorecard's press, run on the fixtures' examples, move a row with the blank before it and write the JSON back as it was laid out, the score's line alone changed, its Writing back sample's example calls the move a drop calls, which keeps a body written a row a line so, and the JSON it lays out as jsonIn writes it, as a rule it states, comes back from a press of a score it has unchanged and from another with one line changed, at the start of a line and indented; and each of its `js` samples passes the check's type check (`diagnoseScript()`) with no word, notes included, a sketch in the mount of a component, the values it names typed; the Script section's names its context `DocComponentContext`, one of the four types `skills/gallery/sdk/doc-kit.d.ts` declares for every script, with no import, and the type check refuses a field that type has not. The default writing preferences (`src/shared/writing-preferences.md`) are no longer held byte for byte: until 2026-11-13 they were the user's own rules (项目报告写作, from SKILL.md and content-catalog.md since 2026-10-23, in Chinese since 2026-10-24) and each of their blocks had to keep the SHA-256 it had on main on 2026-10-22; on 2026-11-13 the user took those out (keeping their own copy) and Gallery ships a short generic default, 通用写作, in their place, so the frozen blocks and their check are gone. Every HTML example (a fenced block of `html`) carries a mark on the line before its fence: `<!-- you write -->` for natural HTML, which one `naturalize` changes with no note (a second changing nothing); `<!-- the file holds -->` for the kit's markup, which `naturalize` leaves as it is; `<!-- the file then holds -->` for what the `you write` example before it becomes, compared with what one `naturalize` makes of it (the white space between tags aside). Only what an example becomes may show the markup the tools write for plain HTML (a table's wrapper, the header look on a header cell, a disclosure's label, a formula's wrapper), so that no example teaches writing it by hand. Each is checked as a document would be (`checkReports`: a fragment in the `main` of a page with the kit's tags, a whole page with the tags put in; an empty file in a folder of its own for every `assets/<file>` it names; the chart types that the `js` samples of custom-chart.md and writing-html.md define in the document's script beside their examples), and none may leave text read-only in the preview or a layout note, nor a note on its script (a type error, only a note for a document, is none an Agent should copy). Write a new example with its mark; `node tests/unit/skill-docs.mjs` names any that does not do what its mark says. The example document, `skills/gallery/references/example-report.html` (the catalogue of blocks an Agent reads before it writes: every block once, its words saying what each is, its numbers placeholders, no document's subject or voice), is held as a `you write` example and more: one `naturalize` makes it the kit's with no note and no paragraph of loose words or node of a step written the short way (it writes both as the file holds them), a second changes nothing; what it becomes holds each kind of block `BLOCKS` lists (the header's icon and source line, the opening summary, a section's divider, `h2`, `h3` and `h4`, bold, inline code, a link, an inline formula, a highlight, a block of a color, bulleted, nested numbered and to-do lists, a quote, a callout, a highlight panel with its icon, a disclosure, a scope line, a table with a caption, a chart from a table, a picture with its caption, a flowchart with a node's icon, Sheet tabs, columns, a titled code block with its language, a formula block, a file with its size, a bookmark and an embed of a file of `assets/` with its height), a detail card its link opens (written the short way, a `<dialog id>` with an `h2` first), a chart of the document's own whose type its script defines and a whole block its `<style>` styles; nothing in it is read-only in the preview or a layout note; and it passes `checkReports` in a folder holding its files (its picture a real PNG), with no note on its script. And `SKILL.md`'s first section, "Before anything: the gallery command", names the versions of the command it works with: `^` and `package.json`'s version, and in words what that takes (`skillRange`, `withRange` of `src/cli/package-skill.mjs`), and the version that made it (`This skill is Gallery <version>'s`, `RECORD` of `src/cli/skill-record.mjs`, which `gallery --version` reads in an installed skill), as the repository's `skills/gallery/SKILL.md` already says them and the skill made from it (`buildSkill`, for another version too) says them, nothing else of it changed; and it has the Agent ask before it installs, saying what to do without Node, on `EACCES`, with `npx`, in PowerShell and in a cloud sandbox, and what to do with the lines `gallery --version` prints after the version, a `技能：` or a `新版本：` line (as `SKILL_LINE` and `NEWER_LINE` of `src/cli/updates.mjs` start them): told to the user in the task with the command it names, run only after their yes; `gallery doctor` for a `需要处理：` line; none stopping the task, nothing updated without the user's yes. No file of the skill asks the Agent to read a file again (no `重读`, no re-read before an edit: what to read is the Agent's, the user's of 2026-11-13), a revision reads of `writing-html.md` what its change needs, and step 4 has `gallery qa` export first, the review 写作偏好 asks for before it, and an `UNVERIFIED` line done as it says, nothing installed. Every command the Agent's documents name in code (`` `gallery …` ``) is one `src/cli/usage.mjs` has (its commands, `ALIASES`), a subcommand among that command's Usage lines where it has them (`gallery page frob` fails), and each option one its usage names; SKILL.md's Custom pages (自定义页) says the flow (the page and block lists first, 首页 is `pages/home`, every other card's id, place and data kept, `gallery page add`, Gallery's own blocks and `gallery/static` first, `gallery page try` then `gallery page check` with its pictures and changes, where in the sidebar, no summary or source line, both themes, no script), and pages-and-data.md's block for a custom page its flow and an example a workspace loads (`local/goals`: places `page`, one setting, a template the catalog takes; its `kit.js` passes the type check with the other samples). The table of `skills/gallery/references/writing-html.md`'s "Blocks at a glance" is what the components' definitions say (`glance`; `node src/cli/glance-table.mjs` writes it between its two marks, and a difference fails with that command): each row names a section the file has, a type by its menu label, and a block of the "/" menu has a row but the headings, whose component says one for them; a type's part names its tag and class (but what the row says only as an Agent writes it: a formula block, a detail card) in what an Agent writes or what the file holds after an export, and the parts that no type stands for are there, the headings' among them. The rest of the table's words are held only by being read.

`tests/unit/skill-install.mjs` covers the skill as it ships and `gallery skill install` (`src/cli/skill.mjs`), each run of the command with a home of its own (`HOME`, `USERPROFILE`, `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` and `CODEX_HOME` temporary folders: never this user's) and a workspace of its own: the skill holds `SKILL_FILES` alone (no `src/`, no `package.json`), and `packageSkill` into a release folder of the test's own writes its folder, the ZIP (`gallery/` and those files alone, byte for byte, read back with `readZip`), the `.sha256` and the manifest, replacing an older ZIP and an older `gallery/` while leaving `npm/`; `--dir`: installed (the files `buildSkill` makes), a second run unchanged, an older Gallery skill there replaced (a file it had gone, no folder of the swap left), no workspace made; refused with nothing changed (exit 1): a folder of something else, a git checkout that looks like an older skill, an older skill whose preview server runs (an announcement naming a running process); older skills in Claude Code's skills folder (one named `gallery`, one under the old name `html-report-studio`) beside a skill of something else: their documents copied into the workspace under their old names, recent versions, 写作偏好 and exports too (a slug and an export name the workspace has left as they were, and said), each moved whole into `.gallery/old-skill-<date>` and `-2` (a digest of each before and after), the other skill left, then a second run unchanged; which agents (`targetsOf`): every one whose home is there, else Claude Code's, `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` else `~/.claude`, `--agent`, `--project` (the project's folders there, else Claude Code's); `gallery --version` (the registry's check off) saying nothing of the skills it wrote, in the agents' homes and the project's, and of one whose `SKILL.md` says an older version the line `skillLine` writes, gone once `gallery skill install` ran again; and the command line's mistakes (exit 2).

`tests/unit/escape.mjs` pins the four sets of `src/shared/escape.mjs` character by character and what the writers that used to carry their own copy write for the five characters that break markup (a bookmark, a file and an embed block, a start tag's attributes, the starter's title and description), byte for byte; and that `$$`, `$&`, `` $` `` and `$'` in a title or a description are written as typed (escaped, never read as replacement patterns) in `<title>`, the `h1` and `report.json`.

`tests/unit/cli.mjs` runs the gallery command (`src/cli/bin.mjs`, the dispatcher `src/cli/gallery.mjs`, the usage of each command in `src/cli/usage.mjs`) in workspaces of its own (`--workspace`) and holds its command line, first read in the test's own process (`readCommandLine()`, `commandArguments()`, `documentArguments()` of `src/cli/arguments.mjs`): `--version` prints the version first (timed against `node -e 0`, the dispatcher importing nothing but Node's and the usage at load, and the module of its notes, `src/cli/updates.mjs`, nothing but Node's and two modules that import nothing; run by itself, `node src/cli/gallery.mjs`, the dispatcher runs the command line too; every command the test runs has the agents' homes, `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` and `CODEX_HOME`, and the update cache, `GALLERY_CACHE_DIR`, of the test's own and `GALLERY_NO_UPDATE_CHECK=1`, so the version is alone), and then its notes: where the cache is on each system, the registry asked (`GALLERY_REGISTRY`, else npm's settings, else npm's own: an `.npmrc` as npm reads it, with its comments, quotes and `${VAR}`; the environment's `npm_config_*` in any case, the user's `.npmrc`, `$HOME`'s or `npm_config_userconfig`, the global npmrc under the prefix of the environment, the user's `.npmrc`, the builtin npmrc or node's folder, or `npm_config_globalconfig`, the builtin npmrc last, `@heycys:registry` before `registry`, all in folders of the test's own), the check's process's environment (`NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1`, npm's proxy and noproxy in place of the environment's, in any case), the lines run by npx (`viaNpx`: npm's `_npx` folder; both lines giving `npx -y @heycys/gallery@^<version> skill install`, `versionNotes` in process), when a cache is stale (none, a day old, another registry's, an hour after a failed check, from the future), what turns the check off (`GALLERY_NO_UPDATE_CHECK`, `CI`, `NO_UPDATE_NOTIFIER`, but not `0` or `false`) and the folders `gallery skill install` writes a skill in, each once; run in a project folder with the agents' homes, the cache and a registry of the test's own (an HTTP server on 127.0.0.1 that counts what it is asked), a `技能：` line for each Gallery skill older than the gallery or saying no version (Claude Code's, Codex's, the project's `.claude/` one with `gallery skill install --project`), in that order, the version first, none for one of this version, a newer one, another skill or a git checkout, none for the skill `buildSkill` makes for this version and one for the skill it makes for an older; a `新版本：` line from a cache that knows a newer version, none from one that knows this version, an older one, a prerelease or what is no version, none with `GALLERY_NO_UPDATE_CHECK`, `CI` or `NO_UPDATE_NOTIFIER` though the cache is stale, the registry asked nothing; a stale cache said as it stands while a check starts that `--version` does not wait for (the registry answers 2 s later), claimed in the cache at once (`checking`, failed, the version known before), two more runs meanwhile starting none, the registry asked once for `/@heycys%2fgallery/latest`, the cache written and the next run's line, a fresh cache asking nothing; a registry that answers 500, a version that is no version, no JSON, or that nobody listens on: no line and no error, the cache marked failed with the version known before kept (the check's own record, not the claim), and not asked again within the hour; a cache folder that cannot be written: no check started, run after run, the registry asked nothing; with no `GALLERY_REGISTRY`, the mirror the user's `.npmrc` names asked (`/mirror/@heycys%2fgallery/latest`), a fresh cache of another registry that knows a newer version saying nothing and checked again of the mirror, and the mirror's version said next; where Node honours `NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY`, npm's proxy from the `.npmrc` (an HTTP server of the test's own that tunnels `CONNECT` to its registry) the way to a registry no one can reach but through it (`registry.invalid`), its answer written; every run with the machine's proxies and `npm_config_*` left out and `.npmrc` files of the test's own, `help` and `<command> --help`/`-h` print the usage and exit 0; an unknown command or option, a wrong number of arguments, no document named (but `check`, which then checks every one), documents named with `--all`, a name of no document that is not a slug (`Natural`, `../outside`, a link or an address no document has) or one the workspace keeps (`exports`), `--workspace` without its folder, two workspaces named, `--blocks` with no ids or with more than one document print why first, then the usage's first line with where the rest is (`gallery <command> --help says more`), two lines in all (for a mistake in a command line of `gallery block try`, try's three lines of the usage in place of that first one, `TRY_SYNOPSIS`, try naming its document `<slug>` as every command does), and exit 2 without reading or writing a document (a document of natural HTML keeps every byte, and so does its copy outside the workspace; nothing made). The workspace each command uses: `--workspace`, else `GALLERY_WORKSPACE`, else the nearest `gallery.json` up from the current folder, else `~/Documents/Gallery` (made on first use, with its `gallery.json`; the test's `HOME` and `USERPROFILE` a folder of its own), `gallery stop` making none; another program's `gallery.json` and a folder that cannot be written refused with what to do. `gallery init` makes a workspace once (`（新建）`, then `（已有）`), with no home page under `GALLERY_NO_DEFAULT_HOME` (every unit test's) and, without it, the default home page said in a line (`首页：<page.html> …`, today's and note's cards, their blocks put in, `gallery.json`'s `pages.homeMade`), none again once taken away, one that cannot be made (a file where `pages/` goes) said on stderr (`首页：The default home page was not made (…)`) with the workspace made and init ending well, and another command that makes a workspace (`gallery list`) making none, and `--from` copies an older Gallery's `reports/` into it (its documents whose slugs are free, the others listed; its 写作偏好, the sidebar order, the 写作偏好 page, the custom emoji and the recent versions beside it, each said; again, nothing copied and the workspace's 写作偏好 kept), the old folder byte for byte (a digest of it before and after). `gallery new` prints where it wrote, the workspace and where 写作偏好 is (made from Gallery's default the first time, byte for byte;
