The browser scenario `packsSettings` starts the studio with the packs installed and goes through 设置 › 组件列表's pack cards (one height, rounded, no line), a pack entered (its blocks alone, ‹ 返回 focused) and left (the focus on its card), a block's line and master switch inside one, 柔光风格 off by default and turned on by its card (both 便签 in 添加自定义块, each in its pack's section, no grey name), 导入 of a zip chosen and of a folder's path, a path not there said, and a pack switched off out of "/"; `packsDocumentBlocks` starts a workspace as 0.3.0 left it (Gallery 风格's `pack.json` of then, an older board as installed, a document written with `gallery/card`, `chart-card`, `kanban`, `metric` and `chart-view`) with this Gallery: the `pack.json` and the board brought up to Gallery's, each of the five drawn by its block and edited as ever (a word in the card's body, a number in the table moving the metric), 设置 › 组件列表 holding them in 默认块包 and Gallery 风格 its ten page cards, Gallery 风格 off leaving the three views in "/" (the card and the chart card are off until turned on) and 默认块包 off taking them out; `packsDefaultOff` holds those two off by their manifests' `listed: false` (2026-11-20): "/" offering the three views alone, a document written with both drawing and editing them as ever, 默认块包 listing both with their master switches off and the card's opened line saying so, the card turned on written in gallery.json's `listed` and offered and put in by "/", the chart card still not, a gallery.json of before (the card unlisted, the metric hidden) kept as the reader left it and the card turned on taking it out of `unlisted`, and a figure's block menu naming it 图形 (`GALLERY_TEST_SHOTS` keeps the pictures of "/", 默认块包 and that menu); `shippedChartCard` turns the chart card on in gallery.json before it puts one in from "/"; `settingsPage`, `settingsMaster`, `settingsDialogFocus`, `netConsent`, `homeCardsFollowGallery` and `pagePlacesMenus` enter a block's pack first (`compPack`). `tests/unit/shipped-blocks.mjs` holds Gallery's own blocks (`src/packs/gallery/`) to what they ship: every one its files (its writing notes naming it, its two pictures of the menus, WebP of their size, light and dark apart, and no `preview.svg`, each JSON part of its `template.html` and `example.html` as `context.editJSON` writes it back: on one line, compact, as `skills/gallery/references/block-script.md` tells an Agent to write one and `--recipe` copies), loading through the catalog in a workspace that added them all as its manifest means it (the pages' ten's `places`, `card` sizes, the static card's eleven common sizes with `min` and `max` beside them (any whole size, `sizeFor` held to none of the list, `fixed`), the countdown's and the weather's `settings`, the weather's `needs` its two Open-Meteo hosts and no other's any, no slot and no text part on a page's), and passing `gallery block check` with nothing to say where the machine has a browser. The logic of each script is run as a page runs it, its `kit.js` in a function of its own with a `DocKit` that keeps what it registers, jsdom's document and a `Date` the test sets, its functions read back by name: today's greeting by the hour, its words in either language and its date line in the language's order, the weekday first in English as the countdown writes a day and after the date in Chinese, its date moved by the clock's `day` at midnight and at a year's end, and the week its card draws (`aria-hidden`: Monday to Sunday across a year's end, each day's initial over its date, today's Today over its date and marked, the days before it gone); the countdown's whole days before, on and after its date, across a leap day and a change of the clocks, a date it cannot read asking for one; the habit's streak from today or from yesterday and stopping at a gap, today pressed in and out, each once and in order, its data refused where it is no habit; the note's words written by `context.editJSON` as the kit writes them (`jsonIn`) and read back as typed, lines, `</script>` and `<!--` among them, its field read-only until `editable`; the weather's sky for each WMO code (the moon by night, a code it does not know said so), its two addresses (a city's name encoded, its place at two decimals, ℉ asked for as such), the first match of a lookup or none, its degrees, the face of each state in either language (`private` too: 网络设置把天气服务指向了本地地址 and The network setup sends the weather service to a local address), and its mount through a `context.fetch` of the test's: refused for consent (said, nothing more asked), allowed (the city looked up, then its weather, at the hosts its needs name, drawn with the days ahead), a city no lookup knows, no city (nothing fetched), offline, and a refusal `private` said as such where another refusal is the generic failure. The workspace's four (`needs` `workspace`, their sizes and settings) are mounted on a `context.workspace` of the test's (`workspaceContext`: the answers by kind, what was asked and opened): the to-dos grouped by document, the open ones or all, a click opening its document at its block (or the document), read again for `data todos` alone, their three empty lines and an export's (`offline`), one document's asked by `slugs`; the documents edited last, when each was said (刚刚 to the date, across midnight), the icon from the server's route, the clock moving the times without a read; the numbers' cells, a dash each with nothing read, 0 each of an empty workspace, a big count short, read again as a day begins; the heatmap's squares that fit a card (`fitOf`: wide, high, low, narrow and far higher than a year needs), the weeks Monday first ending today and the days to come not drawn, a day's level, its line (从 … 开始记录 with nothing counted, the edits or the words added since a day or in the past year), an export's line, and, on a card 53 weeks wide on a Sunday (its body's size the test's), 371 days asked and drawn: a record older than a year (as many of its last days as asked) with no square not recorded yet, one begun ten days ago with the 360 before it drawn apart. A workspace's copies follow Gallery (`followShipped`): a copy as an older Gallery added it (version 1.0.0, today with no chip, recorded so) is Gallery's after `adoptWorkspace`, the dev server's start, a file the new one has not taken out and its record the new version's, while a note changed since it was added stays as it is, `shippedNewer` and 设置 › 组件列表's row (`newer`) giving Gallery's version; the log says each once, and a second start nothing; then, after `followOnRead` for its workspace, the catalog's read brings a copy up as it reads it while another workspace's read writes nothing, and the page service's library brings up its own. The cards' later refinements are held there too: 柔光风格's ten stylesheets are read for the `--soft-*` values they declare (one palette written ten times, so a value two of them declare is the same in both; the static card's grip room is its own); a to-do card's box that is checked keeps the keys' focus through the list drawn anew (Gallery's and 柔光风格's: Space, Tab goes on from there); 工作区统计's layout by its cells (a card four wide and under four high is the compact two by two the phone's flow makes of an 8 × 2) and its 刚开始记录 week caption; a soft to-do group's icon an emoji drawn as the server's picture of it.

`block list --json` (a block's fields, the `net` object, Gallery's own blocks not added, the built-in blocks of the menus), `block pack list --json`, `block pack on` and `off` (one of Gallery's own the workspace has not got installed first, as `gallery init` does, none under `GALLERY_NO_PACKS`), `block menu on` and `off` (a custom block, a block of pages alone, a built-in block by its id or its name in either language, one that stays in the menus) and `block net on` and `off` (the websites printed first, a block whose needs name none refused), each as the dialog's switches write `gallery.json`; and the help of a command with subcommands: `gallery help block` and `gallery help page` a short line for each subcommand, `gallery help block pack` and `gallery block pack --help` (and `gallery component …`) that one in full, an unknown subcommand a mistake that lists them, every option of the old usage still said by the subcommand that takes it and every line of it still there, a command with no sections (`gallery help trash list`) printed whole, and the usage lines a mistake is said with.

`tests/unit/net-route.mjs` holds a block online ([render.md](render.md), "A block online") against servers of this computer (a site, its TLS twin with a certificate of `tests/unit/fixtures/net-tls/` for `api.example.com`, which the test's `ca` trusts, and an http proxy) and a resolver of the test's, the addresses it gives dialed by the test's `connect` to those servers so that what is dialed is what was checked, never the real network: the policy's pure parts (`addressKind` for this computer, the private ranges, a carrier's, link-local, multicast, the reserved, the documents', fake-ip and IPv4 held in IPv6, `*.` hosts (`*.localhost` among the test's needs), a proxy of this computer, the addresses a block may ask for, the names a resolver keeps for this computer (`keptHere`: `localhost` and the names under it), the addresses a proxy that maps names answers with (`mapsHere`: this computer's, 127/8 and `::1`, and an IPv6 unique-local one, never a private IPv4 network's, a carrier's or a link's), `{key}` found and written); the static scan letting `context.fetch` through (and a mount's own `fetch` taken from its context) and refusing the bare `fetch` in a package's script; then, through the route: a fetch direct (one resolution, the checked address dialed, the host's name its Host, a GET with no cookie, as text too) and over https (TLS to the name); **a private address** (this computer, a private network, one among others, IPv6's loopback, IPv4 in IPv6, a cloud's metadata, fake-ip with no proxy of this computer, an IP written in the address), nothing dialed; **a redirect** (to another host of its needs followed, each hop resolved and dialed at its own address; to a host its needs do not name, to one resolving to a private network, a loop past `HOPS`, another port (`url`, its scheme and host said), a Location that does not parse (`url`, never a throw, and the answer kept given over it), a host not allowed: refused); **DNS rebinding** (the name public, then this computer's: the first answer dialed and the name never asked again in that fetch, the next fetch refused); **a proxy** (an http address named at the checked address, an https one by a CONNECT to it with TLS held to the name, a fake-ip address through a proxy of this computer taken and through another's refused, a proxy not running passed over for the same address direct, a fake-ip address then refused, and the why of that refusal saying the proxy that would reach it is not running, its address in the log's line alone); **a name answered as this computer's** (the resolver of the test giving `api.example.com` `127.129.1.73` and `fd00:696e:6974:6578::29:14a`, as the proxy of the blind test of 2026-11-09 did, no address handed to `fetchPinned`): with a proxy on this computer, an https address is a CONNECT naming the host (the name resolved here once, nothing dialed direct), TLS held to the name, the request sent only once the handshake is done; an http address, a name under `localhost`, no proxy, a proxy on another machine and a proxy not running are refused `private`, each with its `why` naming the host and the address (an IPv4 one where there is one) and what kept it, the same line in the log (the proxy's address there alone, never in the why), and so is a name answered with a private IPv4 network's address, alone or beside others (a carrier's too), never handed to the proxy; a certificate for another name and one of no public CA fail with no request sent (the block told `offline` with one why for all, the log naming the TLS code, `NODE_TLS_REJECT_UNAUTHORIZED=0` changing none of it), as does a proxy that hangs up on the CONNECT; a redirect from it to an http address or to a private IP literal refused at that hop; **an oversized answer** (by its length, by its chunks, by what it unpacks to); **no consent** (refused with the hosts waiting, nothing resolved; some allowed, the one waiting), a block that does not load or names no host, a picture, an error status, words asked as JSON; **offline** (a name that does not resolve, a site that does not answer, and the answer kept given from the disk); the cache within `FRESH`, the rate, the key (refused with none, written in by the server, never in the cache, nor in a refusal or the log's line where the site sent it back: a redirect sending its query on, a type naming it), the request's checks (Origin, JSON, its size, a GET) and the export's snapshot (`netSnapshot`: the document's addresses, `{key}` as written, by what `allowed` lets through). `tests/unit/catalog.mjs` holds the consents (`components.net` read as a hand wrote it, the settings line's fourth list, the stamp, a row's `allowed`, written and taken back, other switches leaving them, 允许联网's switch and its refusals, the kit told a block's needs).

no `main.gallery-page`; a board's or a card's id missing, ill-formed or twice; a card outside a board, with no instance or two; cells that are no whole numbers, off the board or past the row's end; two cards sharing cells, none for two side by side; the page service's writes since a time, a line it cannot read skipped); what page try says of a write inside the card's data parts and outside them (another card, the card's own frame, another's place, the page's head, the card gone: `cardChangesOf`, `outsideWhat`, `trialSaid` with `pageHost`); and a page's own files addressed where the server serves them (`withPageAssets`) and brought back by a block's write (`withOwnAssets`); and what a look waits for, in node with a `fetch` and a `location` of the test's in place of the page's: `watching` (`component-look.mjs`) counting each fetch of the workspace's query route while it waits, with the block whose `kit.js` asked read from the stack, an address that is no read of the workspace and the to-do's check route (which starts with the same words) not counted, an answer or a refusal taking its read out, a page that has the script having it once; and `drawn`'s `wait` (`component-try.mjs`), an instance held still for three looks that, with no `wait`, is called still while the answer is on its way, and with one is called still only once the answer has drawn what it reads, and for a read nothing answers waited for the `wait` and no longer. `tests/unit/page-try.mjs` runs both as a user runs them where the machine has a browser (else it says so), in a workspace of its own whose server the first command starts on a port of `GALLERY_TEST_PORTS` and the test stops: a page `desk` with Gallery's note and habit and a block of the test's own whose press also moves another card through the page service; words typed in the note through an input method then Ctrl+Enter (one write of its data part said line by line, one Ctrl+Z of the page's own undo, a fresh page reading it back, the picture under `.gallery/qa/desk/try/`), the habit's press and name in a steps file (two writes, two Ctrl+Z), a press with `--keep` (`page.html` holding it and every other card byte for byte as it was), the meddling press (exit 1, the move named), a card the page has not and a selector that finds nothing (exit 2), `page.html` never written by a trial on a scratch copy and no copy left; then `gallery page check` of the page as it is (exit 0, six pictures and the line of those not opened, no change since the page service's kept version, the page kept as the baseline), of a data part written since (its lines, against the last check, nothing marked the user's), of a card a reader moved since through the running server's page service, a POST as the board sends it (its line ending `(the user's, on the page)` and the line saying to leave it), and of a page with a script, two cards sharing cells, a picture not there and a card whose content does not fit it (exit 1, each at its line, the last at every width, no baseline kept); then `lookWaits`, a look (`page-check.mjs` `look`) of a page of the test's own on a tiny server that answers a read of the workspace as the test says: a card whose `kit.js` reads "recent", answered after 900 ms, after which it draws something wider than itself (pictured drawn: the problem is found; pictured before the answer, nothing is), a card whose `kit.js` reads "todos", never answered, and a card of the page's own inline script that reads "stats", never answered either; the first is no note, the other two are notes (no failure) each said at its card with its read and the looks, the second's read told by the `kit.js` that asked and the third's by its block being one that reads the workspace, and six pictures taken. The baseline line is plain: `the last check that found nothing (<time>, local time, <file>)`, or `the page as it was before its last write (<time>, local time; used because no check has passed yet)`. A look never waits without a bound: `look` run with a fake browser whose calls hang one at a time (a picture never taken, a tab that never closes or opens, a context that never closes or opens, a page that never opens or draws, a step an answer never comes to, and a whole look that outlasts its own bound with every step's long), each ending in a `FAIL` of the page naming what it waited for, the looks before it standing and the later looks not made, in a few seconds; `closed` never failing and never waiting past its time. A card whose block has a `required` setting its part leaves empty (`emptyRequired`, weather's city, countdown's date; one with a value, one with a default, a block with none, a part that is no JSON are none) is a note; `setsOf` and `settingsGiven` (each `--set` of `gallery page add` taken out of the command line, the words after the first `=` its value, held to the field's type).

`writtenWords()`: the words a write put in or took out of its lines, no tags, no JSON keys, entities read, and a box's state written as a class, an attribute or JSON's booleans; `writtenOtherwise()`: a word the page showed and the fresh page not that a write put in, the two sides of a count written, of words with no spaces between them one holding the other, words with spaces matched whole, a line shown and never written, a filter chosen, a box no write ticked, none; a box a write ticked, one), the check's way back of a page's third instance said against it and not against one in the first's slot (`wayBackSaid()`, a custom block of the test's own with a data part and a slot), the scratch copy (`scratchCopy()`: `doc.html` as given, `doc.json` and `assets/` copied, a slug no document has, served by `documentDirectory`, the document left as it was; `dropScratch()`: the copy and what the edit state keeps of it by its slug gone, a document's state and the token kept; `dropStale()`: a copy an hour old taken, a newer one and a folder of no copy's name left), when the server has told its pages of the writes the disk holds (`toldBy()`: no wait when nothing was written lately, the catalog's first look and settle's `QUIET` after the newest write of `doc.html`, `gallery.json` or a file of a custom block), when it has taken a custom block as the disk holds it (`takenBy()`, a stand-in server answering for its catalog in use: the first look and `QUIET` after a file of the custom block written and no wait after another custom block's, `HOLD` after a manifest half written while the server keeps the custom block and not where it has it as not loading too, the server behind a disk that reads the custom block as loading, its files' times old as a copy keeps them, a custom block `gallery.json` turns off, no server answering), how long a page in its fallback is waited for (`untilTaken()`, against the same stand-in: no wait with nothing written lately or another custom block written now; a file of the custom block written now, said on a `Waiting:` line, and the page loading itself again on a `Waited:` line; the server behind the disk, until it catches up and the page loads again, or to the end of the wait, said, the instance then said to stand in a server behind the disk; a manifest half written and put back whole with an old time meanwhile, a change seen whatever its time; a manifest half written to the end of the wait, said), what a page says, heard on a stand-in tab (`listen()`: a script of the custom block that does not parse, at the line the browser says, another script's error, a dialog; `takenAgain()`: once the page that loaded itself again is taken again, what the page before said heard no more, words of it read after that among them, and the new page's kept), why an instance shows what it holds, read-only (`fallbackSaid()`: its script not parsing, the kit's words on it aside, the console's words of its custom block at their line of `kit.js`, turned off, a server behind the disk, not loading or for another `apiVersion`, no such custom block, a page served before the take, nothing said), a custom block `gallery.json` turns off said so before anything runs (exit 1), the instances of a document with one in a slot listed with where it stands, steps files that do not read (exit 2), what a trial says and exits with (`trialSaid()`: a write said line by line, undone on a scratch copy and read back, more Ctrl+Z than steps that wrote, a single gesture's two writes, a read back with a note, kept with `--keep`, of a document's plain HTML with `--keep` and on a copy said nowhere, with someone else's write outside the parts, exit 3, a problem, exit 1 whatever else, a note, steps one of which wrote nothing; A second document built to warn shows a column cut by its body's words while its header fits, named by its caption as the list of tables that scroll names it (and neither a column whose cells reach past the frame with nothing written there, a table a pixel wider than its frame, nor columns whose list or paragraphs in a cell are as wide as the cell while their words end inside the frame), rows below a frame of the kit's height (at 1024px and in the laptop's window; in that window alone; none for a frame of the author's height), two axis labels over each other in a drawing of the document's own, a drawing's text its viewBox scales under 11px (`svg-text-small`, named by its card) and a figure's words past their box (`overflow-in-block`), words and marks cut at the bottom of a box (`cut-in-block`: a card's last line below a box of a fixed height, a shape an svg draws past its bottom edge, a whole block's bar and label drawn past a box of a fixed height and cut by the box around it, each at every width with its words or marks, its box and what to do; nothing for a frame that scrolls, a line set tighter than its font, a box folded to nothing, a label placed absolutely out of the box, a cropped picture or a circle bleeding out of every side), a code block of 60 lines warning on a desktop and pictured on a phone, a title cut on a phone, a short block and title saying nothing, a formula scrolling on a phone alone (pictured), a short one saying nothing, a 60-line sample and a long formula in a closed disclosure, code in a frame of its own height and code with its lines unwrapped (sideways) each info at every width and pictured on a phone alone, a kit note said as info, each table that scrolls by its caption (or its chart card's title, or its note with its tab after a separator) with its directions, each a line of information before the findings, the advice of code and formulas that scroll under their info too, the last line repeating each look's counts, and the kit's own notes of those frames (one measure), its fading edge on only while words lie past it (not over a cut table's padding or an empty part of its last column) and off once scrolled to its end. A third document, of what an Agent draws itself: a whole block (a timeline in HTML and CSS) whose dots its `::before` draws 7px past its box, its picture wider than the box by those and the margin around it, whose years cannot break and run past their 40px column at 360px (`overflow-in-block`, a warning there, with its words, its box and what to do); a detail card holding a table of 13 columns, opened at each width (counted so), its table pictured as a card, scrolling, and its columns out of sight said in the card, pictured into `blocks/` when named and in tiles of its own with `--tiles`; then a new Sheet area of three panels, pictured as it opens and with its second and third panels, and nothing changed at the run after (its panels' blocks seen); then the document's style changed alone, its cards pictured and said so; then a detail card in the middle panel of the three, opened (the run passes and writes its baseline), and a card holding a Sheet area of two panels, a paragraph changed in its second panel pictured into `changed/` and seen at the run after. On a machine without Chrome or Edge it exits 3 and says so. Then `gallery open`'s part of the baseline (`previewBaseline()`, run as the command calls it, in that directory): none for a document still holding the starter's section, one of the file as it is (`from: "preview"`) once written over, never over one there is, one of a document never handed in by its blocks' markup (none taken as changed once the export gives them ids), and, for a document exported before and changed since, one of the text its export was made from, not of the file as it is now. `tests/unit/workspace.mjs` holds `GET /__gallery/workspace/info`'s `server` (the process and the documents' directory) that `gallery open` holds against the announcement. `unopenedSaid` gives each picture by its whole path (no folder, space and names after it), and `inventory` counts a `gallery/chart-view` as a chart (in jsdom, none inside a developer's frame).

`tests/unit/sdk.mjs` passes deliberately wrong code through the compiler and asserts that each is rejected with an HTML line number (the scripts read as a page is: none in a `<template>` or `<noscript>`, one in an `<svg>`, with exact lines and columns under LF, CRLF and a BOM; so are the calls the kit no longer has, such as `DocTables.enhanceAll`, `DocDialogs.enhance` and `DocCharts.validate`), a chart's handle `redraw` and `destroy` alone, and the rows a chart drawn from a table gets typed by `DocTableRows` (a value of it a number, its words or null), and `DocKit.choose` (a block's menu from its own button: rows as pairs, objects, words and a group, a missing `options` refused and its answer used as a string, which may be null, refused); the SDK declares no chart type of its own. code that runs, a block the parser puts in a sample and a formula in the page's text do), what a document's tags lack, the tags loading their files from where the preview names them (`href`: its stamps, the tags put in and those a source writes as the kit writes them alike), a script that calls a function the kit no longer has told what to write instead, the element and attribute an export's inline copy of each tag has, a document shown through `renderReportHtml()` the same in both modes) and, with the starter's `report.json`, that a document asks only a title, a description and a type of its `report.json` (a language is neither required nor refused), `tests/unit/components.mjs` the tree helpers every reader goes through (`src/shared/html-tree.mjs`: `walk` with a template's content in or out), the kit's component descriptions (`src/shared/components/`, docs/internals.md "Components") and the checks of a document's components (`src/render/component-checks.mjs`: their parts and pairs, the kit's names, bindings; a disclosure without its label; attributes opened with curly quotes said first, before the failures they make, and none for curly quotes inside straight ones), and that the server's block scanner (`source/blocks.mjs`) spells none of the classes a description names, and `tests/unit/cards.mjs` the component cards (`docs/components/`, the maintainers' reference: their option tables the SDK's, every example through the checks as an export checks it, its natural HTML made the kit's, each listed in `docs/quick-reference.md`) and the Component Catalog (every component shown; the kit's markup, which `naturalize` leaves as it is). `tests/unit/runtime.mjs` (the kit files as `src/render/kit-build.mjs` makes them; `html[data-doc-drawn]` set only once the frames an instance and the answer to its drawing asked for have run) draws declared charts (a document's own type drawn once defined, from its JSON, again on a theme change and with new data, a part with what it holds, a broken declaration that leaves the others drawing, `get`; the theme's tokens and tones), runs the Component Catalog in jsdom, exercises linkage, restores/resets state, tells a component the system's theme changing once (and not at all under a theme the reader has chosen), exports twice, checks that the runtime carries only the `match` and `runtime` of the descriptions the kernel reads (the field list against `kernel.js`, none of a description's prose in the file, and an export of the view the same as one made by a runtime with whole descriptions), and opens detail cards from a link to the dialog and from `data-doc-dialog-open` (not from other in-page links or clicks already handled). These are DOM behavior tests, not visual QA: browser layout, fonts, and pixel checks still need a real browser.

JavaScript helper parameters in documents may remain implicitly typed (`noImplicitAny: false`); public SDK calls, nested data, callbacks, and `redraw` options are checked. Shared runtime code is covered by behavior tests rather than claimed as fully TypeScript-checked.

## Release smoke test (`npm run test:release`)

`tests/release/restart-smoke.mjs` runs last, once the package's files were compared: on the installed package, a crash (`GALLERY_TEST_CRASH_AFTER=3000`: the first worker throws out of a timer) and a replaced package (its `release.json` changed, put back at the end) each bring a new server on the same port by the same keeper, which answers; `gallery stop` ends the server and its keeper, and nothing answers 3 s later; a keeper ended by another process takes its server with it. `node tests/unit/supervisor.mjs` checks the keeper's rules without a server: which exit codes and signals start another worker, the waits growing and giving up, the count starting over, an upgrade's worker retried, another server taking over, and real child processes over IPC.

`tests/release/sandbox-smoke.mjs` runs the packaged sandbox alongside the main smoke test's service. It checks that both use distinct ports, that a supplied extension is enabled in the sandbox, that the browser shows its banner, and that stopping it cleans up its directory while the main service still responds. `node tests/unit/sandbox.mjs` checks workspace copying and isolation, extension reloads, an extension reached through a junction (and one that is a junction refused), startup protection, and cleanup from a source checkout; `tests/release/extension-dev.mjs` also runs `extension check` in a workspace reached through one.

`tests/release/smoke.mjs` checks the npm package as a user gets it: it runs `npm run release:build`, packs `release/npm/` (`npm pack`) into a folder of the OS temp folder, installs the tarball globally into a prefix there (`npm install -g --prefix <temp>/prefix --cache <temp>/npm-cache`: nothing goes into the user's global folder or npm cache; the package has no dependency, so nothing is fetched), and runs it from another folder of its own (no `node_modules` up from it), on a workspace of its own (`GALLERY_WORKSPACE`) and the first free port of 4241–4260, or of `GALLERY_TEST_PORTS` when set (`GALLERY_PORT`), `GALLERY_NO_BROWSER=1`, the agents' homes, the registry's data folder (`GALLERY_DATA_DIR`) and the update cache of its own with `GALLERY_NO_UPDATE_CHECK=1`: `gallery --version` (also through npm's command shim; then once with the check on and a registry of its own on 127.0.0.1 that says a newer version, the machine's proxies and `npm_config_*` left out and no `.npmrc`: the version alone, the registry's answer written to the cache by the package's `<package>/dist/update-fetch.mjs`, started detached, and the next run's `新版本：` line), `init`, `new` (its server started: the time from its start to listening and to the document's page answering is read from the server's log; no line of a browser, which it opens only with `--open`), the Agent's reference written as the document (`skills/gallery/references/example-report.html`, every kind of block, a chart drawn with d3: its types checked), `check`, `export` (and its options: `--allow-images https://cdn.example.com --embeds-as-links` writing `exports/smoke-web.html`, its policy's `img-src` naming the source and the embed a card, the plain file naming none; a source with a `;` refused, exit 2; the picture made a 2000px PNG for a moment, embedded as a lighter WebP by the bundle's `encodePicture` in the user's Chrome or Edge, or said to be as it is without one, and `--original-images` embedding the PNG to the byte), `list`, `open --no-browser` (the server reused), the server's routes (the workbench and its assets, the editor's modules and a kit file by their stamps, a kit file by its path, KaTeX's fonts, d3, `/__gallery/workspace/info`), a page's event stream told of a write to `doc.html`; `new --under`, `move` and `set`, each told on the workbench's event stream (`?workspace`: the release's own server, its `fs.watch` for `set`), and `list --tree` showing them; in Chrome or Edge (puppeteer, the repository's): the workbench with the document open, then the document's page, a word typed in a paragraph saved to `doc.html`, no page errors, and a one-page PDF printed; `import` of a Markdown file, a Word file (made with `createZip`) and that PDF, each one's words in its `doc.html`; a custom block from the installed command, a static block and one with a script: `component new`, `check` (exit 0, its ten pictures: the six, the two overviews and the two as the editing page draws it; 3 accepted without a browser), a taken id refused, `list`, `show`, the two as instances in a document whose page names the custom block's script by its stamp, served as a kit file is, `check` and `export`, the file holding their style and script inline; `component try` on the official scorecard (`tests/fixtures/components/`) in a document of its own, a dot pressed and Enter on another, which reaches the keyboard's layout of puppeteer's in the bundle (each exit 0, its Wrote line one line of the JSON with that line as it was and as it is, its Undone line of the scratch copy and its Read back line, the file as it was to the byte; 3 accepted without a browser); a custom page: `block add note`, `page new` and `page add` right after it (through the running server, which takes the block a moment later), a picture of the page's own served at `/pages/<slug>/assets/<file>` by its type, `page try` of words typed in the note (its Wrote line the note's data part and nothing else in `page.html`, OK) and `page check` (OK, the card at its three widths in light and dark), `page.html` as it was to the byte (3 accepted for each without a browser); `qa` (exit 0; 3 accepted without a browser), which exports the edited document first; `stop` (the process gone, `server.json` gone, `last-port` kept), and the server's log naming nothing "not in the release". The installed package's files are listed with their hashes before and compared after (on macOS and Linux the folder is also made read-only for the run): nothing is written there. It prints the tarball's size, the unpacked size and file count, what the prefix holds in all, `npm ls -g --all` of the prefix (the runtime dependencies: none) and the time of each command; on a failure it keeps its temporary folder and names it. It needs no network: the build reads the checkout's `node_modules`, and the tarball installs alone.


`tests/unit/shared-tree.mjs` covers the shared ordinary document/link tree: both workspace projections, bidirectional order and hierarchy, ownership checks, legacy mixed-tree normalization and newly discovered files. `tests/unit/copy-tree.mjs` checks cross-workspace copy through the real workspace service: new identities, mixed document/link descendants, assets, HTML attribute rewriting without changing code samples, missing custom blocks copied, conflicting blocks refused, originals retained and failed copies cleaned up. The browser scenario `sharedWorkspaceTree` opens two workbenches and checks live synchronization, foreign links, both directions of sorting, copy confirmation/cancellation, subtree/asset copies, API ownership rejection and persistence after reload. `extensionColumns` covers a program extension's `columns/`: a file there is a sidebar column keyed `~<id>:<column>/<entry>`, a row of the extension's detail and no card, rewritten without reloading the extension (same revision, one activation), hidden while it is disabled and back when enabled, renamed into its own file, and opened at its address; `tests/unit/extensions.mjs` covers its listing, keys, groups, edits and watcher news, `extension-runtime.mjs` the backend's `GALLERY_EXTENSION_COLUMNS`, an update keeping `columns/` and `list --json`, `tests/release/extension-dev.mjs` `extension check` and `list --json` of one. `extensionSection` also checks that an extension's rows are laid out as a document's (the extension list's detail rows are `.ext-detail-row`, which styles nothing in the sidebar) and covers deletion of public/project-only columns, root subtrees including groups with no URL, cancellation, stale confirmation refusal, active-page removal and synchronization across two workbenches. `webSidebarHoverClick` uses real pointer movement to check that a revealed panel cannot take stationary clicks intended for its floating trigger, movement restores row clicks, and a direct button click still expands the sidebar.

- **Single service and extensions:** `tests/unit/projects.mjs` checks shared identity, scoped document assets/commands/search/trash; `tests/unit/extension-runtime.mjs` checks extension installation, replacement, consent, traversal, authenticated proxy, crash restart and shutdown. Browser `unifiedGallery` checks same-origin editing and owner-local persistence.

The 0.2.2 single-service regressions include `unifiedGallery` (equal slugs in home/project, one origin, original-file saves), `unifiedRecovery` (blocked commits, reload recovery, owner-isolated buffers), `unifiedIdleSidebar` (external project edits still update a visible column after 160 seconds idle), `extensionWorkbench` (live switches, owned slots, route callbacks, update/dispose, late async activation, no `window.gallery` and a script without `activate(api)` shown as its error, cross-tab changes, broken manifests, directory/ZIP installation and settings deep links), `projects.mjs` (scoped APIs/assets/search/trash) `extension-runtime.mjs` (ZIP install, replacement, authenticated proxy, restart and child shutdown), `tests/unit/extension-settings.mjs` (an extension's own settings: the manifest's `settings` checked, an unknown type refused; the values in gallery.json and the backend's `GALLERY_EXTENSION_SETTINGS` file, read anew with no restart after `POST /__gallery/extensions/settings`; a disabled extension refused; `gallery extension settings` through the service and without one; removal taking them; and the columns of gone extensions, a 0.2.4 `ext-<id>` among them, taken out of the outline file as the server starts and by `extension remove`, a disabled one's and the user's kept) and `extensionSettings` (the setting's switch on the extension's detail in 拓展列表, no border and no native select, switched off: the backend's next read says false with the same pid, `api.settings` and `api.onSettings` hear it with no reload, gallery.json and `gallery extension settings` say it; greyed while the extension is disabled; an old extension's folder deleted by hand while Gallery runs: its `ext-yuque` leaves outline.json and the sidebar shows no column for it). Every browser studio sets its own GALLERY_DATA_DIR and GALLERY_DEFAULT_WORKSPACE.

## Startup and global appearance regression coverage (2026-11-11)

- tests/unit/startup.mjs: isolated registry adapter lifecycle, disable persistence, failed OS registration, real Windows hidden WSH launch with Chinese/spaced paths, source isolation, macOS plist parsing and removal. Actual macOS login needs a Mac.
- tests/unit/projects.mjs: startup endpoint protections; global appearance from another storage directory, inherited render, retained document overrides, explicit empty reset.
- documentLookAll browser scenario: actual settings action, live other-tab updates without reload, inherited fields with retained overrides, new documents and exports.
- annotateBeside, annotateButton and annotateLayout: delayed attachment and the narrower shared rail, including section and table clearances. Release smoke disables OS startup registration in its isolated installation.

## Review fixes and extension patch integration (2026-11-11)

- `tests/unit/service-ports.mjs` checks the default 8080–8099 candidate range, priority of previously saved ports (including 4317), explicit range overrides, skipping an occupied port without stopping its owner, and exhaustion. Actual listening checks use OS-assigned ports in an isolated data directory.

- `startupSettings` (`tests/browser/startup-settings.mjs`) intercepts all startup network operations: initial delayed GET updates an open menu, an old GET cannot undo a completed POST, failures restore state, duplicate clicks cannot race, and later visibility refresh updates the same menu. No OS registration is changed.
- `tests/unit/cli-settings.mjs` exercises the installed-style CLI command against an isolated service: `open --settings extensions --no-browser` prints the settings deep link, while unsupported settings and document/settings combinations fail before document operations.
- `tests/release/extensions-acceptance.mjs` runs 17 acceptance checks of the extension settings in the existing isolated installed-package smoke environment on Windows/macOS. It covers the no-confirmation switch, persistent update state, backend replacement, disable, CLI enable/disable/JSON/install --enable, old request compatibility, and malformed manifest handling. Login startup stays disabled in this environment.
- `tests/release/parity-acceptance.mjs` ports the 21 acceptance checks of an Agent doing what the user does (a shell script of 2026-11-11, all failing on 0.2.3) to the same installed package, data and workspace, just before qa and stop: the lists' `--json`, `trash` and its `list`/`restore`, `duplicate`, `favorite`, `set --font`, `export --format markdown|pdf`, `copy --to` another workspace made for it, `column new`, `new`/`move --column`, `page set` and a page trashed, `block pack on` (switched off again after, as the rest of the smoke test expects 柔光风格 off), `notes`/`status --json`, `revert` of a write made beside the server and `history --json`. It compares paths as the system does (the script's `/tmp` path never matches a Windows path) and fails the smoke test after all 21 are said.
- `tests/unit/ui-commands.mjs` checks the table of 界面操作 → 命令 (`src/cli/ui-commands.mjs`): it reads every `label: t('…')` of the workbench's menu files the table lists (reportMenus, SidebarTree, SidebarExtension, Topbar, LookMenu, AnnotateButton, Presentation, WritingPreferences, theme) and fails on an item the table neither maps to a command nor excuses, on an item it names that no menu holds any more, and on a command whose usage (gallery help) lacks the options the line names. A menu item added later comes with its command, or a line saying why none, in the same change.
- `tests/unit/workspace-events.mjs` checks one shared event stream across workbench and extension consumers, cross-tab broadcasting, Web Lock handoff, reconnect refreshes and cleanup after the final subscriber leaves.

The multi-preference and UI-preview checks are `tests/unit/preference-documents.mjs` (an old `<home>/preferences.md` migrated to `<home>/preferences/项目报告写作.md`, a new workspace starting with `<home>/preferences/通用写作.md` made of the default once and first in the order, order, enable/disable, CRUD and safe paths) and `tests/unit/previews.mjs` (standalone embedding and source protection).

`tests/release/previews.mjs [package-root]` builds a standalone preview and takes its light, dark and narrow screenshots with the packaged assets; the source file and workspace remain unchanged.

`multipleWritingPreferences` covers menu hit-testing above the dialog, right-click rename through a real pointer click, Escape closing the menu first, retained drafts, rapid rename/open, saving every dirty tab, CLI enable events and external discovery. It also types into an empty duplicate without programmatically focusing its body, checks live heading/tab synchronization, duplicates unsaved title/body edits, saves and reopens the copy, and checks reveal failure feedback. `extensionWorkbench` covers card/detail focus, nested dependency hot reload and error recovery. `tests/release/extension-dev.mjs [package-root]` checks generated UI/backend templates, real sandbox activation, screenshots and failure diagnostics (a script without `activate(api)` among them) without enabling the original.
